## Configuration Layers: Dari Paling Rendah ke Paling Tinggi

```text id="g9uxbc"
Layer 0: Code defaults
Layer 1: JDK defaults
Layer 2: Application packaged config
Layer 3: External config file
Layer 4: Environment variables
Layer 5: Java system properties -D
Layer 6: Command-line application args
Layer 7: Dynamic runtime/config service/feature flag
```

Rekomendasi precedence praktis:

| Priority | Source | Alasan |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Command-line application args | Paling eksplisit untuk satu execution |
| 2 | Java system properties `-D` | Eksplisit di JVM command |
| 3 | Environment variables | Natural untuk container/K8s |
| 4 | External mounted config file | Bagus untuk config banyak/terstruktur |
| 5 | Packaged config file | Default per artifact |
| 6 | Code default | Fallback terakhir dan harus aman |

Yang penting bukan urutan ini harus sama di semua perusahaan, tetapi **urutan harus terdokumentasi dan bisa dibuktikan**.

---

## System Property vs Environment Variable

| Aspek | Java System Property | Environment Variable |
|---|---|---|
| Cara set | `-Dkey=value` | `KEY=value` |
| Cara baca | `System.getProperty("key")` | `System.getenv("KEY")` |
| Scope | JVM process | OS/container process environment |
| Cocok untuk | JVM/framework config | Deployment/platform config |
| Risiko | Terlihat di command line/process metadata | Bisa bocor via env dump/log |
| Contoh | `-Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta` | `APP_ENV=prod` |

Contoh:

```bash id="aw41gr"
java \
  -Dapp.env=prod \
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta \
  -jar app.jar
```

```java id="7dakyy"
String env = System.getProperty("app.env");
String timezone = System.getProperty("user.timezone");
```

Environment variable:

```bash id="xz0mc3"
APP_ENV=prod DB_HOST=postgres java -jar app.jar
```

```java id="3av5vo"
String env = System.getenv("APP_ENV");
String dbHost = System.getenv("DB_HOST");
```

---

## Properties File Pattern

### Basic `app.properties`

```properties id="1g73ty"
app.env=prod
app.name=order-service

http.port=8080
http.requestTimeoutMillis=30000

db.host=postgres.internal
db.port=5432
db.name=orders
db.pool.max=20
db.pool.connectionTimeoutMillis=3000

camunda.jobExecutor.enabled=true
camunda.jobExecutor.maxJobsPerAcquisition=5

feature.newOrderFlow=false
```

### Loader sederhana

```java id="v35k0p"
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Properties;

public final class AppConfig {
    private final Properties properties;

    private AppConfig(Properties properties) {
        this.properties = properties;
    }

    public static AppConfig load(Path path) {
        Properties props = new Properties();

        try (InputStream input = Files.newInputStream(path)) {
            props.load(input);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to load config file: " + path, e);
        }

        return new AppConfig(props);
    }

    public String requiredString(String key) {
        String value = properties.getProperty(key);
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing required config: " + key);
        }
        return value.trim();
    }

    public int requiredInt(String key, int min, int max) {
        String raw = requiredString(key);

        try {
            int value = Integer.parseInt(raw);
            if (value < min || value > max) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(
                    "Config out of range: " + key + "=" + value +
                    ", expected " + min + ".." + max
                );
            }
            return value;
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid integer config: " + key + "=" + raw, e);
        }
    }

    public boolean requiredBoolean(String key) {
        String raw = requiredString(key).toLowerCase();

        return switch (raw) {
            case "true" -> true;
            case "false" -> false;
            default -> throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Invalid boolean config: " + key + "=" + raw
            );
        };
    }
}
```

Pattern ini sengaja eksplisit supaya:

```text id="5c3jm5"
missing config     → fail fast
wrong type         → fail fast
out-of-range value → fail fast
```

---

## Recommended Config Resolution Pattern

```text id="ue19ud"
resolve(key):
  1. command-line app args
  2. Java system property
  3. environment variable
  4. external config file
  5. packaged config file
  6. safe default
  7. fail if required and unresolved
```

Contoh resolver:

```java id="egbopv"
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Properties;

public final class ConfigResolver {
    private final Map<String, String> env;
    private final Properties fileProps;

    public ConfigResolver(Map<String, String> env, Properties fileProps) {
        this.env = env;
        this.fileProps = fileProps;
    }

    public String get(String propertyKey, String envKey, String defaultValue) {
        String fromSystemProperty = System.getProperty(propertyKey);
        if (fromSystemProperty != null && !fromSystemProperty.isBlank()) {
            return fromSystemProperty.trim();
        }

        String fromEnv = env.get(envKey);
        if (fromEnv != null && !fromEnv.isBlank()) {
            return fromEnv.trim();
        }

        String fromFile = fileProps.getProperty(propertyKey);
        if (fromFile != null && !fromFile.isBlank()) {
            return fromFile.trim();
        }

        return defaultValue;
    }

    public String required(String propertyKey, String envKey) {
        String value = get(propertyKey, envKey, null);
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Missing required config: property=" + propertyKey + ", env=" + envKey
            );
        }
        return value;
    }
}
```

---

## Jersey/JAX-RS Configuration

Jersey configuration biasanya masuk lewat `ResourceConfig`, `Application#getProperties`, atau container/framework integration. Jersey `ResourceConfig` API mendukung konfigurasi properties dan resource/provider registration; dokumentasi Jersey juga menyebut property untuk mengontrol feature auto-discovery. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/3.1.10/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/server/ResourceConfig.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```java id="zv2nsc"
import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public final class ApiApplication extends ResourceConfig {
    public ApiApplication(AppConfig config) {
        packages("com.company.orders.api");

        property("jersey.config.server.tracing.type", "OFF");

        register(new JsonProvider(config));
        register(new ErrorMapper());
        register(new RequestIdFilter());
    }
}
```

Prinsip penting:

| Area | Praktik |
|---|---|
| Resource registration | Prefer eksplisit untuk service kritikal |
| Auto-discovery | Boleh, tapi pahami cost startup dan determinism |
| Provider config | JSON/XML provider harus jelas |
| Error mapper | Pastikan config error tidak bocor ke response |
| Tracing/debug | Jangan aktifkan verbose tracing di production tanpa kontrol |

---

## MyBatis Configuration

MyBatis punya file konfigurasi yang mencakup `properties`, `settings`, `typeAliases`, `typeHandlers`, `plugins`, `environments`, dan `mappers`; dokumentasi resmi MyBatis menekankan bahwa settings/properties dapat berdampak besar pada behavior MyBatis. ([mybatis.org](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh `mybatis-config.xml`:

```xml id="sqp6l4"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" ?>
<!DOCTYPE configuration
  PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Config 3.0//EN"
  "https://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-config.dtd">

<configuration>
  <properties resource="db.properties"/>

  <settings>
    <setting name="mapUnderscoreToCamelCase" value="true"/>
    <setting name="jdbcTypeForNull" value="NULL"/>
    <setting name="defaultStatementTimeout" value="30"/>
  </settings>

  <typeAliases>
    <package name="com.company.orders.domain"/>
  </typeAliases>

  <mappers>
    <package name="com.company.orders.persistence.mapper"/>
  </mappers>
</configuration>
```

Config yang perlu diaudit:

| Config | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| `mapUnderscoreToCamelCase` | Mengubah mapping column ke property |
| `defaultStatementTimeout` | Menghindari query menggantung terlalu lama |
| `localCacheScope` | Memengaruhi memory dan repeat query behavior |
| mapper package/resource | Salah scan membuat mapper tidak ditemukan |
| type handler | Konversi JSONB, enum, timestamp, custom type |
| transaction manager | Harus konsisten dengan lifecycle aplikasi |

---

## Camunda Configuration

Untuk Camunda, konfigurasi bukan hanya “connection string”. Ia memengaruhi process execution, transaction boundary, job acquisition, history, retry, deployment, dan incident visibility.

Contoh area yang harus dipetakan:

| Area | Config penting | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Engine datasource | JDBC URL, pool, transaction manager | Deadlock, connection exhaustion |
| History level | none/activity/audit/full | Audit kurang atau DB membengkak |
| Job executor | enablement, acquisition, max jobs, wait time | Worker overload atau job lambat |
| Deployment | auto deploy BPMN/DMN atau manual | Model salah deploy |
| Retry | failed job retries, backoff | Incident flood atau silent retry terlalu lama |
| Serialization | variable serializer | Data tidak bisa dibaca setelah upgrade |
| Metrics | enable/disable/reporting | Observability kurang |

Prinsipnya: **Camunda config harus dianggap bagian dari domain runtime**, bukan sekadar technical config.

---

## JDK-Level Configuration Files

| File / Config | Fungsi | Contoh risiko |
|---|---|---|
| `$JAVA_HOME/conf/logging.properties` | Default Java Util Logging | Log level berubah karena base image berbeda |
| `$JAVA_HOME/conf/security/java.security` | Security providers/properties | TLS/cipher/provider berubah setelah JDK upgrade |
| truststore | Root/intermediate CA trust | Service gagal connect HTTPS/DB TLS |
| keystore | Client cert/private key | mTLS gagal |
| policy/security provider config | Security behavior advanced | Runtime behavior beda antar image |

Oracle Java Logging overview menyebut default logging config JDK ada di `java-home/conf/logging.properties`; `LogManager` juga mendukung `java.util.logging.config.file` untuk override startup logging config. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/core/java-logging-overview.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```bash id="6goi2g"
java \
  -Djava.util.logging.config.file=/etc/app/logging.properties \
  -jar app.jar
```

---

## Kubernetes Pattern

### ConfigMap untuk non-secret

```yaml id="wnhpmw"
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: order-service-config
data:
  APP_ENV: "prod"
  LOG_LEVEL: "INFO"
  DB_HOST: "postgres.internal"
  DB_PORT: "5432"
```

### Secret untuk sensitive value

```yaml id="80qslc"
apiVersion: v1
kind: Secret
metadata:
  name: order-service-secret
type: Opaque
stringData:
  DB_USERNAME: "orders_app"
  DB_PASSWORD: "replace-me"
```

### Inject ke Deployment

```yaml id="n2n18l"
envFrom:
  - configMapRef:
      name: order-service-config
  - secretRef:
      name: order-service-secret
```

### Mounted config file

```yaml id="z96ik3"
volumeMounts:
  - name: app-config
    mountPath: /etc/app
    readOnly: true

volumes:
  - name: app-config
    configMap:
      name: order-service-file-config
```

### JVM reads mounted file

```bash id="yxfpmg"
java \
  -Dapp.config.file=/etc/app/app.properties \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

---

## Docker Pattern

### Anti-pattern

```dockerfile id="srmnn0"
ENV DB_PASSWORD=hardcoded-password
ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-Ddb.password=hardcoded-password", "-jar", "/app/app.jar"]
```

Masalah:

```text id="v4cyj8"
1. Secret masuk image/history.
2. Secret terlihat di process args.
3. Image tidak reusable antar environment.
```

### Better pattern

```dockerfile id="o7o75n"
ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "/app/app.jar"]
```

Lalu inject dari runtime:

```bash id="60muta"
docker run \
  -e APP_ENV=prod \
  -e DB_HOST=postgres.internal \
  --env-file ./secrets.env \
  app:latest
```

---

## Production Config Contract Template

Gunakan tabel seperti ini di repo:

| Key | Env var | Type | Required | Default | Sensitive | Owner | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `app.env` | `APP_ENV` | enum | yes | none | no | platform | Runtime environment |
| `http.port` | `HTTP_PORT` | int | yes | `8080` | no | app | HTTP server port |
| `db.host` | `DB_HOST` | string | yes | none | no | database | PostgreSQL host |
| `db.password` | `DB_PASSWORD` | secret | yes | none | yes | platform/security | DB password |
| `db.pool.max` | `DB_POOL_MAX` | int | yes | `20` | no | app/database | Max DB connections |
| `http.client.timeout` | `HTTP_CLIENT_TIMEOUT` | duration | yes | `5s` | no | app | Outbound request timeout |
| `camunda.jobExecutor.enabled` | `CAMUNDA_JOB_EXECUTOR_ENABLED` | boolean | yes | `false` | no | workflow | Enable job executor |
| `feature.newOrderFlow` | `FEATURE_NEW_ORDER_FLOW` | boolean | no | `false` | no | product/app | Feature rollout |

---

## Startup Config Validation Checklist

Saat aplikasi start, lakukan validasi ini:

| Check | Reason |
|---|---|
| Required config present | Hindari failure saat request pertama |
| Type valid | Hindari parsing error runtime |
| Range valid | Pool/timeout tidak ekstrem |
| Cross-field valid | `min <= max`, timeout order benar |
| File path readable | Mounted config/secret benar |
| Secret not empty | Secret injection berhasil |
| DB config reachable jika mode membutuhkan DB | Fail early untuk service yang wajib DB |
| Timezone explicit | Audit/scheduler konsisten |
| Feature flag valid | Tidak ada flag typo |
| Redacted config summary logged | Debuggable tanpa bocor secret |

Contoh startup log yang aman:

```text id="w4g45k"
Resolved configuration:
  app.env=prod
  http.port=8080
  db.host=postgres.internal
  db.port=5432
  db.username=orders_app
  db.password=****
  db.pool.max=20
  user.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
  feature.newOrderFlow=false
```

---

## Runtime Verification Commands

### JVM command dan system properties

```bash id="aesxti"
jcmd <pid> VM.command_line
jcmd <pid> VM.system_properties
jcmd <pid> VM.flags
```

### Environment variables container

```bash id="x17v46"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- printenv | sort
```

### Mounted files

```bash id="kc55w7"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- ls -lah /etc/app
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- cat /etc/app/app.properties
```

### Jangan tampilkan secret sembarangan

Untuk secret, cukup cek metadata atau checksum, bukan value mentah:

```bash id="90s9fq"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- sh -c 'test -n "$DB_PASSWORD" && echo "DB_PASSWORD is set"'
```

---

## Failure Mode Map

| Symptom | Kemungkinan root cause config | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| App start local, gagal di pod | Config file tidak termount / env var hilang | `printenv`, `ls /etc/app`, startup log |
| DB connection refused | Host/port/profile salah | resolved config, DB logs |
| DB auth failed | Secret salah atau tidak terinject | secret presence check, DB audit |
| Request timeout tinggi | Timeout default terlalu besar | app config dump, JFR socket events |
| Pool exhausted | `db.pool.max` terlalu kecil atau leak | pool metrics, thread dump |
| Camunda job tidak jalan | job executor disabled / acquisition config salah | engine config, job table |
| Log terlalu verbose | log level/properties salah | logging config, startup log |
| TLS gagal setelah base image upgrade | truststore/security provider berubah | JDK version, truststore, `java.security` |
| Time-based bug | timezone berbeda app/DB/container | `user.timezone`, DB timezone |
| Config berubah tapi pod behavior sama | pod belum restart / config mounted behavior tidak reload | rollout status, pod age |
| Secret bocor di log | config dump tidak redacted | startup logs |
| Feature aktif di prod tanpa sengaja | flag default/prod override salah | effective config summary |

---

## Senior-Level Design Rules

| Rule | Explanation |
|---|---|
| Config is part of runtime contract | Harus direview seperti code. |
| Defaults must be safe | Jangan default ke dev endpoint atau permissive security. |
| Required config should fail fast | Jangan menunggu request pertama. |
| Secrets are not normal config | Jangan log, jangan taruh command line, jangan commit. |
| Effective config must be observable | Minimal redacted startup summary. |
| Config precedence must be deterministic | Tidak boleh “tergantung siapa yang baca duluan”. |
| Unit must be explicit | `5s`, `500ms`, `20Mi`, bukan angka ambigu. |
| Runtime and test config must be comparable | CI harus mendekati production contract. |
| Config changes need rollback plan | Terutama pool, timeout, retry, GC/memory. |
| Every tuning knob needs owner | Kalau tidak ada owner, knob akan jadi technical debt. |

---

## Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Contoh | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Config default dev | `db.host=localhost` di packaged prod artifact | Prod connect ke target salah | No unsafe default untuk required config |
| Secret di `-D` | `-Ddb.password=abc` | Bocor via process command | Secret env/file/secret manager |
| Secret di ConfigMap | password di ConfigMap | Tidak sesuai security boundary | Kubernetes Secret / vault |
| Tidak ada precedence | env dan file beda value | Behavior tidak deterministik | Dokumentasikan urutan |
| Config stringly-typed | semua `String` | Unit/range bug | typed config object |
| Silent fallback | missing config fallback ke default | Bug tersembunyi | fail fast |
| Logging semua config | dump raw properties | Secret bocor | redaction |
| Config tersebar di banyak class | setiap class baca env sendiri | Sulit audit | central config resolver |
| Runtime config beda dari test | test pakai H2/local config | Prod bug lolos | parity config |
| Mengubah pool/timeout tanpa metrics | tuning buta | Incident makin parah | observe → change → compare |

---

## Practical Java/Jersey Config Skeleton

```text id="8q3ffm"
com.company.app.config
├─ AppConfig.java
├─ ConfigResolver.java
├─ ConfigSource.java
├─ ConfigValidationException.java
├─ DatabaseConfig.java
├─ HttpConfig.java
├─ CamundaConfig.java
├─ FeatureFlagConfig.java
└─ RedactedConfigPrinter.java
```

Contoh typed config:

```java id="e9sogr"
public record DatabaseConfig(
    String host,
    int port,
    String database,
    String username,
    String password,
    int maxPoolSize,
    int connectionTimeoutMillis
) {
    public DatabaseConfig {
        if (host == null || host.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("db.host is required");
        }
        if (port < 1 || port > 65535) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("db.port out of range: " + port);
        }
        if (maxPoolSize < 1 || maxPoolSize > 200) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("db.pool.max out of range: " + maxPoolSize);
        }
        if (connectionTimeoutMillis < 100 || connectionTimeoutMillis > 60000) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "db.connectionTimeoutMillis out of range: " + connectionTimeoutMillis
            );
        }
    }

    public String redacted() {
        return "DatabaseConfig[" +
            "host=" + host +
            ", port=" + port +
            ", database=" + database +
            ", username=" + username +
            ", password=****" +
            ", maxPoolSize=" + maxPoolSize +
            ", connectionTimeoutMillis=" + connectionTimeoutMillis +
            "]";
    }
}
```

## Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Step | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | System property vs environment variable | Bisa membedakan `-D` dan env var |
| 2 | Config file loading | Bisa load external `.properties` dengan fail-fast |
| 3 | Config precedence | Bisa mendesain urutan override yang deterministik |
| 4 | Typed config object | Bisa validasi type/range/cross-field |
| 5 | Framework config | Bisa mapping config ke Jersey, MyBatis, Camunda, DB pool |
| 6 | Secret handling | Bisa memisahkan secret dari non-secret config |
| 7 | Runtime verification | Bisa membuktikan effective config di pod |
| 8 | Config contract documentation | Bisa membuat config catalog per service |

## Mini Practice

Untuk satu service Java/Jersey yang sedang Anda onboarding, buat inventory seperti ini:

```text id="5b3aor"
1. JVM flags
2. Java system properties
3. Environment variables
4. Mounted config files
5. MyBatis config
6. Jersey ResourceConfig properties
7. Camunda config
8. DB pool config
9. HTTP client config
10. Feature flags
11. Secrets
12. Defaults
```

Lalu jawab:

| Pertanyaan | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Config mana yang required? | Fail-fast contract |
| Config mana yang secret? | Redaction/security |
| Config mana yang boleh berubah runtime? | Dynamic config boundary |
| Config mana yang punya unsafe default? | Production hardening |
| Config mana yang tidak terdokumentasi? | Onboarding gap |
| Config mana yang beda antara local, CI, UAT, prod? | Drift detection |

# Seri 4/12 - JVM Memory Model: Heap, Non-Heap, Native Memory, RSS, dan OOM

## Introduction

Seri ini fokus pada satu mental model penting:

```text
Memory Java process ≠ hanya heap.
```

Di production, khususnya Docker/Kubernetes, banyak engineer hanya melihat `-Xmx`, lalu mengira service aman. Padahal process Java memakai beberapa area memory:

```text
Total process memory / RSS
=
Java heap
+ metaspace / class metadata
+ code cache
+ thread stacks
+ direct buffer / off-heap buffer
+ mapped files
+ JNI / native libraries
+ GC internal structures
+ JIT compiler memory
+ libc/native allocator overhead
+ application/native agent overhead
```

Oracle mendokumentasikan `-Xmx` sebagai batas maksimum Java heap, tetapi JVM process tetap memiliki memory lain di luar heap. Untuk melihat native memory HotSpot, Oracle menyediakan **Native Memory Tracking / NMT**, yang dapat diakses dengan `jcmd`, meskipun NMT tidak melacak semua alokasi third-party native code. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/specs/man/java.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Di Kubernetes, memory limit ditegakkan oleh kernel; jika container memakai memory melebihi limit, container dapat dihentikan dengan OOM kill. Jadi pod bisa `OOMKilled` walaupun Java heap belum penuh. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/manage-resources-containers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Diagram

```text
JVM Process Memory Model
│
├─ 1. Java Heap
│  ├─ young generation / young regions
│  ├─ old generation / old regions
│  ├─ object allocation
│  ├─ live set
│  ├─ temporary objects
│  └─ application object graph
│
├─ 2. Non-Heap JVM Memory
│  ├─ metaspace
│  │  ├─ class metadata
│  │  ├─ reflection metadata
│  │  └─ generated/proxy classes
│  │
│  ├─ compressed class space
│  ├─ code cache
│  │  ├─ JIT compiled methods
│  │  └─ runtime stubs
│  │
│  └─ GC internal structures
│
├─ 3. Thread Memory
│  ├─ Java thread stacks
│  ├─ native thread stacks
│  ├─ request threads
│  ├─ worker threads
│  ├─ DB pool threads
│  ├─ scheduler threads
│  └─ Camunda job executor threads
│
├─ 4. Off-Heap / Native Memory
│  ├─ direct ByteBuffer
│  ├─ NIO buffers
│  ├─ mmap / memory-mapped files
│  ├─ JNI native allocations
│  ├─ compression / crypto / image / media native libs
│  ├─ monitoring agents
│  └─ libc allocator overhead
│
├─ 5. Container / OS View
│  ├─ RSS
│  ├─ virtual memory
│  ├─ cgroup memory limit
│  ├─ page cache / mapped files
│  └─ OOM killer
│
└─ 6. Diagnostics
   ├─ jcmd VM.flags
   ├─ jcmd VM.native_memory
   ├─ jcmd GC.heap_info
   ├─ jcmd Thread.print
   ├─ jmap heap dump
   ├─ JFR allocation profile
   ├─ GC logs
   └─ kubectl describe pod
```

---

## Mental Model Utama

```text
-Xmx only limits Java heap.
Container memory limit limits the whole process.
```

Contoh:

```text
Kubernetes memory limit = 1024 MiB
-Xmx                  = 768 MiB

Sisa non-heap/native     = 256 MiB
```

Sisa 256 MiB itu harus cukup untuk:

```text
metaspace
code cache
thread stacks
direct buffers
JIT compiler
GC structures
native libs
JFR / agents
temporary native allocations
```

Kalau tidak cukup, gejalanya bisa:

```text
Pod OOMKilled
exit code 137
tidak ada java.lang.OutOfMemoryError
heap metrics terlihat normal
```

---

## Tabular Decomposition Map

| # | Area / Skill | Penjelasan praktis | Relevansi untuk Java/Jersey microservice | Failure mode | Evidence / Command |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Java Heap** | Area utama object Java dialokasikan. `-Xmx` membatasi maksimum heap. Oracle `java` command mendokumentasikan `-Xmx` sebagai maximum size memory allocation pool. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/specs/man/java.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | DTO, entity, JSON object, collection, MyBatis result, Camunda variable. | `java.lang.OutOfMemoryError: Java heap space`, GC thrash. | `jcmd <pid> GC.heap_info`, heap dump, GC log, JFR allocation. |
| 2 | **Initial Heap** | Heap awal yang disiapkan JVM. | Berpengaruh ke startup dan resizing heap. | Heap terlalu kecil → early GC; terlalu besar → RSS awal tinggi. | `-Xms`, `jcmd VM.flags`. |
| 3 | **Maximum Heap** | Batas maksimum heap. | Tuning utama untuk service memory budget. | `-Xmx` terlalu tinggi dalam container → native memory starvation. | `-Xmx`, `-XX:MaxRAMPercentage`. |
| 4 | **Heap Ergonomics** | JVM dapat memilih heap berdasarkan environment dan flag seperti RAM percentage. Oracle docs mendokumentasikan `MaxRAMPercentage`, `InitialRAMPercentage`, dan `MinRAMPercentage`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/specs/man/java.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Praktis untuk Docker/Kubernetes agar heap mengikuti limit. | Default percentage tidak cocok untuk pod kecil/besar. | `java -XshowSettings:vm -version`, `jcmd VM.flags`. |
| 5 | **Young / Old Memory Concept** | Banyak collector membedakan object baru dan object lama, meski detailnya tergantung collector. | High allocation dari JSON/mapper akan menekan young collection. | Minor/young GC terlalu sering, promotion pressure. | GC log, JFR allocation. |
| 6 | **Live Set** | Object yang benar-benar masih reachable dan harus dipertahankan GC. | Cache, session, pending job, queue, large result list. | Live set mendekati `Xmx`, GC tidak bisa reclaim cukup. | Heap dump dominator tree, GC after-full usage. |
| 7 | **Allocation Rate** | Kecepatan object baru dibuat. Tinggi allocation rate bisa menekan GC walau tidak leak. | JSON serialization, MapStruct/MyBatis mapping, logging, exception creation. | CPU tinggi di GC, p99 latency naik. | JFR allocation, GC log frequency. |
| 8 | **Temporary Object Pressure** | Object short-lived yang cepat mati. | REST request/response biasanya banyak temporary object. | Young GC sering, CPU overhead. | JFR allocation by class/method. |
| 9 | **Object Retention** | Object tidak bocor secara teknis, tapi tertahan lebih lama dari perlu. | Queue tidak terkuras, cache tidak bounded, batch result list besar. | Heap naik perlahan, GC makin mahal. | Heap dump, retained size. |
| 10 | **Metaspace** | Menyimpan metadata class. Berada di luar Java heap. | Jersey provider, MyBatis mapper proxy, Camunda delegates, generated classes. | `OutOfMemoryError: Metaspace`, RSS naik walau heap normal. | `jcmd VM.metaspace`, `jcmd VM.native_memory`. |
| 11 | **Compressed Class Space** | Area untuk metadata class ketika compressed class pointers aktif. | Biasanya tidak disentuh langsung, tapi muncul di NMT. | Class metadata pressure. | NMT summary/detail. |
| 12 | **Code Cache** | Menyimpan hasil JIT compiled native code. | Service dengan banyak hot methods, framework, proxies. | Code cache penuh → JIT terbatas, performance turun. | `jcmd Compiler.codecache`, JFR compiler events. |
| 13 | **JIT Compiler Memory** | Memory untuk compiler thread dan struktur JIT. | Startup/warmup dan hot path optimization. | CPU/memory spike saat warmup. | JFR, compiler logs. |
| 14 | **Thread Stack** | Setiap thread punya stack native. Ukuran dikontrol `-Xss`. | Request thread, worker pool, DB pool, scheduler, Camunda job executor. | Terlalu banyak thread → native memory habis; stack terlalu kecil → `StackOverflowError`. | `jcmd Thread.print`, `-Xss`, NMT Thread category. |
| 15 | **Direct ByteBuffer** | Off-heap memory untuk NIO/direct buffer. | HTTP client/server, file processing, Netty-based libs, compression, DB drivers. | RSS naik, heap normal; direct buffer OOM. | NMT, JFR, buffer pool MBeans. |
| 16 | **Mapped Files / mmap** | File dipetakan ke memory. | Large file processing, Lucene-like libs, PDF/media processing. | RSS/page cache membingungkan, file descriptor pressure. | `pmap`, `/proc/<pid>/smaps`, NMT. |
| 17 | **JNI / Native Library Memory** | Native allocations dari JNI atau library native. | OCR, media processing, compression, crypto, monitoring agents. | Native leak tidak terlihat di heap dump. | NMT sebagian, OS tools, vendor diagnostics. |
| 18 | **GC Internal Structures** | Collector butuh memory sendiri untuk card table, remembered sets, marking bitmap, region metadata. | G1/ZGC punya internal overhead. | Heap sizing terlalu agresif menyisakan native kecil. | NMT GC category, GC logs. |
| 19 | **JFR / Diagnostics Overhead** | Recording, buffers, dan diagnostics juga pakai memory. | Profiling production. | Recording terlalu panjang/detail → overhead. | `jcmd JFR.check`, JFR settings. |
| 20 | **Agent Memory** | APM, profiler, security agent, instrumentation agent memakai heap/native/code cache. | New Relic, Datadog, OpenTelemetry agent, security scanner. | RSS naik setelah agent ditambahkan. | NMT, process comparison with/without agent. |
| 21 | **RSS** | Resident Set Size: memory fisik yang sedang resident untuk process. | Metrik utama container memory usage. | RSS > cgroup limit → OOMKill. | `ps`, `top`, `kubectl top pod`. |
| 22 | **Virtual Memory** | Address space yang dicadangkan, tidak selalu resident. | JVM reserve heap/metaspace/code cache. | Salah baca VIRT sebagai memory benar-benar dipakai. | `top`, `pmap`. |
| 23 | **cgroup Memory Limit** | Limit memory container. Kubernetes menyatakan memory limits ditegakkan kernel dengan OOM kills. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/manage-resources-containers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Semua pod Java production. | Pod mati tanpa Java OOM. | `kubectl describe pod`, container status, exit code 137. |
| 24 | **Java OOM vs Kernel OOM** | Java OOM dilempar JVM; kernel OOMKill membunuh process. | Penting untuk root cause. | Salah analisis: heap dump dicari padahal process dibunuh kernel. | Java logs vs pod events. |
| 25 | **Heap Dump** | Snapshot heap object graph. | Debug heap leak/retention. | File sangat besar, disk penuh, sensitive data bocor. | `-XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError`, `jmap -dump`. |
| 26 | **Native Memory Tracking** | HotSpot feature untuk melihat internal native memory category. Oracle menyebut NMT dapat diakses via `jcmd`, tetapi tidak melacak semua third-party native allocations. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/vm/native-memory-tracking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Debug RSS tinggi, non-heap/native memory. | NMT belum aktif karena harus diset saat start. | `-XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary`, `jcmd VM.native_memory`. |
| 27 | **Memory Leak Diagnosis** | Bedakan leak, retention, pressure, fragmentation, cache growth. Oracle troubleshooting guide menyebut memory leak terjadi saat virtual memory dialokasikan tetapi tidak dikembalikan saat tidak lagi dibutuhkan. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/troubleshoot/troubleshooting-memory-leaks.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Incident memory naik perlahan. | Naikkan `Xmx` hanya menunda crash. | Heap dump diff, NMT baseline/diff, JFR. |
| 28 | **Memory Budgeting** | Membagi limit container untuk heap dan non-heap. | Wajib di Kubernetes. | `Xmx` terlalu dekat dengan pod limit. | Budget sheet, `kubectl top`, `jcmd`. |
| 29 | **GC Pressure vs Leak** | GC pressure bisa karena allocation tinggi, bukan leak. | Endpoint high-throughput. | Salah diagnosis leak padahal allocation rate tinggi. | JFR allocation rate, GC log. |
| 30 | **Large Object / Humongous Allocation** | Object besar dapat berdampak khusus pada GC tertentu seperti G1. | File upload, large JSON, Excel/PDF generation, byte array besar. | Pause spike, fragmentation, full GC. | GC log humongous events, heap histogram. |
| 31 | **Thread Explosion** | Jumlah thread tidak terkendali menghabiskan native memory. | Misconfigured executor, unbounded scheduler, per-request thread. | Native OOM, pod OOMKilled. | `jcmd Thread.print | grep`, NMT Thread. |
| 32 | **Direct Memory Limit** | Direct buffer dapat dibatasi dengan `-XX:MaxDirectMemorySize`, tetapi default/behavior perlu dicek di JDK target. | NIO-heavy apps. | Direct buffer OOM atau RSS tinggi. | BufferPool MBean, NMT. |
| 33 | **Container Observability Gap** | JVM metrics bisa sehat, container metrics naik. | Heap normal, RSS naik. | Fokus hanya Prometheus JVM heap metrics. | Combine JMX + container metrics + NMT. |
| 34 | **Memory-Safe Rollout** | Perubahan heap/pool/thread/config harus diuji under load. | Production tuning. | Naikkan heap menurunkan native headroom. | Before/after dashboard. |

---

## Memory Areas Detail

### 1. Java Heap

Heap adalah area object Java:

```text
DTO
List / Map / Set
String
byte[]
JSON tree
XML object model
MyBatis result object
Camunda variable object
exception stack trace
cache entry
```

Flag utama:

```bash
-Xms512m
-Xmx1024m
```

Container-aware alternative:

```bash
-XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=70.0
```

Prinsip:

```text
Xmx besar tidak selalu lebih baik.
Xmx terlalu kecil  → GC terlalu sering / OOM heap.
Xmx terlalu besar  → native memory kekurangan / pod OOMKilled.
```

---

### 2. Metaspace

Metaspace menyimpan metadata class. Ini penting untuk framework-heavy Java:

```text
Jersey resources/providers
MyBatis mapper proxies
Camunda delegates/listeners
Jackson/Jakarta JSON/XML related classes
dynamic proxies
reflection metadata
generated classes
```

Gejala metaspace problem:

```text
java.lang.OutOfMemoryError: Metaspace
class count naik terus
redeploy/plugin/classloader leak
RSS naik walau heap stabil
```

Command:

```bash
jcmd <pid> VM.metaspace
jcmd <pid> VM.classloader_stats
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory summary
```

---

### 3. Code Cache

Code cache menyimpan hasil kompilasi JIT:

```text
hot method native code
runtime stubs
compiled framework paths
compiled serializers/mappers
```

Command:

```bash
jcmd <pid> Compiler.codecache
jcmd <pid> Compiler.codelist
```

Gejala:

```text
performance turun setelah running lama
JIT compilation disabled/limited
warning code cache full
```

---

### 4. Thread Stack

Setiap thread punya stack. Jika thread count besar, total stack memory bisa signifikan.

Contoh kasar:

```text
300 threads × 1 MiB stack = 300 MiB native memory reserved/used depending OS/JVM behavior
```

Thread yang umum di microservice:

```text
HTTP request worker
Jersey/container thread
DB pool helper
scheduler
Camunda job executor
async executor
Kafka/RabbitMQ consumer
JMX/JFR/agent thread
GC/JIT/compiler thread
```

Flag:

```bash
-Xss512k
-Xss1m
```

Command:

```bash
jcmd <pid> Thread.print
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory summary
```

Anti-pattern:

```text
Unbounded executor
new Thread per request
pool size mengikuti CPU tanpa melihat memory
Camunda worker/job thread terlalu banyak
DB pool > DB capacity
```

---

### 5. Direct / Off-Heap Memory

Direct memory sering muncul dari:

```text
java.nio.ByteBuffer.allocateDirect
HTTP client/server internals
file/channel processing
compression
TLS/crypto
Netty-based library
database driver buffering
media/OCR processing
```

Gejala:

```text
heap usage normal
RSS terus naik
pod OOMKilled
heap dump tidak menunjukkan object besar
```

Evidence:

```bash
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory summary
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory detail
jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram
```

JMX yang sering berguna:

```text
java.nio:type=BufferPool,name=direct
java.nio:type=BufferPool,name=mapped
```

---

## Java OOM vs Kubernetes OOMKilled

| Aspek | Java OOM | Kubernetes / Kernel OOMKill |
|---|---|---|
| Siapa yang mendeteksi? | JVM | Kernel/cgroup |
| Error Java? | Ada `java.lang.OutOfMemoryError` | Biasanya tidak ada Java exception |
| Process sempat handle? | Kadang bisa log/dump | Process langsung dibunuh |
| Heap dump otomatis? | Bisa jika `HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError` aktif | Tidak, karena bukan Java OOM |
| Exit code | Tergantung JVM/app | Sering 137 |
| Evidence utama | Java log, heap dump, GC log | `kubectl describe pod`, container status, node event |
| Root cause umum | Heap/metaspace/direct OOM | Total RSS > memory limit |

Kubernetes mendokumentasikan bahwa memory limit container dapat menyebabkan kernel melakukan OOM kill saat container melebihi limit. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/manage-resources-containers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## OOM Taxonomy

| Symptom | Kemungkinan jenis OOM | Area | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `java.lang.OutOfMemoryError: Java heap space` | Heap OOM | Heap | Heap dump, GC log |
| `java.lang.OutOfMemoryError: GC overhead limit exceeded` | Heap pressure berat | Heap/GC | GC log, heap after GC |
| `java.lang.OutOfMemoryError: Metaspace` | Metaspace OOM | Non-heap | `VM.metaspace`, classloader stats |
| `java.lang.OutOfMemoryError: Direct buffer memory` | Direct memory OOM | Off-heap | BufferPool MBean, NMT |
| `java.lang.OutOfMemoryError: unable to create native thread` | Thread/native OOM | Native/thread stack | thread count, `ulimit`, NMT Thread |
| Pod `OOMKilled`, exit 137 | Container memory limit exceeded | Whole process RSS | `kubectl describe pod`, container metrics |
| `hs_err_pid*.log` | Fatal JVM crash/native crash | JVM/native | error file, core dump |
| Linux `Killed` only | Kernel killed process | OS/cgroup | dmesg/node events/container status |

---

## Memory Budgeting for Kubernetes

### Formula praktis

```text
container_memory_limit
=
heap
+ metaspace
+ code cache
+ thread stacks
+ direct/mapped buffers
+ GC/JIT/JVM internals
+ agents
+ native libraries
+ safety margin
```

### Example 1 - 1 GiB pod

```text
Pod memory limit:       1024 MiB

Suggested starting budget:
  Heap max:              512-650 MiB
  Metaspace/code cache:   80-150 MiB
  Thread stacks:          80-200 MiB
  Direct/native/agents:   80-200 MiB
  Safety margin:          80-150 MiB
```

Possible JVM args:

```bash
-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
-XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
-Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
-XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
-XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
```

### Example 2 - 4 GiB pod

```text
Pod memory limit:       4096 MiB

Starting budget:
  Heap max:             2500-3000 MiB
  Non-heap/native:       700-1100 MiB
  Safety margin:         300-600 MiB
```

Possible JVM args:

```bash
-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=70.0
-XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
-Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
-XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary
```

### Jangan otomatis pakai 75% untuk semua service

`MaxRAMPercentage=75` sering masuk akal untuk service sederhana, tetapi berisiko untuk service yang punya:

```text
banyak thread
banyak direct buffer
APM/security agent berat
file/media processing
large native libraries
Camunda worker banyak
high DB/HTTP client concurrency
```

---

## Diagnostic Commands

### 1. Lihat JVM flags aktif

```bash
jcmd <pid> VM.flags
jcmd <pid> VM.command_line
```

Tujuan:

```text
Validasi Xmx / MaxRAMPercentage / Xss / NMT / GC flags benar-benar aktif.
```

---

### 2. Lihat heap info

```bash
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_info
```

Untuk snapshot ringkas heap.

---

### 3. Heap histogram

```bash
jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram
```

Untuk melihat class object terbanyak/terbesar secara cepat.

---

### 4. Heap dump

```bash
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_dump /dumps/heap-$(date +%s).hprof
```

Atau saat OOM:

```bash
-XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
-XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
```

Catatan: heap dump bisa sangat besar dan mengandung data sensitif.

---

### 5. Native Memory Tracking

NMT harus diaktifkan saat JVM start:

```bash
-XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary
```

Lalu:

```bash
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory summary
```

Untuk detail:

```bash
-XX:NativeMemoryTracking=detail
```

```bash
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory detail
```

NMT menyediakan kategori internal HotSpot dan bisa diakses via `jcmd`, tetapi Oracle mencatat NMT tidak melacak semua third-party native code allocations. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/vm/native-memory-tracking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

### 6. Thread dump

```bash
jcmd <pid> Thread.print
```

Hitung thread kasar:

```bash
jcmd <pid> Thread.print | grep -c '^"'
```

---

### 7. Kubernetes evidence

```bash
kubectl describe pod <pod-name>
```

Cari:

```text
State
Last State
Reason: OOMKilled
Exit Code: 137
Started / Finished
Restart Count
```

Metrics:

```bash
kubectl top pod <pod-name>
kubectl top node
```

---

### 8. Linux process memory

Di dalam container:

```bash
ps -o pid,rss,vsz,comm -p 1
cat /proc/1/status | egrep 'VmRSS|VmSize|Threads'
```

Jika available:

```bash
pmap -x 1 | tail -n 20
```

---

## JVM Memory Baseline Template

Untuk service Java/Jersey umum di Kubernetes:

```bash
java \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0 \
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta \
  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8 \
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps \
  -XX:ErrorFile=/dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

Untuk investigasi native/RSS:

```bash
java \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0 \
  -XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

Untuk service dengan banyak thread:

```bash
java \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0 \
  -Xss512k \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

Catatan: `-Xss512k` harus diuji. Jangan turunkan stack sembarangan kalau call stack dalam, recursion, parser, atau framework stack berat.

---

## Memory Failure Pattern di Java/Jersey/MyBatis/Camunda

| Pattern | Contoh | Gejala | Evidence | Fix direction |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Large JSON request | Request body besar di-buffer penuh | Heap spike, GC pause | JFR allocation, heap histogram | streaming, request limit, pagination |
| MyBatis select tanpa limit | Query result sangat besar | Heap OOM | heap dump: list/entity besar | pagination, cursor/streaming, query limit |
| Camunda variable besar | Object/blob variable disimpan/dibaca besar | Heap/DB pressure | Camunda variable table, heap dump | store reference not payload, externalize large data |
| Unbounded cache | Map/cache tidak dibatasi | Heap naik perlahan | heap dump retained size | bounded cache, TTL, eviction |
| Thread pool terlalu besar | Executor/Camunda worker banyak | RSS naik, native thread OOM | thread dump, NMT Thread | bound pool, tune queue/backpressure |
| Direct buffer leak | NIO/file/media lib | RSS naik, heap normal | NMT, BufferPool MBean | close resource, update lib, limit direct memory |
| Classloader leak | Dynamic loading/redeploy | metaspace naik | classloader stats | fix static refs, lifecycle cleanup |
| APM agent overhead | Agent baru dipasang | RSS/code cache/metaspace naik | compare before/after, NMT | tune agent, sampling, memory budget |
| Excessive exceptions | Exception untuk normal flow | allocation tinggi | JFR exception/allocation | use validation/result type |
| Large file generation | Excel/PDF/ZIP di memory | heap/direct spike | allocation, heap dump | stream to file/object storage |

---

## Reading NMT Summary

Contoh kategori yang biasanya muncul:

```text
Native Memory Tracking:

Total: reserved=..., committed=...

- Java Heap
- Class
- Thread
- Code
- GC
- Compiler
- Internal
- Symbol
- Native Memory Tracking
- Arena Chunk
```

Cara membaca:

| NMT Category | Interpretasi |
|---|---|
| Java Heap | Heap reserved/committed |
| Class | Class metadata/metaspace/compressed class space |
| Thread | Thread stack/native thread memory |
| Code | Code cache/JIT compiled code |
| GC | Struktur internal garbage collector |
| Compiler | JIT compiler memory |
| Internal | JVM internal allocations |
| Symbol | Symbols/string/class metadata internals |
| NMT | Overhead Native Memory Tracking sendiri |
| Arena Chunk | Arena allocator internal |

Gunakan NMT untuk membedakan:

```text
Heap problem?
Class/metaspace problem?
Thread explosion?
Code cache issue?
GC overhead?
Native/internal growth?
```

---

## Practical Incident Playbook

### Case A - `Java heap space`

```text
1. Cek GC log.
2. Ambil heap dump.
3. Cek class histogram.
4. Cari dominator terbesar.
5. Bedakan leak vs request/batch besar.
6. Fix retention/allocation.
7. Baru pertimbangkan naikkan heap.
```

Commands:

```bash
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_info
jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_dump /dumps/heap.hprof
```

---

### Case B - Pod `OOMKilled`, no Java OOM

```text
1. Cek kubectl describe pod.
2. Cek exit code 137 / Reason OOMKilled.
3. Compare container RSS vs JVM heap metrics.
4. Cek JVM flags: Xmx / MaxRAMPercentage / Xss.
5. Cek thread count.
6. Aktifkan NMT di next rollout jika belum aktif.
7. Turunkan heap atau kurangi native/thread/direct memory.
```

Commands:

```bash
kubectl describe pod <pod>
kubectl top pod <pod>
jcmd 1 VM.flags
jcmd 1 Thread.print
jcmd 1 VM.native_memory summary
```

---

### Case C - Heap normal, RSS naik

```text
1. Suspect native/off-heap/thread/metaspace.
2. Cek NMT.
3. Cek BufferPool MBeans.
4. Cek thread count.
5. Cek metaspace/classloader stats.
6. Cek agent/native library changes.
7. Compare before/after deployment.
```

Commands:

```bash
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory summary
jcmd <pid> VM.metaspace
jcmd <pid> VM.classloader_stats
jcmd <pid> Thread.print
```

---

### Case D - `unable to create native thread`

```text
1. Hitung thread.
2. Cek -Xss.
3. Cek OS/container limits.
4. Cek executor/pool config.
5. Cek thread leak.
6. Kurangi thread atau stack size dengan testing.
```

Commands:

```bash
jcmd <pid> Thread.print
cat /proc/<pid>/status | grep Threads
ulimit -a
```

---

## Production Memory SLO / Dashboard

Minimal panel yang harus ada:

| Metric | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Container memory RSS / working set | Apakah mendekati limit |
| JVM heap used / max | Heap pressure |
| JVM non-heap used | Metaspace/code cache trend |
| GC pause p95/p99 | Latency impact |
| GC frequency | Allocation/pressure indicator |
| Allocation rate | Pressure source |
| Thread count | Native memory/thread leak |
| Direct buffer memory | Off-heap pressure |
| Metaspace used | Classloader/generated class issue |
| Pod restarts / OOMKilled | Hard failure |
| DB pool active/waiting | Memory + thread blocking correlation |
| Request p95/p99 | User impact |

---

## Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa salah | Yang benar |
|---|---|---|
| `Xmx = pod memory limit` | Tidak ada ruang untuk native/non-heap | Sisakan native headroom |
| Semua OOM dianggap heap leak | Kernel OOMKill bisa tanpa Java OOM | Bedakan Java OOM vs pod OOMKilled |
| Naikkan heap untuk semua memory issue | Bisa mempercepat container OOM | Cari area memory yang tumbuh |
| Tidak aktifkan GC log | Kehilangan evidence | Aktifkan `-Xlog:gc*` baseline |
| Heap dump tanpa volume | Dump gagal atau memenuhi disk | Mount `/dumps` dengan quota |
| Abaikan thread count | Thread stack makan native memory | Monitor thread count |
| Abaikan direct memory | Heap normal tapi RSS naik | Monitor BufferPool/NMT |
| Pakai NMT detail terus-menerus tanpa alasan | Overhead lebih besar | Pakai summary default investigasi, detail saat perlu |
| Load test hanya lihat heap | Container bisa mati karena RSS | Lihat JVM + container metrics |
| Config pool/thread tanpa memory budget | Pool besar meningkatkan memory | Budget thread/connection/object |

---

## Checklist Onboarding Memory untuk Service Baru

| Checklist | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Runtime memory limit | Berapa pod/container memory limit? |
| Heap config | Pakai `-Xmx` atau `MaxRAMPercentage`? |
| Native headroom | Berapa sisa setelah heap? |
| Thread count baseline | Berapa thread idle dan under load? |
| DB pool size | Berapa max connection dan waiting behavior? |
| HTTP client pool | Apakah bounded? |
| Camunda worker/job config | Berapa worker/acquisition concurrency? |
| File/media processing | Apakah ada byte array besar/direct buffer/native lib? |
| Agent | APM/security/profiler apa yang aktif? |
| GC log | Sudah aktif dan tersimpan? |
| Heap dump path | Writable dan cukup space? |
| NMT | Aktif di non-prod atau saat investigasi? |
| Dashboard | Ada heap + RSS + non-heap + thread + GC? |

---

| Step | Fokus | Target |

| 1 | Heap vs RSS | Bisa menjelaskan kenapa `Xmx` bukan total memory |

## Mini Practice

Ambil satu pod Java service, lalu jalankan:

```bash
kubectl top pod <pod>
kubectl describe pod <pod>
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.flags
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 GC.heap_info
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 Thread.print
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- cat /proc/1/status | egrep 'VmRSS|VmSize|Threads'
```

Jika NMT aktif:

```bash
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.native_memory summary
```

Lalu isi tabel:

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Pod memory limit |  |
| Current RSS |  |
| Max heap |  |
| Current heap used |  |
| Thread count |  |
| Metaspace used |  |
| Direct/mapped buffer estimate |  |
| GC frequency |  |
| Last restart reason |  |

# Seri 5/12 - Heap Sizing for Docker/Kubernetes

## Introduction

Seri ini fokus pada **cara menentukan heap JVM secara aman di container**, terutama Docker/Kubernetes.

Masalah paling umum:

```text
Pod memory limit = 1Gi
-Xmx = 1Gi
```

Ini terlihat masuk akal, tetapi sebenarnya berbahaya karena `-Xmx` hanya membatasi **Java heap**, bukan seluruh memory process. Java process tetap butuh metaspace, code cache, thread stack, direct buffer, GC internal memory, native library, JIT, agent, dan OS/runtime overhead. Oracle mendokumentasikan `-Xmx` sebagai maksimum heap, dan `-Xmx` ekuivalen dengan `-XX:MaxHeapSize`, bukan total process memory. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/specs/man/java.html))

Di Kubernetes, `memory limit` ditegakkan oleh kernel/cgroup. Jika container memakai memory melebihi limit, process dapat dihentikan dengan OOM kill; dokumentasi Kubernetes juga menunjukkan `Reason: OOMKilled` dan `Exit Code: 137` sebagai bukti container melewati memory limit. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/manage-resources-containers/))

---

## Diagram

```text
Heap Sizing for Java in Docker/Kubernetes
│
├─ 1. Container Memory Limit
│  ├─ Kubernetes resources.limits.memory
│  ├─ Docker --memory
│  ├─ cgroup memory limit
│  └─ kernel OOM enforcement
│
├─ 2. JVM Container Awareness
│  ├─ UseContainerSupport enabled by default on Linux HotSpot
│  ├─ JVM sees container memory/CPU constraints
│  ├─ MaxRAM / MaxRAMPercentage
│  └─ ActiveProcessorCount
│
├─ 3. Java Heap Budget
│  ├─ -Xms
│  ├─ -Xmx
│  ├─ -XX:InitialRAMPercentage
│  ├─ -XX:MaxRAMPercentage
│  └─ live set + allocation headroom
│
├─ 4. Non-Heap / Native Budget
│  ├─ metaspace
│  ├─ code cache
│  ├─ thread stacks
│  ├─ direct buffers
│  ├─ GC internal structures
│  ├─ JIT/compiler memory
│  ├─ native libraries / agents
│  └─ safety margin
│
├─ 5. Runtime Evidence
│  ├─ jcmd VM.flags
│  ├─ jcmd GC.heap_info
│  ├─ jcmd VM.native_memory
│  ├─ jcmd Thread.print
│  ├─ GC logs
│  ├─ JFR
│  └─ kubectl describe/top pod
│
└─ 6. Sizing Loop
   ├─ estimate
   ├─ deploy
   ├─ load test
   ├─ observe RSS / heap / GC / p99
   ├─ adjust
   └─ document baseline
```

---

## Core Rule

```text
Container memory limit must be larger than JVM heap.
```

Formula praktis:

```text
container_memory_limit
=
max_heap
+ metaspace
+ code_cache
+ thread_stacks
+ direct_buffers
+ GC/JIT/JVM internals
+ native libraries / agents
+ safety_margin
```

Atau dibalik:

```text
safe_max_heap
=
container_memory_limit
- non_heap_native_budget
- safety_margin
```

---

## Tabular Decomposition Map

| # | Category / Skill | Penjelasan praktis | Flag / artefak penting | Kapan dipakai | Failure mode |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Container memory limit** | Batas total memory container, bukan batas heap. Kubernetes menegakkan memory limit melalui kernel/cgroup dan container bisa dihentikan bila melebihi limit. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/manage-resources-containers/)) | `resources.limits.memory`, `docker --memory` | Semua Java service di Docker/K8s. | Pod `OOMKilled`, exit code 137. |
| 2 | **Memory request vs limit** | Request dipakai scheduler untuk placement; limit adalah batas runtime. Kubernetes menyatakan request dipakai kube-scheduler, sedangkan kubelet/kernel menegakkan limit. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/manage-resources-containers/)) | `resources.requests.memory`, `resources.limits.memory` | Capacity planning dan scheduling. | Request terlalu kecil → node pressure/eviction risk; limit terlalu kecil → OOMKilled. |
| 3 | **JVM container awareness** | HotSpot Linux mendukung container detection agar JVM melihat memory/CPU yang tersedia di container. Oracle mendokumentasikan `UseContainerSupport` default `true`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/specs/man/java.html)) | `-XX:+UseContainerSupport`, `-Xlog:os+container=trace` | Validasi JVM membaca cgroup limit. | JVM sizing berdasarkan node memory, bukan container limit, terutama pada runtime/JDK lama atau konfigurasi tidak standar. |
| 4 | **Fixed heap sizing** | Heap ditentukan eksplisit. | `-Xms512m`, `-Xmx1024m` | Service stabil dengan memory requirement jelas. | Hardcoded heap tidak cocok untuk environment berbeda. |
| 5 | **Percentage-based heap sizing** | Heap dihitung dari memory yang tersedia untuk JVM. Oracle mendokumentasikan `MaxRAMPercentage` sebagai persentase maksimum memory untuk Java heap, default 25%. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/specs/man/java.html)) | `-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0` | K8s multi-env dengan limit berbeda. | Persentase terlalu tinggi menyisakan native headroom terlalu kecil. |
| 6 | **Initial heap sizing** | Heap awal bisa fixed atau percentage-based. Oracle mendokumentasikan `InitialRAMPercentage` untuk initial heap, default 1.5625%. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/specs/man/java.html)) | `-Xms`, `-XX:InitialRAMPercentage` | Startup, warmup, latency awal. | Initial heap terlalu kecil → GC awal sering; terlalu besar → RSS startup tinggi. |
| 7 | **Max heap sizing** | Batas heap maksimum. | `-Xmx`, `-XX:MaxHeapSize`, `-XX:MaxRAMPercentage` | Batas utama object allocation. | `Xmx` terlalu dekat dengan container limit. |
| 8 | **Native headroom** | Sisa memory untuk metaspace, stack, direct buffer, code cache, agents, GC/JIT. | Budget sheet, NMT | Wajib sebelum set heap. | Heap terlihat aman, tapi RSS melewati limit. |
| 9 | **Thread stack budget** | Tiap thread membutuhkan stack. Banyak worker/thread berarti native memory naik. | `-Xss`, `jcmd Thread.print` | Jersey request threads, Camunda workers, DB pool, scheduler. | `unable to create native thread`, pod OOMKilled. |
| 10 | **Metaspace budget** | Class metadata di luar heap. | `jcmd VM.metaspace`, `-XX:MaxMetaspaceSize` | Framework-heavy app: Jersey, MyBatis, Camunda, proxies. | `OutOfMemoryError: Metaspace`. |
| 11 | **Code cache budget** | JIT compiled methods memakai code cache. | `jcmd Compiler.codecache` | Long-running service dengan banyak hot code. | Code cache full, JIT terbatas, throughput turun. |
| 12 | **Direct buffer budget** | Off-heap buffer untuk NIO, file, HTTP, DB, media processing. | BufferPool MBean, NMT | File upload/download, large JSON, media/OCR, network-heavy app. | Heap normal, RSS naik. |
| 13 | **GC internal overhead** | GC perlu struktur internal sendiri. | NMT `GC`, GC logs | G1/ZGC/large heap. | Heap sizing terlalu agresif mengabaikan GC overhead. |
| 14 | **APM/agent overhead** | Monitoring/security/profiler agent memakai heap/native/code cache. | Agent config, NMT, JFR | Datadog/New Relic/OpenTelemetry/security agent. | Memory naik setelah agent dipasang. |
| 15 | **Heap live set** | Object yang tetap hidup setelah GC. | Heap dump, GC after-collection usage | Menentukan minimal heap realistis. | Heap terlalu kecil untuk live set → GC thrash. |
| 16 | **Allocation headroom** | Ruang tambahan untuk object sementara saat request traffic. | JFR allocation rate, GC log | REST API high throughput. | Young GC terlalu sering, p99 naik. |
| 17 | **Safety margin** | Buffer untuk spike, fragmentation, native overhead, observability. | 10-25% dari container limit | Semua production service. | Sizing terlalu presisi → fragile saat traffic spike. |
| 18 | **Heap dump space** | Heap dump bisa sebesar heap atau lebih. | `-XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps` | OOM investigation. | Dump gagal atau disk penuh. |
| 19 | **tmpfs emptyDir memory impact** | Kubernetes mencatat memory-backed `emptyDir` dihitung sebagai container memory usage. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/manage-resources-containers/)) | `emptyDir.medium: Memory` | Temp file, upload, generated reports. | File temporary menghabiskan memory limit. |
| 20 | **Runtime verification** | Effective heap harus dicek di process aktual. | `jcmd VM.flags`, `jcmd GC.heap_info` | Setelah deploy. | Helm/README bilang A, JVM runtime pakai B. |
| 21 | **NMT investigation** | Native Memory Tracking membantu melihat kategori memory internal HotSpot. NMT harus diaktifkan saat startup dan diakses via `jcmd`; Oracle mencatat NMT off by default dan summary/detail punya overhead. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/vm/native-memory-tracking.html)) | `-XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary`, `jcmd VM.native_memory summary` | RSS tinggi, heap normal. | Tidak bisa pakai NMT karena lupa enable saat start. |
| 22 | **OOMKilled diagnosis** | Bedakan Java OOM dan kernel OOM. | `kubectl describe pod`, `Last State`, `Exit Code` | Pod restart tanpa Java exception. | Salah cari heap dump padahal process dibunuh kernel. |
| 23 | **CPU and heap interaction** | Heap terlalu kecil menaikkan GC frequency; heap terlalu besar bisa memperpanjang pause/working set. | GC logs, p99 latency | Performance tuning. | Naikkan heap tanpa cek latency/GC behavior. |
| 24 | **Sizing experiment discipline** | Sizing harus berdasarkan load test dan evidence. | before/after metrics | Production hardening. | Copy-paste angka dari service lain. |

---

## `-Xmx` vs `MaxRAMPercentage`

### Fixed heap

```bash
java -Xms512m -Xmx1024m -jar app.jar
```

Cocok jika:

```text
1. Pod memory limit stabil.
2. Service punya memory profile yang sudah diketahui.
3. Environment tidak banyak variasi.
4. Anda ingin hard cap eksplisit.
```

Risiko:

```text
1. Kalau limit pod turun, Xmx bisa terlalu besar.
2. Kalau limit pod naik, heap tetap kecil.
3. Native headroom harus dihitung manual.
```

---

### Percentage-based heap

```bash
java \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0 \
  -jar app.jar
```

Cocok jika:

```text
1. Pod memory limit berbeda per environment.
2. Anda ingin heap mengikuti cgroup/container memory.
3. Image yang sama dipakai dev/UAT/prod.
```

Risiko:

```text
1. Persentase yang aman untuk pod 4Gi belum tentu aman untuk pod 512Mi.
2. Service dengan banyak thread/direct buffer/native lib butuh persentase heap lebih rendah.
3. Default MaxRAMPercentage 25% mungkin terlalu kecil untuk service tertentu, tetapi menaikkannya tanpa budget native bisa berbahaya.
```

Oracle mendokumentasikan bahwa `MaxRAM` mempertimbangkan memory yang tersedia untuk JVM process, termasuk constraint dari environment seperti container, dan `MaxRAMPercentage` memakai basis tersebut untuk menghitung maksimum Java heap. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/specs/man/java.html))

---

## Sizing Formula Praktis

### Step 1 - Ambil container limit

```text
container_limit = Kubernetes resources.limits.memory
```

Contoh:

```yaml
resources:
  requests:
    memory: "512Mi"
    cpu: "500m"
  limits:
    memory: "1024Mi"
    cpu: "1000m"
```

Maka:

```text
container_limit = 1024 MiB
```

---

### Step 2 - Estimasi non-heap/native budget

```text
non_heap_native_budget =
  metaspace
+ code cache
+ thread stacks
+ direct buffers
+ GC/JIT internals
+ native libraries / agents
+ tmp / mmap / safety margin
```

Contoh service Jersey/MyBatis/Camunda sedang:

```text
metaspace                100 MiB
code cache                64 MiB
thread stacks            128 MiB
direct/native buffers    128 MiB
GC/JIT/internal          128 MiB
agent/observability       64 MiB
safety margin            128 MiB
--------------------------------
non-heap/native total    740 MiB
```

Kalau pod cuma 1Gi, angka di atas terlalu besar. Artinya Anda harus mengurangi thread/direct/agent, menaikkan limit, atau menurunkan heap expectation.

---

### Step 3 - Hitung safe heap

```text
safe_max_heap = container_limit - non_heap_native_budget
```

Contoh 1Gi pod:

```text
container_limit          1024 MiB
non_heap_native_budget    350 MiB
--------------------------------
safe_max_heap             674 MiB
```

Maka jangan set:

```bash
-Xmx1024m
```

Lebih aman mulai dari:

```bash
-Xmx640m
```

Atau percentage:

```bash
-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
```

---

## Practical Sizing Matrix

| Pod Memory Limit | Conservative Heap | Balanced Heap | Aggressive Heap | Catatan |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| 512 MiB | 220-280 MiB | 300-340 MiB | 360-400 MiB | Hati-hati metaspace/thread/agent. |
| 768 MiB | 350-420 MiB | 450-520 MiB | 550-600 MiB | Cocok untuk service kecil-sedang. |
| 1 GiB | 480-600 MiB | 620-700 MiB | 740-800 MiB | Jangan agresif jika banyak thread/direct buffer. |
| 2 GiB | 1.1-1.3 GiB | 1.4-1.5 GiB | 1.6 GiB | Umum untuk service menengah. |
| 4 GiB | 2.3-2.6 GiB | 2.8-3.1 GiB | 3.2-3.4 GiB | Perhatikan GC pause dan live set. |
| 8 GiB | 4.8-5.5 GiB | 5.8-6.4 GiB | 6.6-7.0 GiB | Butuh GC tuning/observability lebih matang. |

Ini bukan angka absolut. Ini **starting point** sebelum load test. Service dengan Camunda worker, file processing, OCR/media, banyak thread, atau APM berat harus lebih konservatif.

---

## Recommended Starting Profiles

### Profile A - Small REST API

Karakter:

```text
Jersey REST
MyBatis sederhana
DB pool kecil
sedikit background worker
tidak banyak file processing
```

Kubernetes:

```yaml
resources:
  requests:
    memory: "512Mi"
    cpu: "500m"
  limits:
    memory: "1024Mi"
    cpu: "1000m"
```

JVM:

```bash
java \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0 \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

---

### Profile B - Medium API + MyBatis + Camunda Worker

Karakter:

```text
REST API
MyBatis queries
Camunda job executor aktif
scheduler
DB pool sedang
APM agent aktif
```

Kubernetes:

```yaml
resources:
  requests:
    memory: "1Gi"
    cpu: "1000m"
  limits:
    memory: "2Gi"
    cpu: "2000m"
```

JVM:

```bash
java \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=30.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0 \
  -Xss768k \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

Catatan: `-Xss768k` harus diuji. Kalau ada stack dalam, parser kompleks, recursive code, atau framework call chain berat, jangan turunkan stack tanpa evidence.

---

### Profile C - File / Document / Media Processing Service

Karakter:

```text
large file upload/download
PDF/Excel/Word generation
byte[] besar
direct buffer
native libraries
temporary files
```

Kubernetes:

```yaml
resources:
  requests:
    memory: "2Gi"
    cpu: "1000m"
  limits:
    memory: "4Gi"
    cpu: "2000m"
```

JVM:

```bash
java \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=20.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=55.0 \
  -XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

Kenapa heap percentage lebih rendah? Karena service jenis ini sering butuh non-heap/native/temp headroom lebih besar.

---

### Profile D - Memory-Heavy Batch / Migration Job

Karakter:

```text
large query result
batch processing
migration/seeding
report generation
tidak selalu latency-sensitive
```

Kubernetes:

```yaml
resources:
  requests:
    memory: "4Gi"
    cpu: "2000m"
  limits:
    memory: "6Gi"
    cpu: "4000m"
```

JVM:

```bash
java \
  -Xms3g \
  -Xmx4g \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps \
  -jar /app/job.jar
```

Untuk batch job, fixed heap sering lebih mudah diaudit karena workload lebih terkontrol.

---

## `Xms` Strategy

| Strategy | Contoh | Kapan cocok | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| Small initial heap | `-XX:InitialRAMPercentage=10` | Service idle/spiky, ingin RSS awal kecil | GC/resizing awal lebih sering |
| Medium initial heap | `-XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25` | Default praktis untuk microservice | Startup RSS sedang |
| `Xms = Xmx` | `-Xms2g -Xmx2g` | Batch, latency predictable, dedicated memory | RSS tinggi sejak awal |
| No explicit `Xms` | hanya `-Xmx` / `MaxRAMPercentage` | Simple service | Initial heap ergonomics bisa beda antar env |

Oracle mendokumentasikan `-Xms` sebagai minimum sekaligus initial heap size, sedangkan `-Xmx` sebagai maximum heap size. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/specs/man/java.html))

### Rekomendasi praktis

Untuk Kubernetes microservice:

```bash
-XX:InitialRAMPercentage=20.0
-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
```

atau:

```bash
-XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0
```

Untuk batch/worker stabil:

```bash
-Xms<size>
-Xmx<size>
```

---

## Request vs Limit: Senior-Level Guidance

### Pattern umum

```yaml
resources:
  requests:
    memory: "1Gi"
    cpu: "1000m"
  limits:
    memory: "2Gi"
    cpu: "2000m"
```

Interpretasi:

| Field | Makna praktis |
|---|---|
| `requests.memory` | Memory yang digunakan scheduler untuk penempatan pod |
| `limits.memory` | Batas maksimal yang ditegakkan kernel/cgroup |
| `requests.cpu` | Bobot scheduling CPU |
| `limits.cpu` | Batas CPU runtime; bisa menyebabkan throttling |
| JVM heap | Sebaiknya dihitung dari limit, bukan request |

Kubernetes menyatakan memory request terutama digunakan untuk scheduling, sedangkan memory limit menjadi batas cgroup; jika container melewati limit, Linux OOM subsystem dapat menghentikan process. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/manage-resources-containers/))

---

## Heap Sizing by Workload Type

| Workload | Heap ratio awal | Native headroom | Alasan |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Simple REST API | 60-70% | 30-40% | Object mostly heap, native relatif kecil |
| REST + MyBatis + Camunda | 55-65% | 35-45% | Worker/thread/framework overhead lebih besar |
| File upload/download | 45-60% | 40-55% | Byte buffer/temp/direct memory |
| Media/OCR/native processing | 35-55% | 45-65% | Native library memory risk |
| High thread worker | 45-60% | 40-55% | Thread stack besar |
| APM/security-agent heavy | 50-60% | 40-50% | Agent overhead |
| Batch job | 65-80% | 20-35% | Bisa fixed heap jika native kecil |
| Low latency service | 55-70% | 30-45% | Butuh GC headroom dan stable live set |

---

## Heap Sizing Decision Tree

```text
Start
│
├─ Is this running in Kubernetes/Docker?
│  ├─ No  → use host memory budget; still reserve native headroom
│  └─ Yes
│
├─ Is memory limit set?
│  ├─ No  → set memory limit first; otherwise JVM may size unexpectedly
│  └─ Yes
│
├─ Is workload simple REST with low native/direct/thread usage?
│  ├─ Yes → start MaxRAMPercentage 60-70%
│  └─ No
│
├─ Does service use many threads / Camunda workers / schedulers?
│  ├─ Yes → start 50-65%, count thread stack budget
│  └─ No
│
├─ Does service process large files/media/direct buffers/native libs?
│  ├─ Yes → start 40-60%, enable NMT in non-prod
│  └─ No
│
├─ Is it batch/offline and memory profile predictable?
│  ├─ Yes → consider fixed -Xms/-Xmx
│  └─ No → use percentage-based sizing
│
└─ Load test and adjust using:
   ├─ heap after GC
   ├─ RSS vs limit
   ├─ GC pause p95/p99
   ├─ allocation rate
   ├─ thread count
   └─ pod restart/OOM evidence
```

---

## Runtime Verification

### 1. Check JVM effective flags

```bash
jcmd <pid> VM.flags
jcmd <pid> VM.command_line
```

Cari:

```text
MaxHeapSize
InitialHeapSize
MaxRAMPercentage
InitialRAMPercentage
UseContainerSupport
ThreadStackSize
```

---

### 2. Check heap info

```bash
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_info
```

---

### 3. Check container detection

```bash
java -Xlog:os+container=trace -version
```

Atau di pod:

```bash
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- java -Xlog:os+container=trace -version
```

Oracle mendokumentasikan `-Xlog:os+container=trace` untuk diagnostic container support. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/specs/man/java.html))

---

### 4. Check pod memory

```bash
kubectl top pod <pod>
kubectl describe pod <pod>
```

Cari:

```text
Last State: Terminated
Reason: OOMKilled
Exit Code: 137
Restart Count
```

---

### 5. Check RSS and threads inside container

```bash
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- cat /proc/1/status | egrep 'VmRSS|VmSize|Threads'
```

---

### 6. Check native memory if NMT enabled

Enable saat startup:

```bash
-XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary
```

Inspect:

```bash
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory summary scale=MB
```

NMT dapat membuat summary/detail report, baseline, dan diff via `jcmd`, tetapi harus diaktifkan saat JVM start; Oracle juga mencatat overhead NMT sekitar 5-10%. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/vm/native-memory-tracking.html))

---

## Example Calculation

### Case 1 - 1Gi REST API

Input:

```text
container limit = 1024 MiB
expected threads = 100
Xss = 1 MiB
metaspace estimate = 100 MiB
code cache = 64 MiB
direct/native = 96 MiB
GC/JIT/internal = 96 MiB
safety margin = 128 MiB
```

Non-heap/native:

```text
thread stacks    100 MiB
metaspace        100 MiB
code cache        64 MiB
direct/native     96 MiB
GC/JIT/internal   96 MiB
safety margin    128 MiB
------------------------
total            584 MiB
```

Safe heap:

```text
1024 - 584 = 440 MiB
```

Kesimpulan: 1Gi terlalu kecil jika thread benar-benar 100 dan native estimate realistis. Opsi:

```text
1. Naikkan pod limit ke 1536Mi / 2Gi.
2. Turunkan thread count.
3. Turunkan Xss setelah testing.
4. Kurangi direct/native buffer.
5. Set heap sekitar 400-500Mi, bukan 700-800Mi.
```

---

### Case 2 - 2Gi API + Worker

Input:

```text
container limit = 2048 MiB
non-heap/native budget = 650 MiB
safety margin included
```

Safe heap:

```text
2048 - 650 = 1398 MiB
```

Recommended:

```bash
-Xmx1280m
```

atau:

```bash
-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=62.5
```

Karena:

```text
2048 MiB × 62.5% = 1280 MiB
```

---

### Case 3 - 4Gi File Processing

Input:

```text
container limit = 4096 MiB
native/direct/temp risk high
reserve non-heap/native = 1800 MiB
```

Safe heap:

```text
4096 - 1800 = 2296 MiB
```

Recommended:

```bash
-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=55.0
```

Karena:

```text
4096 MiB × 55% ≈ 2253 MiB
```

---

## Production Templates

### Template 1 - Conservative Microservice

```bash
JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS="
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=20.0
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags

"
```

```bash
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0

  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags

"
```

## Kubernetes Deployment Example

```yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-service
spec:
  template:
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-service
          image: company/order-service:1.0.0
          resources:
            requests:
              memory: "1Gi"
              cpu: "1000m"
            limits:
              memory: "2Gi"
              cpu: "2000m"
          env:
            - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
              value: >-
                -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
                -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0
                -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
                -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
                -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
                -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
                -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
                -XX:ErrorFile=/dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log
          volumeMounts:
            - name: dumps
              mountPath: /dumps
      volumes:
        - name: dumps
          emptyDir: {}
```

Catatan penting: kalau `emptyDir` memakai memory-backed volume (`medium: Memory`), isinya dihitung sebagai memory usage container/pod; jangan pakai memory-backed dump path untuk heap dump besar. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/manage-resources-containers/))

---

## Sizing Validation Checklist

| Check | Command / Evidence | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| JVM membaca container limit | `java -Xlog:os+container=trace -version` | cgroup memory terdeteksi |
| Effective heap benar | `jcmd 1 VM.flags` | `MaxHeapSize` sesuai target |
| Heap masih punya headroom | GC log / JFR / heap metrics | after-GC heap jauh dari max |
| RSS aman | `kubectl top pod` | stabil di bawah 75-85% limit |
| Tidak OOMKilled | `kubectl describe pod` | no recent OOMKilled |
| Thread count sesuai budget | `/proc/1/status`, `jcmd Thread.print` | thread count bounded |
| Direct/native tidak naik liar | NMT / BufferPool MBean | stabil |
| GC pause acceptable | GC log / metrics | p95/p99 sesuai SLO |
| Heap dump path writable | test write / volume | dump tidak gagal |
| Config documented | repo/ADR/runbook | owner dan baseline jelas |

---

## Failure Mode Map

| Symptom | Kemungkinan sizing issue | Evidence | Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| Pod `OOMKilled`, no Java OOM | Total RSS > memory limit | `kubectl describe pod` | Turunkan heap / naikkan limit / kurangi native/thread |
| `Java heap space` | Heap terlalu kecil atau leak | heap dump, GC log | Fix retention/leak atau naikkan heap |
| GC terlalu sering | Heap terlalu kecil atau allocation rate tinggi | GC log frequency, JFR allocation | Naikkan heap atau kurangi allocation |
| p99 naik setelah heap dinaikkan | Heap lebih besar mengubah GC behavior/working set | latency + GC log | Review GC pause, live set |
| RSS tinggi, heap normal | Native/direct/thread/metaspace | NMT, BufferPool, thread count | Investigasi non-heap |
| `unable to create native thread` | Thread count / stack / native budget | thread count, NMT Thread | Kurangi thread, review `-Xss` |
| Heap dump gagal | Dump path tidak writable / disk penuh | app log, filesystem | Mount volume dan quota |
| Local aman, pod crash | Container limit lebih kecil dari asumsi JVM | `jcmd VM.flags`, K8s limit | Align runtime sizing |

---

## Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa salah | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| `-Xmx` sama dengan pod limit | Tidak ada ruang native/non-heap | Sisakan 30-50% tergantung workload |
| Pakai `MaxRAMPercentage=80` untuk semua service | Workload berbeda butuh native headroom berbeda | Profile per workload |
| Mengandalkan default heap 25% tanpa cek | Bisa terlalu kecil untuk service tertentu | Validasi dengan load test |
| Tidak set memory limit | JVM/container behavior sulit diprediksi | Set request dan limit eksplisit |
| Tidak lihat RSS | JVM heap metrics tidak cukup | Monitor container memory |
| Heap dump ke tmpfs | Heap dump memakan memory limit | Pakai disk-backed volume |
| Menurunkan `Xss` tanpa test | Bisa StackOverflow | Test call path terburuk |
| Naikkan heap saat OOMKilled | Bisa makin cepat OOMKilled | Cek apakah Java OOM atau kernel OOM |
| Copy setting dari service lain | Memory profile beda | Ukur sendiri |
| Tidak punya GC log | Tidak ada evidence sizing | Aktifkan `-Xlog:gc*` baseline |

---

## Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Step | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Bedakan heap vs container memory | Bisa menjelaskan kenapa `Xmx != limit` |
| 2 | Pahami request vs limit | Bisa membaca K8s memory contract |
| 3 | Fixed heap vs percentage heap | Bisa memilih `-Xmx` atau `MaxRAMPercentage` |
| 4 | Native headroom budgeting | Bisa menghitung sisa non-heap |
| 5 | Thread/direct/metaspace impact | Bisa mengenali memory di luar heap |
| 6 | Runtime verification | Bisa membuktikan effective heap di pod |
| 7 | Load test sizing | Bisa menilai heap/RSS/GC/p99 bersama |
| 8 | Runbook production | Bisa membuat template sizing per service type |

---

## Mini Practice

Untuk satu service Java di Kubernetes, isi ini:

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Pod memory request |  |
| Pod memory limit |  |
| Current RSS |  |
| `MaxHeapSize` dari `jcmd VM.flags` |  |
| Heap used after GC |  |
| Thread count |  |
| `-Xss` / ThreadStackSize |  |
| Metaspace used |  |
| Direct buffer estimate |  |
| GC pause p95/p99 |  |
| Last OOMKilled? |  |
| Native headroom estimate |  |

Commands:

```bash
kubectl describe pod <pod>
kubectl top pod <pod>
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.flags
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 GC.heap_info
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- cat /proc/1/status | egrep 'VmRSS|VmSize|Threads'
```

Jika NMT aktif:

```bash
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.native_memory summary scale=MB
```
