# Seri 6/12 - GC Fundamentals: Allocation, Pause, Throughput, Latency, Live Set, Promotion, Full GC

## Introduction

Seri ini membangun **mental model dasar Garbage Collection (GC)** sebelum masuk ke tuning spesifik seperti G1/ZGC.

GC bukan sekadar “pembersih memory”. Di production Java microservice, GC adalah mekanisme runtime yang menentukan:

```text id="mry9rx"
berapa cepat object bisa dialokasikan
berapa lama request thread berhenti
berapa besar heap yang dibutuhkan
berapa banyak CPU dipakai GC
apakah latency p95/p99 naik
apakah service OOM karena leak, pressure, atau sizing buruk
```

Oracle GC tuning guide menekankan bahwa pemilihan dan tuning GC selalu terkait trade-off antara **throughput**, **latency/responsiveness**, dan **footprint**. Untuk Java server modern, G1 adalah default collector pada banyak konfigurasi, dan G1 mencoba mencapai pause-time goal dengan pendekatan regional/incremental. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/gctuning/introduction-garbage-collection-tuning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Diagram

```text id="h27q9w"
GC Fundamentals for Java Microservice
│
├─ 1. Allocation
│  ├─ object created by application
│  ├─ DTO / JSON / List / Map / String / byte[]
│  ├─ allocation rate
│  └─ temporary object pressure
│
├─ 2. Object Lifetime
│  ├─ short-lived objects
│  ├─ medium-lived objects
│  ├─ long-lived objects
│  └─ accidentally retained objects
│
├─ 3. Reachability
│  ├─ GC roots
│  ├─ live objects
│  ├─ unreachable objects
│  └─ reclaimable memory
│
├─ 4. Heap Pressure
│  ├─ live set too large
│  ├─ allocation rate too high
│  ├─ heap too small
│  ├─ promotion pressure
│  └─ fragmentation / humongous objects
│
├─ 5. Collection Work
│  ├─ young / minor collection
│  ├─ old / major collection
│  ├─ mixed collection
│  ├─ concurrent marking
│  ├─ evacuation / compaction
│  └─ full GC
│
├─ 6. Runtime Impact
│  ├─ stop-the-world pause
│  ├─ application throughput loss
│  ├─ CPU consumed by GC
│  ├─ tail latency spike
│  └─ allocation stall
│
├─ 7. Metrics & Evidence
│  ├─ GC logs: -Xlog:gc*
│  ├─ JFR GC events
│  ├─ heap usage before/after GC
│  ├─ allocation rate
│  ├─ pause duration
│  ├─ GC frequency
│  └─ heap dump / histogram
│
└─ 8. Diagnosis
   ├─ leak?
   ├─ allocation pressure?
   ├─ heap too small?
   ├─ live set too large?
   ├─ collector mismatch?
   ├─ CPU throttling?
   └─ bad application pattern?
```

---

## Core Mental Model

```text id="8uxkyd"
Application allocates objects
        ↓
Heap usage rises
        ↓
GC identifies reachable objects
        ↓
Unreachable objects are reclaimed
        ↓
Live objects stay
        ↓
If live set + allocation pressure > heap capacity
        ↓
GC becomes frequent / expensive / ineffective
        ↓
Latency, throughput, and stability degrade
```

GC tuning dimulai dari pertanyaan:

```text id="qqlylc"
Apakah masalahnya:
1. heap terlalu kecil?
2. allocation rate terlalu tinggi?
3. live set terlalu besar?
4. object leak / retention?
5. collector tidak cocok?
6. CPU kurang / throttled?
7. workload aplikasi memang membuat object terlalu besar?
```

---

## Tabular Decomposition Map

| # | Category / Skill | Penjelasan praktis | Relevansi untuk Java/Jersey microservice | Evidence | Failure mode |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Garbage Collection Purpose** | GC mereklaim object yang tidak lagi reachable sehingga developer tidak perlu free memory manual. | Semua object DTO, JSON, MyBatis result, Camunda variable, collection, exception. | GC log, JFR, heap usage. | Mengira GC bisa menyelesaikan object yang masih tertahan reference. |
| 2 | **GC Root** | Titik awal reachability: thread stack, static fields, JNI refs, class metadata, dan runtime refs. | Static cache, ThreadLocal, request context, framework registry. | Heap dump dominator tree. | Object tidak bisa direclaim karena masih reachable dari root. |
| 3 | **Reachable Object** | Object masih dianggap hidup jika dapat dijangkau dari GC roots. | Cache, queue, pending jobs, maps, sessions. | Heap dump retained size. | Logical memory leak. |
| 4 | **Unreachable Object** | Object tidak lagi dapat dijangkau dan dapat direclaim GC. | Temporary object dari request/response. | GC before/after heap usage. | Allocation tinggi tetapi object cepat mati; bukan leak, tetapi pressure. |
| 5 | **Allocation Rate** | Kecepatan aplikasi membuat object baru. | JSON serialization, MyBatis mapping, validation, logging, DTO conversion. | JFR allocation profile, GC frequency. | GC sering walau tidak ada leak. |
| 6 | **Live Set** | Total object yang tetap hidup setelah GC. | Cache, loaded config, workflow state, connection pools, active tasks. | Heap after full/mixed GC. | Live set mendekati `Xmx`, GC tidak bisa reclaim cukup. |
| 7 | **Heap Occupancy** | Seberapa penuh heap sebelum/sesudah collection. | Indikator pressure dan headroom. | GC log `before/after`, metrics. | Heap selalu tinggi setelah GC. |
| 8 | **Young Object** | Object baru dan biasanya short-lived. | Request DTO, temporary String, JSON node, mapper intermediate. | Young GC logs, JFR allocation. | Young GC terlalu sering. |
| 9 | **Old Object** | Object yang bertahan lebih lama dan masuk old generation/old regions. | Cache, global registry, long-running workflow data. | Old occupancy trend. | Old space naik terus. |
| 10 | **Promotion** | Object yang bertahan dari young collection dipindah/dianggap mature. | Object request yang tertahan queue/cache/threadlocal. | GC log promotion/old growth. | Promotion pressure, old gen cepat penuh. |
| 11 | **Minor / Young GC** | Collection untuk object muda. Biasanya lebih sering dan lebih pendek. | REST workload dengan banyak temporary object. | GC log young pause. | Banyak pause kecil yang mengganggu p99. |
| 12 | **Major / Old GC** | Collection yang melibatkan object lama. Tergantung collector. | Long-running services. | GC log old/mixed/full events. | Pause lebih mahal, old space tidak turun. |
| 13 | **Mixed GC** | Pada G1, collection dapat mencakup young regions dan sebagian old regions. | Default server Java modern dengan G1. | G1 GC log. | Mixed GC tidak mengejar old growth. |
| 14 | **Full GC** | Collection menyeluruh, sering stop-the-world dan mahal. | Biasanya sinyal kondisi buruk untuk service latency-sensitive. | GC log `Full GC`. | Latency spike besar, throughput drop. |
| 15 | **Stop-The-World Pause** | Aplikasi thread dihentikan sementara untuk pekerjaan GC tertentu. | Request latency p95/p99/p999. | GC pause duration, JFR. | User-facing latency spike. |
| 16 | **Throughput** | Persentase waktu aplikasi bekerja dibanding waktu total. GC time mengurangi throughput. | API throughput, worker job throughput. | GC time ratio. | CPU banyak habis di GC. |
| 17 | **Latency** | Waktu respons request/job. GC pause bisa menaikkan tail latency. | p95/p99 endpoint, Camunda job delay. | APM + GC log correlation. | p99 spike saat GC pause. |
| 18 | **Footprint** | Total memory yang dibutuhkan aplikasi/JVM. | Pod limit, RSS, heap/non-heap. | container memory + JVM memory metrics. | Heap terlalu besar untuk container. |
| 19 | **Pause-Time Goal** | Target pause GC, bukan hard guarantee. Pada G1, `MaxGCPauseMillis` dipakai sebagai soft goal. Oracle menyebut G1 mencoba memenuhi pause-time goals dan tuning throughput/latency dilakukan dengan mengubah target pause atau heap. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/gctuning/garbage-first-garbage-collector-tuning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Tuning p99 latency. | `-XX:MaxGCPauseMillis`. | Mengira pause goal adalah SLA pasti. |
| 20 | **GC Frequency** | Seberapa sering GC terjadi. | Indikator allocation pressure dan heap headroom. | GC log count/time. | Terlalu sering → CPU overhead tinggi. |
| 21 | **GC Duration** | Lama setiap event GC. | Menentukan impact ke latency. | GC log pause ms, JFR. | Pause pendek tapi sangat sering, atau pause panjang jarang. |
| 22 | **GC CPU Cost** | GC memakai CPU untuk marking, copying, compacting, barriers, concurrent work. | Kubernetes CPU limit/throttling. | CPU metrics + GC logs. | GC lambat karena CPU kurang. |
| 23 | **Allocation Stall** | Thread aplikasi tertahan karena tidak bisa allocate cepat. | High traffic API, heap hampir penuh. | JFR allocation stall, GC log. | Request latency spike tanpa obvious DB issue. |
| 24 | **Humongous / Large Object** | Object sangat besar dapat memengaruhi collector seperti G1. | Large JSON, byte array, PDF/Excel, file processing. | GC log humongous, heap histogram. | Fragmentation, full GC, pause spike. |
| 25 | **Memory Leak** | Object tidak lagi diperlukan tetapi masih reachable. | Static map, ThreadLocal, unbounded cache, listener registry. | Heap dump diff. | Heap after-GC naik terus. |
| 26 | **Memory Pressure** | Memory sulit mencukupi karena allocation/live set/workload besar, belum tentu leak. | Burst request, batch query besar. | JFR allocation, GC log. | Salah diagnosis sebagai leak. |
| 27 | **GC Overhead Limit** | JVM dapat melempar OOM jika terlalu banyak waktu dipakai GC dengan hasil reclaim kecil. | Heap terlalu penuh / leak. | Java OOM log. | Service habis waktu di GC. |
| 28 | **Explicit GC** | `System.gc()` meminta GC eksplisit. Bisa menyebabkan pause, tergantung config/JVM. | Library/framework lama, manual call. | GC log cause `System.gc()`. | Unexpected full GC. |
| 29 | **GC Logging** | Bukti utama GC. JDK modern memakai unified logging `-Xlog`. JDK 26 docs memetakan legacy GC logging flags ke `-Xlog`, dan OpenJDK JEP 271 mendesain unified GC logging di atas unified JVM logging. ([openjdk.org](https://openjdk.org/jeps/271?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Semua incident memory/latency. | `-Xlog:gc*`. | Tidak ada data saat incident. |
| 30 | **JFR GC Events** | Java Flight Recorder memberi event GC, allocation, safepoint, lock, thread, socket/file. | Correlate GC dengan latency/CPU/allocation. | JFR recording. | Hanya lihat GC log tanpa tahu allocation source. |
| 31 | **Heap Dump** | Snapshot object graph untuk leak/retention. | Leak analysis. | `.hprof`, MAT/JMC. | Dump besar dan mengandung data sensitif. |
| 32 | **Class Histogram** | Ringkasan jumlah/size object per class. | Quick triage tanpa full heap dump. | `jcmd GC.class_histogram`. | Salah menyimpulkan tanpa retained-size analysis. |
| 33 | **GC Tuning Discipline** | Ubah satu variabel, ukur, bandingkan. | Production tuning. | before/after GC log, p99, CPU, RSS. | Copy-paste flag tanpa evidence. |
| 34 | **Application-Level Fix** | Banyak masalah GC lebih baik diselesaikan di kode/query/pool/cache daripada flag. | MyBatis query besar, JSON buffer, cache. | allocation profile + heap dump. | Tuning JVM menutupi bug desain. |

---

## GC Concepts yang Harus Hafal

### 1. Allocation

Allocation adalah saat aplikasi membuat object baru:

```java id="lbadn3"
OrderResponse response = new OrderResponse();
List<OrderItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
String payload = objectMapper.writeValueAsString(response);
```

Di microservice Java/Jersey, allocation tinggi biasanya berasal dari:

```text id="3gmful"
JSON serialization/deserialization
XML processing
DTO/entity mapping
MyBatis result mapping
validation error object
logging string/context
exception stack trace
large List/Map
byte[] untuk file/PDF/Excel
Camunda variable serialization
```

Yang perlu diingat:

```text id="3162po"
High allocation rate can cause GC pressure even without memory leak.
```

---

### 2. Object Lifetime

Object lifetime biasanya terbagi:

| Lifetime | Contoh | GC impact |
|---|---|---|
| Short-lived | request DTO, temporary String, JSON parser buffer | Biasanya mudah direclaim di young GC |
| Medium-lived | request context, pending async task, queue item | Bisa survive beberapa GC |
| Long-lived | cache, config, singleton, pool, workflow engine state | Masuk old space/old regions |
| Accidentally retained | ThreadLocal tidak dibersihkan, unbounded map | Leak/retention |

---

### 3. Reachability

GC tidak tahu object “masih dibutuhkan secara bisnis” atau tidak. GC hanya tahu:

```text id="8p40cx"
reachable   → keep
unreachable → reclaim
```

Contoh leak logical:

```java id="b8ggxe"
private static final Map<String, OrderResponse> CACHE = new HashMap<>();

public void handle(OrderResponse response) {
    CACHE.put(response.id(), response);
}
```

Kalau tidak ada eviction, semua `OrderResponse` tetap reachable dari static field `CACHE`.

---

### 4. Live Set

Live set adalah object yang tetap hidup setelah GC.

```text id="nvkt4t"
Heap before GC: 900 MiB
Heap after GC:  700 MiB
Reclaimed:      200 MiB
Live set:       ~700 MiB
```

Kalau `Xmx = 768 MiB`, maka live set 700 MiB berbahaya. GC hanya punya sedikit ruang untuk allocation berikutnya.

---

### 5. Allocation Rate vs Live Set

Dua service bisa sama-sama “memory tinggi” tetapi root cause beda.

| Pattern | Heap after GC | Allocation rate | Diagnosis |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Banyak temporary object | rendah/stabil | tinggi | Allocation pressure |
| Cache/leak tumbuh | naik terus | sedang/tinggi | Retention/leak |
| Heap terlalu kecil | after-GC sehat tapi cepat penuh | tinggi | Sizing issue |
| Query/result besar | spike besar | bursty | Workload/object-size issue |

---

## GC Metrics yang Penting

| Metric | Arti | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|---|
| Heap before GC | Heap terpakai sebelum collection | Menunjukkan trigger pressure |
| Heap after GC | Heap tersisa setelah collection | Mengestimasi live set |
| Reclaimed bytes | Before - after | Efektivitas GC |
| Pause duration | Lama stop-the-world | Impact ke latency |
| GC frequency | Seberapa sering GC | Pressure dan overhead |
| GC time ratio | Waktu GC / waktu total | Throughput loss |
| Allocation rate | Object baru per detik | Penyebab pressure |
| Promotion rate | Object masuk old | Risiko old space penuh |
| Old occupancy | Pemakaian old gen/regions | Leak/retention |
| Full GC count | Jumlah full GC | Sinyal bahaya |
| Humongous allocation | Object besar | G1/fragmentation risk |
| CPU usage GC | CPU untuk GC | Sensitif di K8s CPU limit |
| RSS vs heap | Total process vs heap | Native/off-heap risk |

---

## GC Log Baseline

Untuk Java 17+ modern, gunakan unified logging:

```bash id="0adpt7"
-Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
```

Untuk file dengan rotasi:

```bash id="0o6gwo"
-Xlog:gc*:file=/var/log/app/gc.log:time,uptime,level,tags:filecount=5,filesize=20M
```

Untuk investigasi lebih detail:

```bash id="2zbcgx"
-Xlog:gc*,safepoint:file=/var/log/app/jvm.log:time,uptime,level,tags:filecount=5,filesize=50M
```

`-Xlog` adalah framework logging JVM modern dengan tag/level/decorator; JDK 26 docs juga menyatakan beberapa legacy runtime logging flags sudah tidak dikenali dan harus memakai equivalent unified logging. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/26/docs/specs/man/java.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Cara Membaca GC Log secara Praktis

Bukan hafal semua format dulu. Mulai dari pertanyaan ini:

```text id="8wlzl5"
1. GC terjadi seberapa sering?
2. Pause-nya berapa ms?
3. Heap turun banyak atau sedikit setelah GC?
4. Old usage naik terus atau stabil?
5. Ada Full GC?
6. Ada humongous allocation?
7. Ada allocation stall?
8. Ada correlation dengan p99 latency?
```

### Contoh interpretasi sederhana

```text id="5vplzt"
GC pause: 30ms
Heap before: 900M
Heap after: 300M
```

Interpretasi:

```text id="3cd491"
GC efektif.
Banyak garbage berhasil direclaim.
Kemungkinan allocation pressure, belum tentu leak.
```

---

```text id="0md6vh"
GC pause: 150ms
Heap before: 950M
Heap after: 900M
```

Interpretasi:

```text id="b4kac4"
GC kurang efektif.
Live set sangat besar atau leak/retention.
Heap hampir penuh setelah GC.
```

---

```text id="xqcsjf"
Young GC every 200ms
Pause 5-20ms each
```

Interpretasi:

```text id="p1ba8d"
Pause individual kecil, tetapi frekuensi tinggi.
GC overhead bisa besar dan p99 bisa terganggu.
Cek allocation rate.
```

---

```text id="fv287x"
Full GC occurred
Pause 2.5s
```

Interpretasi:

```text id="kjy4g9"
Sinyal serius untuk service latency-sensitive.
Cek old occupancy, live set, humongous object, heap pressure, CPU, dan collector behavior.
```

---

## GC Failure Mode Map

| Symptom | Kemungkinan root cause | Evidence | Action direction |
|---|---|---|---|
| Young GC sangat sering | Allocation rate tinggi / heap young kecil | GC log, JFR allocation | Kurangi allocation, review heap sizing |
| Heap after GC naik terus | Leak / retention / cache growth | heap dump diff | Cari dominator, bounded cache |
| Full GC muncul | Old pressure, fragmentation, allocation failure | GC log cause | Cek live set, large object, sizing |
| p99 spike bersamaan GC | STW pause impact | APM + GC log timestamp | Tune GC/heap, reduce allocation |
| CPU tinggi di GC | Heap pressure atau allocation tinggi | CPU + GC time ratio | Kurangi allocation / tambah heap / CPU |
| Pod OOMKilled tanpa Java OOM | RSS melewati limit, bukan heap OOM | K8s event | Review heap/native budget |
| `GC overhead limit exceeded` | GC banyak tapi reclaim sedikit | Java OOM + GC log | Heap dump, leak/retention |
| Heap normal tapi RSS tinggi | Native/direct/thread/metaspace | NMT, BufferPool, thread count | Debug non-heap |
| Latency tinggi setelah scale down CPU | GC concurrent/STW work lambat | CPU throttling + GC log | Review CPU limit/request |
| Large request bikin pause | Object besar / humongous allocation | heap histogram, GC log | Limit payload, streaming |

---

## GC dan Application Pattern

### 1. JSON-heavy REST API

Pattern:

```text id="u4xtu2"
request body → byte[] / stream
JSON parser → intermediate object
DTO → validation
domain object → response DTO
response JSON → String/byte[]
```

Risiko:

```text id="giq05b"
high allocation rate
large byte[]
temporary object burst
young GC frequency tinggi
```

Mitigasi:

```text id="8ugmv0"
request size limit
streaming parser untuk payload besar
hindari materialisasi object tidak perlu
reuse immutable config, bukan mutable shared buffer
ukur dengan JFR allocation
```

---

### 2. MyBatis large result

Pattern:

```java id="bpt0fk"
List<Order> orders = orderMapper.findAllOrders();
```

Risiko:

```text id="106e2j"
large List
banyak entity/DTO hidup bersamaan
heap spike
old promotion
OOM heap
```

Mitigasi:

```text id="3sfoqi"
pagination
cursor/streaming jika cocok
query limit
projection kolom secukupnya
batch processing
```

---

### 3. Camunda variables

Pattern:

```text id="il36lb"
large object variable
serialized workflow payload
history variable
job retry holds context
```

Risiko:

```text id="zwsx6i"
heap pressure
DB pressure
serialization overhead
old retention
```

Mitigasi:

```text id="z5z7dp"
simpan reference/id, bukan payload besar
external object storage untuk file/blob
batasi variable size
audit history level
```

---

### 4. Unbounded cache

Pattern:

```java id="q5gnrm"
Map<String, Result> cache = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
```

Risiko:

```text id="h8sq46"
old gen naik terus
GC makin tidak efektif
heap after-GC naik
```

Mitigasi:

```text id="ui0blc"
bounded cache
TTL/TTI
max size/weight
eviction metrics
cache hit-rate monitoring
```

---

### 5. Excessive exception allocation

Pattern:

```java id="xn142g"
try {
    parse(value);
} catch (Exception e) {
    return fallback;
}
```

Risiko:

```text id="9wgszt"
exception stack trace allocation mahal
CPU + allocation naik
GC pressure
```

Mitigasi:

```text id="svz8tk"
validasi input sebelum parse
gunakan result object untuk expected failure
jangan pakai exception sebagai normal control flow
monitor JFR exception events
```

---

## Throughput vs Latency vs Footprint

GC tuning selalu trade-off:

| Goal | Optimisasi | Trade-off |
|---|---|---|
| Higher throughput | Kurangi waktu GC total, heap lebih besar, pause target lebih longgar | Pause bisa lebih panjang |
| Lower latency | Pause lebih pendek, collector low-latency, target pause lebih ketat | CPU/heap overhead bisa naik |
| Lower footprint | Heap lebih kecil | GC lebih sering dan CPU naik |
| Faster startup | Initial heap kecil, CDS, lazy init | First-request latency bisa naik |
| Stable p99 | Headroom cukup, allocation rendah, GC logs/JFR | Memory/CPU cost lebih besar |

Oracle GC tuning guide menempatkan throughput, responsiveness/latency, dan footprint sebagai faktor yang perlu dipertimbangkan saat memilih dan men-tune collector. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/gctuning/introduction-garbage-collection-tuning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Pause-Time Goal: Jangan Salah Paham

Contoh flag:

```bash id="f5i113"
-XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=200
```

Makna praktis:

```text id="x7me68"
“JVM/collector akan mencoba mencapai target pause ini.”
```

Bukan:

```text id="q0aez4"
“GC pause pasti tidak akan pernah lebih dari 200ms.”
```

Untuk G1, Oracle menjelaskan bahwa G1 mencoba memenuhi pause-time goal; jika throughput lebih penting, pause-time goal bisa dibuat lebih longgar atau heap diperbesar. Oracle juga memperingatkan agar tidak mengunci young generation dengan opsi seperti `-Xmn` karena G1 memakai ukuran young generation sebagai salah satu alat untuk memenuhi pause goal. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/gctuning/garbage-first-garbage-collector-tuning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## GC Evidence Collection Template

### JVM args baseline

```bash id="xmovor"
-Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
-XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
-XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
```

### Optional investigasi

```bash id="hcnmm3"
-XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary
-XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=/recordings/startup.jfr,duration=120s,settings=profile
```

### Runtime commands

```bash id="hka8e1"
jcmd <pid> VM.flags
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_info
jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram
jcmd <pid> Thread.print
```

### JFR on demand

```bash id="jjgpp2"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=gc-profile settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/gc-profile.jfr
```

---

## Diagnostic Decision Tree

```text id="2zt2cq"
GC problem suspected
│
├─ Is there GC log?
│  ├─ No → enable -Xlog:gc*
│  └─ Yes
│
├─ Are pauses correlated with p99 latency?
│  ├─ No → GC may not be root cause
│  └─ Yes
│
├─ Is GC frequent?
│  ├─ Yes → check allocation rate and heap size
│  └─ No
│
├─ Is each pause long?
│  ├─ Yes → check live set, collector, old GC/full GC
│  └─ No
│
├─ Does heap after GC keep rising?
│  ├─ Yes → suspect leak/retention
│  └─ No → suspect allocation pressure / traffic burst
│
├─ Is Full GC present?
│  ├─ Yes → serious old/fragmentation/allocation failure signal
│  └─ No
│
├─ Is RSS high but heap normal?
│  ├─ Yes → native/direct/thread/metaspace investigation
│  └─ No
│
└─ Change one thing, load test, compare
```

---

## Practical Commands

### 1. Aktifkan GC log

```bash id="y7g1e7"
java \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -jar app.jar
```

### 2. GC log ke file dengan rotasi

```bash id="4qoplf"
java \
  -Xlog:gc*:file=/var/log/app/gc.log:time,uptime,level,tags:filecount=5,filesize=20M \
  -jar app.jar
```

### 3. Cek heap saat runtime

```bash id="mi05dj"
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_info
```

### 4. Ambil histogram object

```bash id="jb8px7"
jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram
```

### 5. Trigger heap dump

```bash id="688te7"
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_dump /dumps/heap-$(date +%s).hprof
```

### 6. Cek flags GC aktif

```bash id="szyswp"
jcmd <pid> VM.flags
```

### 7. Cek command line aktual

```bash id="7atd44"
jcmd <pid> VM.command_line
```

## Interpreting Common GC Situations

### Situation A - Banyak Young GC, Heap After GC Rendah

```text id="arz7n9"
Young GC frequent
Pause 5-30ms
Heap after GC low/stable
Old usage stable
```

Diagnosis:

```text id="4o8quf"
Allocation pressure, bukan leak.
```

Action:

```text id="iljhop"
JFR allocation profiling
reduce temporary allocations
review JSON/mapper/logging
consider heap headroom
```

---

### Situation B - Heap After GC Naik Terus

```text id="13z348"
Heap after GC:
10:00 = 400 MiB
10:10 = 600 MiB
10:20 = 850 MiB
10:30 = 1100 MiB
```

Diagnosis:

```text id="bsq52k"
Leak atau retention.
```

Action:

```text id="j0vja8"
heap dump
dominator tree
compare dump over time
check cache/ThreadLocal/static map/queue
```

---

### Situation C - Full GC dan Reclaim Kecil

```text id="s9cbil"
Full GC pause 2s
Heap before 1900 MiB
Heap after 1850 MiB
Xmx 2048 MiB
```

Diagnosis:

```text id="o5mno7"
Live set terlalu besar atau leak.
Heap hampir habis.
```

Action:

```text id="g8vqh0"
heap dump immediately
reduce live set
fix retention
increase heap only as temporary mitigation
```

---

### Situation D - GC Pause Besar Saat Large Payload

```text id="4u96yk"
Large request received
byte[] / String large allocation
GC pause spike
```

Diagnosis:

```text id="gbt4ap"
Large object / humongous allocation / materialization issue.
```

Action:

```text id="ec2ysh"
limit payload size
streaming processing
avoid full in-memory buffering
chunk file processing
```

---

### Situation E - Pod OOMKilled, GC Log Tidak Ada OOM

```text id="k6d67j"
No Java OOM
Pod restart
Reason: OOMKilled
Exit code: 137
```

Diagnosis:

```text id="pymyp8"
Container memory exceeded.
Could be heap + native + thread + direct + metaspace.
```

Action:

```text id="3aai71"
review Xmx / MaxRAMPercentage
check RSS
enable NMT
check thread count/direct buffer/metaspace
```

---

## GC Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa salah | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Menambah `Xmx` setiap ada GC issue | Bisa menunda leak atau membuat pod OOMKilled | Tentukan leak vs pressure dulu |
| Mengubah collector tanpa GC log | Tidak tahu masalah sebenarnya | Kumpulkan GC log/JFR |
| Menargetkan pause terlalu rendah | Bisa menaikkan CPU/GC frequency | Sesuaikan dengan SLO realistis |
| Mengunci young gen untuk G1 | Mengganggu pause-time control G1 | Biarkan G1 mengatur young size kecuali ada alasan kuat |
| Fokus hanya average latency | GC impact sering terlihat di p99/p999 | Pantau tail latency |
| Hanya lihat heap used | RSS/native/direct/thread bisa jadi root cause | Gabungkan JVM + container metrics |
| Mengabaikan allocation rate | Banyak GC issue berasal dari object churn | JFR allocation profiling |
| Mengabaikan full GC | Full GC adalah sinyal serius untuk API latency-sensitive | Investigasi segera |
| Heap dump tanpa redaction/security | Heap dump bisa berisi PII/secret/token | Treat heap dump as sensitive artifact |
| Tuning banyak flag sekaligus | Tidak bisa atribusi efek | One change, measure, compare |

---

## Production Dashboard Minimum untuk GC

| Panel | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| GC pause p50/p95/p99 | Impact latency |
| GC count by type | Frequency young/mixed/full |
| GC time ratio | Throughput loss |
| Heap used before/after GC | Live set dan reclaim effectiveness |
| Old gen / old regions usage | Retention/leak signal |
| Allocation rate | Pressure source |
| Promotion rate | Old pressure |
| Full GC count | Severe signal |
| Container RSS | Whole process memory |
| CPU usage/throttling | GC speed dan app contention |
| Thread count | Native memory/thread pressure |
| Request p95/p99 | Correlate with GC |
| DB pool wait/active | Pisahkan GC issue dari DB blocking |

---

## Senior-Level GC Review Checklist

Gunakan checklist ini saat review service baru:

| Question | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| GC apa yang aktif? | Jangan asumsi default |
| GC log aktif? | Evidence saat incident |
| Heap max berapa? | Memory budget |
| Heap after-GC stabil? | Live set/leak signal |
| Allocation rate berapa? | Pressure source |
| Ada Full GC? | Severe signal |
| Ada humongous allocation? | Large object/file/payload issue |
| p99 latency correlate dengan GC? | Bukti user impact |
| RSS vs heap gap berapa? | Native/off-heap risk |
| Thread count bounded? | Native memory dan scheduling |
| Ada APM/security agent? | Memory/CPU overhead |
| Payload/query size bounded? | Large allocation prevention |
| Cache bounded? | Leak prevention |
| Heap dump path aman? | Forensic readiness |
| JFR allowed in prod/non-prod? | Runtime profiling |

---

## Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Step | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Allocation & reachability | Bisa membedakan garbage, live object, dan retained object |
| 2 | Live set vs allocation rate | Bisa membedakan leak dan pressure |
| 3 | Young/old/mixed/full GC | Bisa membaca jenis event GC |
| 4 | Pause/throughput/latency/footprint | Bisa memahami trade-off GC |
| 5 | GC log reading | Bisa membaca frequency, duration, before/after heap |
| 6 | JFR allocation profiling | Bisa menemukan sumber object churn |
| 7 | Heap dump basics | Bisa menemukan dominator/leak |
| 8 | Production correlation | Bisa menghubungkan GC dengan p99, CPU, RSS, OOM |

Ambil satu service Java dan kumpulkan 10-30 menit GC log under load:


Lalu jawab:

| Pertanyaan | Jawaban |

## Response:
7/14/2026, 12:26:06 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 7/12: G1 GC Tuning**. Fokusnya G1 sebagai default practical baseline: region, pause target, mixed GC, humongous object, full GC fallback, dan workflow tuning berbasis GC log.

# Seri 7/12 - G1 GC Tuning

## Introduction

Seri ini fokus pada **G1 GC**, collector yang paling sering menjadi baseline untuk Java 17+ server-side microservice.

G1 berarti **Garbage-First**. Ide dasarnya: heap dibagi menjadi banyak **region**, lalu G1 memilih region yang paling “menguntungkan” untuk dikumpulkan lebih dulu, terutama region yang punya banyak garbage. Oracle JDK 25 GC guide menjelaskan bahwa G1 membagi heap menjadi equal-sized heap regions dan melakukan collection secara incremental untuk memenuhi pause-time goal. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/gctuning/garbage-first-g1-garbage-collector1.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk Java/Jersey/JAX-RS microservice, G1 biasanya cocok sebagai default karena workload-nya sering seperti ini:

```text id="dfymw1"
REST request/response banyak object sementara
JSON/XML serialization/deserialization
MyBatis result mapping
Camunda job execution
cache/config object long-lived
thread pool dan DB pool long-running
latency p95/p99 penting
throughput tetap harus stabil
```

Namun G1 bukan magic. Kalau masalahnya adalah query terlalu besar, payload tidak dibatasi, cache tidak bounded, atau heap terlalu mepet dengan live set, tuning G1 hanya menunda masalah.

---

## Diagram

```text id="joo42h"
G1 GC Mental Model
│
├─ 1. Heap as Regions
│  ├─ eden regions
│  ├─ survivor regions
│  ├─ old regions
│  ├─ humongous regions
│  └─ free regions
│
├─ 2. Allocation Path
│  ├─ application allocates object
│  ├─ object goes to eden
│  ├─ young GC evacuates live objects
│  ├─ survivors stay young
│  ├─ long-lived objects become old
│  └─ large objects may become humongous
│
├─ 3. Concurrent Marking
│  ├─ starts when old occupancy threshold reached
│  ├─ identifies live objects in old regions
│  ├─ computes reclaimable space
│  └─ prepares mixed collections
│
├─ 4. Collection Types
│  ├─ young GC
│  ├─ concurrent marking cycle
│  ├─ mixed GC
│  ├─ remark / cleanup
│  └─ full GC fallback
│
├─ 5. Main Tuning Levers
│  ├─ heap size / headroom
│  ├─ -XX:MaxGCPauseMillis
│  ├─ -XX:InitiatingHeapOccupancyPercent
│  ├─ -XX:G1HeapRegionSize
│  ├─ -XX:ConcGCThreads
│  ├─ -XX:ParallelGCThreads
│  └─ avoid fixed young gen sizing
│
├─ 6. Evidence
│  ├─ -Xlog:gc*
│  ├─ -Xlog:gc+heap
│  ├─ -Xlog:gc+humongous
│  ├─ JFR GC events
│  ├─ allocation rate
│  ├─ old occupancy
│  ├─ pause duration
│  └─ full GC cause
│
└─ 7. Diagnosis
   ├─ allocation pressure?
   ├─ live set too large?
   ├─ marking starts too late?
   ├─ mixed GC not enough?
   ├─ humongous allocation?
   ├─ evacuation failure?
   ├─ CPU throttling?
   └─ application design issue?
```

---

## Core Mental Model

```text id="uieqf6"
G1 does not collect the whole heap every time.

It divides the heap into regions,
tracks which regions contain live objects,
then chooses collection sets that fit a pause-time goal.
```

G1 mencoba menyeimbangkan:

```text id="mylsuj"
pause time
throughput
heap footprint
old-region cleanup
allocation headroom
```

Hal paling penting:

```text id="k0c8ml"
G1 tuning starts from heap sizing and GC log analysis,
not from randomly adding -XX flags.
```

---

## G1 Region Model

```text id="yw4yx7"
Heap
│
├─ Region 1: Eden
├─ Region 2: Eden
├─ Region 3: Survivor
├─ Region 4: Old
├─ Region 5: Old
├─ Region 6: Humongous
├─ Region 7: Free
└─ Region N: ...
```

G1 heap tidak dipahami terutama sebagai satu continuous young/old block seperti model lama. G1 membagi heap menjadi region-region. Region bisa berubah peran: free, eden, survivor, old, atau humongous.

Oracle menjelaskan bahwa G1 menggunakan region-based heap layout, dan region yang berisi object besar dapat menjadi **humongous regions**. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/gctuning/garbage-first-g1-garbage-collector1.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Tabular Decomposition Map

| # | Category / Skill | Penjelasan praktis | Flag / Evidence | Kapan dipakai | Failure mode |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **G1 collector selection** | Mengaktifkan G1 secara eksplisit. Di banyak JDK modern, G1 sudah default untuk server-class machine, tapi sebaiknya tetap verifikasi runtime. | `-XX:+UseG1GC`, `jcmd VM.flags` | Baseline Java server microservice. | Mengira G1 aktif padahal runtime memakai collector lain. |
| 2 | **Region-based heap** | G1 membagi heap menjadi region dan memilih collection set dari region tersebut. | GC log `region`, `heap`, JFR | Memahami young/mixed/humongous behavior. | Menganalisis G1 seperti collector generational lama. |
| 3 | **Eden region** | Tempat allocation object baru. | GC log heap before/after | REST request object churn. | Eden cepat penuh karena allocation rate tinggi. |
| 4 | **Survivor region** | Object muda yang masih hidup setelah young GC. | GC age/survivor info | Object yang hidup beberapa GC. | Survivor overflow/promotion pressure. |
| 5 | **Old region** | Object long-lived. | old region occupancy | Cache, static object, workflow state. | Old regions naik terus, mixed GC tidak mengejar. |
| 6 | **Humongous region** | Object besar di G1 ditangani khusus dan dapat memakai satu atau lebih region. | `-Xlog:gc+humongous=debug` | Large JSON, byte array, file, PDF/Excel. | Fragmentation, full GC fallback, pause spike. |
| 7 | **Young GC** | Evakuasi live object dari eden/survivor ke survivor/old. | GC log young pause | Workload request/response. | Pause sering karena allocation rate tinggi. |
| 8 | **Concurrent marking** | G1 menandai live object di old regions secara concurrent untuk menentukan reclaimable regions. | GC log marking cycle | Saat old occupancy tumbuh. | Marking terlambat → allocation pressure/full GC. |
| 9 | **Remark pause** | Bagian marking yang stop-the-world untuk finalisasi marking. | GC log `Remark` | Melihat STW phase dalam marking. | Remark terlalu lama karena reference processing/class unloading. |
| 10 | **Cleanup** | Membersihkan metadata marking dan menyiapkan reclaim candidates. | GC log cleanup | Setelah concurrent marking. | Cleanup tidak cukup karena live set besar. |
| 11 | **Mixed GC** | G1 mengumpulkan young regions plus sebagian old regions yang menguntungkan. | GC log mixed collections | Membersihkan old garbage secara incremental. | Mixed GC terlalu sedikit/terlambat. |
| 12 | **Full GC fallback** | G1 gagal menjaga headroom atau menghadapi kondisi berat, lalu melakukan full GC yang mahal. | GC log `Full GC` | Sinyal serius. | Stop-the-world panjang, throughput drop. |
| 13 | **Pause-time goal** | Target pause yang dicoba dipenuhi G1, bukan hard SLA. | `-XX:MaxGCPauseMillis` | Latency tuning. | Pause target terlalu rendah → GC makin sering/CPU naik. |
| 14 | **Default pause target** | `MaxGCPauseMillis` sering default 200ms untuk G1. Oracle docs mencantumkan default target 200 ms. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/24/gctuning/garbage-first-garbage-collector-tuning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | `jcmd VM.flags` | Baseline tuning. | Mengira 200ms cocok untuk semua endpoint/SLO. |
| 15 | **Avoid fixed young sizing** | G1 memakai young size sebagai alat untuk memenuhi pause target. Oracle menyarankan menghindari membatasi young generation dengan `-Xmn`, `NewRatio`, dan sejenisnya karena dapat mengganggu pause-time control. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/24/gctuning/garbage-first-garbage-collector-tuning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Hindari `-Xmn` | Hampir semua G1 tuning. | G1 kehilangan fleksibilitas pause control. |
| 16 | **Heap headroom** | Ruang kosong diperlukan untuk allocation dan evacuation. | heap after GC, old occupancy | Mencegah evacuation failure/full GC. | Heap terlalu kecil atau `MaxRAMPercentage` terlalu rendah. |
| 17 | **IHOP** | Threshold old occupancy untuk mulai concurrent marking. | `-XX:InitiatingHeapOccupancyPercent` | Jika marking terlambat/terlalu cepat. | Marking start terlalu late → full GC; terlalu early → CPU overhead. |
| 18 | **Adaptive IHOP** | G1 dapat menyesuaikan IHOP berdasarkan allocation/marking behavior. | GC log IHOP | Default modern behavior. | Manual IHOP tanpa data bisa memperburuk. |
| 19 | **Evacuation failure** | G1 tidak punya cukup ruang untuk memindahkan live objects. | GC log evacuation failure | Heap pressure berat. | Full GC fallback. |
| 20 | **To-space exhausted** | Ruang tujuan evakuasi habis. | GC log | Heap headroom kurang. | Long pause/full GC. |
| 21 | **Remembered sets** | Struktur untuk melacak reference antar region. | GC log `remset`, JFR | Banyak cross-region refs. | Pause overhead tinggi. |
| 22 | **Reference processing** | Soft/weak/phantom/final reference processing bisa menambah pause. | GC log ref | Cache berbasis reference, finalizer/cleaner. | Remark/pause lebih lama. |
| 23 | **String deduplication** | G1 dapat deduplicate String jika diaktifkan. | `-XX:+UseStringDeduplication` | Banyak duplicate String. | CPU overhead jika manfaat kecil. |
| 24 | **Region size** | Ukuran region dipilih ergonomically, bisa diatur. | `-XX:G1HeapRegionSize` | Humongous object tuning khusus. | Region size salah bisa ubah behavior humongous/object layout. |
| 25 | **Parallel GC threads** | Thread untuk STW GC phase. | `-XX:ParallelGCThreads` | CPU-limited container. | Terlalu banyak thread vs CPU quota. |
| 26 | **Concurrent GC threads** | Thread untuk concurrent marking. | `-XX:ConcGCThreads` | Old cleanup dan CPU budget. | Terlalu sedikit → marking terlambat; terlalu banyak → app CPU terganggu. |
| 27 | **G1ReservePercent** | Reserve heap untuk mengurangi risiko evacuation failure. | `-XX:G1ReservePercent` | To-space/exhausted issues. | Reserve terlalu tinggi mengurangi usable heap. |
| 28 | **G1HeapWastePercent** | Mempengaruhi threshold waste untuk mixed GC decisions. | `-XX:G1HeapWastePercent` | Advanced old reclaim tuning. | Tuning premature. |
| 29 | **G1MixedGCCountTarget** | Target jumlah mixed GC setelah marking cycle. | `-XX:G1MixedGCCountTarget` | Advanced mixed GC behavior. | Mixed collection terlalu agresif/kurang. |
| 30 | **G1MixedGCLiveThresholdPercent** | Old region dengan live data di bawah threshold dipilih untuk mixed GC. | `-XX:G1MixedGCLiveThresholdPercent` | Advanced old region cleanup. | Mengumpulkan region yang terlalu live bisa mahal. |
| 31 | **GC log analysis** | Sumber utama evidence G1. | `-Xlog:gc*` | Semua tuning. | Tuning buta tanpa log. |
| 32 | **JFR correlation** | Menghubungkan GC dengan allocation, CPU, lock, socket/file I/O. | JFR | Incident p99/CPU. | GC disalahkan padahal bottleneck DB/lock. |
| 33 | **Application allocation fix** | Mengurangi object churn lebih efektif daripada banyak flag. | JFR allocation by class/method | JSON/MyBatis/DTO/cache. | GC tuning dipakai menutupi desain buruk. |
| 34 | **Production tuning loop** | Baseline → observe → hypothesis → one change → compare. | dashboard + GC log | Perubahan production. | Banyak flag sekaligus, tidak bisa atribusi. |

---

## G1 Collection Lifecycle

```text id="s9y03c"
Application allocates into eden regions
        ↓
Eden fills
        ↓
Young GC evacuates live objects
        ↓
Some objects remain survivor
        ↓
Long-lived objects become old
        ↓
Old occupancy grows
        ↓
Concurrent marking starts
        ↓
G1 identifies old regions with garbage
        ↓
Mixed GC collects young + selected old regions
        ↓
If G1 cannot keep up
        ↓
Full GC / evacuation failure / allocation stall
```

---

## G1 GC Event Types

| Event | Meaning | Normal? | Concern if |
|---|---|---|---|
| Young GC | Mengumpulkan eden/survivor | Ya | Terlalu sering atau pause tinggi |
| Concurrent Mark Cycle | Menandai live old objects | Ya | Mulai terlalu late atau terlalu sering |
| Remark | STW final marking | Ya | Pause remark panjang |
| Cleanup | Setelah marking | Ya | Old garbage tidak cukup reclaim |
| Mixed GC | Young + sebagian old regions | Ya | Tidak cukup menurunkan old occupancy |
| Full GC | Collection menyeluruh/fallback | Tidak ideal | Ada di latency-sensitive service |
| Evacuation Failure | G1 gagal memindahkan live object | Buruk | Hampir selalu butuh tindakan |
| Humongous Allocation | Object besar | Bisa normal | Sering dan memicu fragmentation/full GC |

---

## G1 Tuning Levers: Urutan yang Benar

### 1. Heap sizing dulu

Sebelum utak-atik G1 advanced flags:

```bash id="inbpwh"
-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
# atau
-Xmx<size>
```

Pertanyaan utama:

```text id="z6pbtj"
Apakah live set punya ruang cukup?
Apakah allocation burst punya headroom?
Apakah container masih punya native memory headroom?
```

G1 tidak bisa menyelamatkan service yang heap-nya lebih kecil dari live set + allocation pressure.

---

### 2. Pause target

```bash id="f427b3"
-XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=200
```

Jika latency lebih penting:

```bash id="aa6vh2"
-XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=100
```

Tetapi makin kecil target pause, G1 bisa melakukan GC lebih sering dan throughput bisa turun. Oracle menyebut jika throughput lebih penting, pause goal bisa dilonggarkan atau heap diperbesar; jika latency lebih penting, pause-time target bisa dimodifikasi. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/24/gctuning/garbage-first-garbage-collector-tuning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Praktis:

| Goal | Direction |
|---|---|
| Throughput lebih penting | Pause target lebih longgar, heap cukup |
| Latency lebih penting | Pause target lebih ketat, CPU/heap headroom cukup |
| CPU terbatas | Jangan terlalu agresif menurunkan pause target |
| Pod memory kecil | Jangan berharap pause kecil tanpa cost |

---

### 3. IHOP / marking start

```bash id="07ra7z"
-XX:InitiatingHeapOccupancyPercent=45
```

Gunakan jika evidence menunjukkan concurrent marking terlambat. Tetapi hati-hati: G1 modern punya adaptive IHOP. Manual tuning tanpa log bisa merusak ergonomics.

Gejala marking terlambat:

```text id="ordhtj"
old occupancy tumbuh cepat
concurrent cycle tidak selesai sebelum heap pressure
to-space exhausted
evacuation failure
Full GC
```

---

### 4. Humongous object handling

Humongous object biasanya muncul dari:

```text id="rkrbnl"
large byte[]
large String
large JSON payload
large XML document
large PDF/Excel/ZIP generation
large MyBatis result materialized as list
large Camunda variable payload
```

Evidence:

```bash id="pmqtqo"
-Xlog:gc+humongous=debug
```

Action utama biasanya bukan flag, tetapi desain:

```text id="etkumc"
limit request payload
stream file processing
paginate query
avoid giant byte[]
avoid full in-memory document generation
store reference instead of large workflow variable
```

---

### 5. Thread count vs CPU quota

Di Kubernetes, CPU limit kecil bisa membuat GC lambat. G1 punya STW parallel threads dan concurrent threads. Jangan otomatis tuning thread flags dulu; cek dulu CPU throttling dan effective CPU.

Evidence:

```bash id="exhs7e"
jcmd <pid> VM.flags
kubectl top pod <pod>
container_cpu_cfs_throttled_seconds_total
```

Possible advanced flags:

```bash id="aq0ir4"
-XX:ParallelGCThreads=<n>
-XX:ConcGCThreads=<n>
-XX:ActiveProcessorCount=<n>
```

---

## Recommended Baseline for G1

### Baseline microservice

```bash id="dqkiw8"
java \
  -XX:+UseG1GC \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0 \
  -XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=200 \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

### Lower latency attempt

```bash id="mz29qh"
java \
  -XX:+UseG1GC \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0 \
  -XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=100 \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

Cek setelah perubahan:

```text id="ef2sjp"
GC frequency naik atau tidak?
GC CPU naik atau tidak?
p99 membaik atau justru memburuk?
throughput turun atau tidak?
heap after-GC stabil atau tidak?
```

### Investigation mode

```bash id="9su0ay"
java \
  -XX:+UseG1GC \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0 \
  -XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary \
  -Xlog:gc*,gc+heap=debug,gc+humongous=debug,safepoint:file=/var/log/app/jvm.log:time,uptime,level,tags:filecount=5,filesize=50M \
  -XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=/recordings/startup.jfr,duration=120s,settings=profile \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

---

## What Not to Tune First

| Jangan mulai dari | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| `G1HeapRegionSize` | Bisa mengubah banyak behavior; gunakan hanya jika evidence humongous/region issue jelas |
| `G1NewSizePercent` / `G1MaxNewSizePercent` | Young sizing adalah alat G1 untuk pause control |
| `-Xmn` | Oracle menyarankan hindari fixed young gen karena mengganggu pause-time control G1 ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/24/gctuning/garbage-first-garbage-collector-tuning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Banyak mixed-GC flags | Advanced; butuh GC log yang matang |
| `ConcGCThreads` | Bisa membantu, tapi salah tuning bisa rebut CPU dari aplikasi |
| `System.gc()` workaround | Bisa memicu pause dan menutupi root cause |
| Naikkan heap tanpa cek RSS | Bisa membuat pod OOMKilled |
| Ganti ke ZGC langsung | Mungkin benar, tapi pahami G1 problem dulu |

---

## Reading G1 GC Logs: Checklist Praktis

Aktifkan:

```bash id="sil5d1"
-Xlog:gc*:file=/tmp/gc.log:time,uptime,level,tags:filecount=5,filesize=50M
```

Baca dengan pertanyaan ini:

| Pertanyaan | Sinyal |
|---|---|
| G1 aktif? | `Using G1` / `UseG1GC` |
| Young GC seberapa sering? | Banyak event young per detik/menit |
| Pause young berapa p95/p99? | Impact ke p99 |
| Ada concurrent mark cycle? | Normal jika old tumbuh |
| Marking selesai sebelum pressure? | Jika tidak, risk full GC |
| Mixed GC terjadi? | Old garbage cleanup |
| Old usage turun setelah mixed GC? | Jika tidak, live set besar |
| Ada humongous allocation? | Large object problem |
| Ada evacuation failure/to-space exhausted? | Heap headroom kurang |
| Ada Full GC? | Sinyal serius |
| Heap after-GC naik terus? | Leak/retention |
| GC time ratio tinggi? | Throughput loss |

---

## Common G1 Problems & Diagnosis

### Problem 1 - Young GC terlalu sering

Gejala:

```text id="yfnm7h"
Young GC every few hundred milliseconds
pause individually small
CPU GC time high
p99 latency noisy
```

Kemungkinan root cause:

```text id="npn49y"
allocation rate tinggi
heap terlalu kecil
payload terlalu besar
JSON/MyBatis mapping object churn
logging/exception allocation
```

Evidence:

```text id="fns99s"
JFR allocation by class/method
GC log young frequency
heap after young GC rendah/stabil
```

Action:

```text id="wcgir9"
kurangi allocation
review DTO mapping
limit payload
increase heap if RSS allows
relax pause target if throughput more important
```

---

### Problem 2 - Old occupancy naik terus

Gejala:

```text id="gubx7g"
old regions grow over time
heap after GC rises
mixed GC does not reduce enough
eventually Full GC/OOM
```

Kemungkinan root cause:

```text id="k2xj1d"
cache tidak bounded
ThreadLocal leak
queue backlog
large workflow/job state
static map/list
listener registry
MyBatis result retained
```

Evidence:

```text id="d8ids7"
heap dump dominator tree
heap dump diff
old occupancy trend
```

Action:

```text id="9cbtsp"
fix retention
bound cache/queue
clean ThreadLocal
reduce live set
increase heap only as temporary mitigation
```

---

### Problem 3 - Concurrent marking terlambat

Gejala:

```text id="xbvp7z"
old grows fast
marking cycle starts late
to-space exhausted / evacuation failure
Full GC
```

Evidence:

```text id="c5wxou"
GC log concurrent cycle timing
old occupancy at cycle start
allocation rate during marking
Full GC cause
```

Action:

```text id="ja61t5"
increase heap headroom
reduce allocation rate
possibly lower IHOP after evidence
increase CPU/headroom if marking too slow
```

Possible flag after evidence:

```bash id="iorlp3"
-XX:InitiatingHeapOccupancyPercent=30
```

But first check adaptive behavior and whether heap/CPU is the real issue.

---

### Problem 4 - Humongous allocation

Gejala:

```text id="1a01gj"
humongous allocation logs
large byte[] / String
pause spike
fragmentation
Full GC around large payload
```

Evidence:

```bash id="1tcoi2"
-Xlog:gc+humongous=debug
jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram
JFR allocation profile
```

Action:

```text id="npwh2f"
stream payload
limit request size
paginate DB result
avoid materializing file/document fully in memory
review byte[]/String usage
```

Advanced option only after evidence:

```bash id="qln79y"
-XX:G1HeapRegionSize=<size>
```

But this is not first-line tuning.

---

### Problem 5 - Full GC appears

Gejala:

```text id="lrk2mr"
Full GC pause seconds
throughput drops
p99/p999 spike
heap after Full GC still high
```

Diagnosis possibilities:

```text id="j63u5o"
live set too large
old gen pressure
evacuation failure
humongous fragmentation
heap too small
allocation spike
CPU throttling
```

Action:

```text id="gomriq"
capture GC log around Full GC
capture heap dump if safe
check old occupancy before/after
check humongous logs
check RSS/container limit
fix root cause
```

---

## G1 Flags: Practical Table

| Flag | Meaning | Default/Note | Use carefully when |
|---|---|---|---|
| `-XX:+UseG1GC` | Select G1 | Often default in modern server JDK, verify | You want explicit collector |
| `-XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=200` | Pause target | G1 common default 200ms ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/24/gctuning/garbage-first-garbage-collector-tuning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Tune latency/throughput trade-off |
| `-XX:InitiatingHeapOccupancyPercent=45` | Old occupancy threshold for marking | Adaptive behavior may apply | Marking starts too late/early |
| `-XX:G1HeapRegionSize=...` | Region size | Ergonomic default | Humongous/region issue proven |
| `-XX:G1ReservePercent=...` | Reserve free heap for evacuation | Advanced | To-space exhausted/evacuation failure |
| `-XX:ParallelGCThreads=...` | STW worker threads | Ergonomic | CPU-limited container |
| `-XX:ConcGCThreads=...` | Concurrent GC threads | Ergonomic | Marking too slow or app CPU contention |
| `-XX:+UseStringDeduplication` | Deduplicate duplicate strings | Off unless enabled | Many duplicate strings proven |
| `-XX:+DisableExplicitGC` | Ignore `System.gc()` | Optional | Libraries call explicit GC unexpectedly |
| `-Xlog:gc*` | GC logging | Must-have evidence | Always baseline |

---

## Application-Level Fixes that Beat G1 Tuning

| Problem | JVM symptom | Better fix |
|---|---|---|
| Large MyBatis result | heap spike, humongous/old pressure | pagination/cursor/projection |
| Large JSON payload | allocation spike, pause | request size limit/streaming |
| Unbounded cache | old occupancy grows | max size/TTL/eviction |
| Camunda variable huge | heap + DB pressure | store reference, not payload |
| Excessive exceptions | allocation + CPU | avoid exception for normal flow |
| Logging huge object | allocation + IO | structured compact logging |
| Too many worker threads | native memory + context switching | bounded executor/backpressure |
| File generation in memory | byte[] humongous | stream to disk/object storage |
| DTO copy chain excessive | allocation churn | reduce mapping layers on hot path |
| DB pool too large | memory/thread/DB pressure | right-size pool |

---

## Tuning Workflow

```text id="6rho35"
1. Confirm G1 is active
2. Enable GC logs
3. Run representative load
4. Capture p50/p95/p99 latency + CPU + RSS + heap
5. Read GC frequency, pause, before/after heap
6. Identify pattern:
   allocation pressure / live set / humongous / full GC / CPU
7. Fix application/config first if obvious
8. Change one JVM parameter
9. Repeat same load
10. Compare before/after
11. Document decision and rollback
```

---

| Measurement | Before | After | Improved? |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Throughput req/s |  |  |  |
| p95 latency |  |  |  |
| p99 latency |  |  |  |
| Avg CPU |  |  |  |
| Container RSS |  |  |  |
| Heap after GC |  |  |  |

| Mixed GC/min |  |  |  |
| Full GC count |  |  |  |
| GC time ratio |  |  |  |

## G1 Production Baseline for Kubernetes

```yaml id="7d57qa"
env:
  - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
    value: >-
      -XX:+UseG1GC
      -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
      -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0
      -XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=200
      -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
      -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
      -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
      -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
      -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
      -XX:ErrorFile=/dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log
```

For investigation:

```yaml id="v74wwn"
env:
  - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
    value: >-
      -XX:+UseG1GC
      -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
      -XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary
      -Xlog:gc*,gc+heap=debug,gc+humongous=debug,safepoint:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
      -XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=/recordings/startup.jfr,duration=120s,settings=profile
```

---

## G1 Troubleshooting Matrix

| Symptom | Likely cause | Evidence | First action |
|---|---|---|---|
| Young GC very frequent | Allocation rate high / heap small | JFR allocation, GC frequency | Reduce allocation or increase heap |
| Pause too long | Collection set too large / CPU low / live objects high | GC pause phases | Check CPU, heap, pause target |
| Old occupancy grows | Retention/leak/live set | heap after GC trend | Heap dump/dominator |
| Mixed GC ineffective | Old regions too live | mixed GC after heap | Reduce live set |
| Full GC | Severe pressure/failure | GC cause | Capture dump/log, fix root cause |
| To-space exhausted | Not enough evacuation headroom | GC log | More heap/reserve/reduce allocation |
| Humongous allocation | Large arrays/strings/files | humongous log | Stream/limit/chunk |
| RSS high, heap ok | Native/direct/thread/metaspace | NMT | Non-heap investigation |
| CPU high after lowering pause target | More GC work | CPU + GC count | Relax target or add CPU |
| p99 unchanged after GC tuning | GC not root cause | APM correlation | Check DB/lock/network |

---

## Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa salah | Yang benar |
|---|---|---|
| Copy G1 flags dari blog lama | JDK behavior berubah, workload beda | Pakai docs + evidence runtime |
| Set `-Xmn` dengan G1 | Mengganggu pause-time control G1 | Biarkan G1 adapt young size |
| Turunkan `MaxGCPauseMillis` ekstrem | Bisa menaikkan GC frequency/CPU | Tuning bertahap: 200 → 150 → 100 |
| Naikkan heap saat pod OOMKilled | Bisa memperparah RSS | Bedakan Java OOM vs kernel OOM |
| Tuning IHOP tanpa full log | Bisa mulai marking terlalu early/late | Lihat marking cycle dan old growth |
| Mengabaikan humongous objects | Large payload bisa bikin full GC | Log humongous dan ubah design |
| Mengabaikan CPU throttling | GC butuh CPU | Monitor throttling |
| Hanya lihat average pause | Tail latency yang penting | p95/p99/p999 |
| Tidak punya rollback | Tuning bisa regresi | Document before/after |
| Banyak flag sekaligus | Tidak tahu efek masing-masing | One change at a time |

---

## Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Step | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Region model | Bisa menjelaskan eden/survivor/old/humongous regions |
| 2 | Young/mixed/full GC | Bisa membaca event utama G1 |
| 3 | Pause target | Paham `MaxGCPauseMillis` sebagai soft goal |
| 4 | Heap headroom | Bisa menghindari evacuation failure |
| 5 | Concurrent marking/IHOP | Bisa mengenali marking terlambat |
| 6 | Humongous object | Bisa menemukan large allocation problem |
| 7 | GC log analysis | Bisa menentukan root cause dari log |
| 8 | Tuning workflow | Bisa melakukan before/after secara aman |
