## Response:
7/14/2026, 12:29:25 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 8/12: ZGC / Low-Latency GC**. Saya akan tekankan kapan ZGC benar-benar layak dipakai, trade-off-nya, dan bagaimana membedakannya dari masalah aplikasi yang seharusnya diselesaikan di kode/config.

# Seri 8/12 - ZGC / Low-Latency GC

## Introduction

Seri ini fokus pada **ZGC**, garbage collector untuk workload yang sangat sensitif terhadap latency, terutama tail latency seperti p95, p99, dan p999.

ZGC adalah collector yang dirancang agar pekerjaan GC berat berjalan **concurrently** dengan application threads, sehingga pause aplikasi sangat kecil. Dokumentasi Oracle JDK 25 menjelaskan ZGC sebagai **scalable low-latency garbage collector** yang melakukan expensive work secara concurrent dan tidak menghentikan application threads lebih dari sekitar satu milidetik; pause time juga independen dari ukuran heap dan ZGC cocok untuk heap dari ratusan MB sampai 16 TB. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/gctuning/z-garbage-collector.html))

Namun keputusan memakai ZGC tidak boleh dimulai dari “ZGC lebih modern”. Keputusan yang benar dimulai dari pertanyaan:

```text id="9ynayn"
Apakah service benar-benar punya strict pause-time requirement?
Apakah p99/p999 latency memang terganggu oleh GC pause?
Apakah throughput loss dan CPU overhead ZGC bisa diterima?
Apakah heap punya cukup headroom untuk concurrent GC?
Apakah root cause bukan query besar, object churn, cache leak, atau native memory issue?
```

Oracle sendiri menyarankan: kecuali aplikasi punya strict pause-time requirement, biarkan VM memilih collector lebih dulu; G1 default di banyak konfigurasi, sedangkan ZGC ditujukan untuk aplikasi yang membutuhkan low latency dengan trade-off throughput. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/gctuning/available-collectors.html))

---

## Diagram

```text id="7mv3si"
ZGC / Low-Latency GC Mental Model
│
├─ 1. When ZGC Makes Sense
│  ├─ strict p99 / p999 latency
│  ├─ large heap with pause sensitivity
│  ├─ GC pause proven as root cause
│  ├─ enough CPU headroom
│  └─ enough heap headroom
│
├─ 2. ZGC Design Model
│  ├─ concurrent marking
│  ├─ concurrent relocation
│  ├─ colored pointers
│  ├─ load barriers
│  ├─ store barriers in generational mode
│  └─ very short stop-the-world phases
│
├─ 3. Generational ZGC
│  ├─ young generation
│  ├─ old generation
│  ├─ young collection
│  ├─ old collection
│  ├─ remembered set
│  └─ adaptive sizing / tenuring
│
├─ 4. Main Tuning Knobs
│  ├─ -XX:+UseZGC
│  ├─ -Xmx / MaxRAMPercentage
│  ├─ -XX:SoftMaxHeapSize
│  ├─ -XX:ConcGCThreads
│  ├─ -XX:-ZUncommit
│  └─ GC logging / JFR
│
├─ 5. Trade-Offs
│  ├─ lower pause
│  ├─ possible lower throughput
│  ├─ more concurrent CPU work
│  ├─ more memory headroom needed
│  ├─ barrier overhead
│  └─ different observability patterns than G1
│
├─ 6. Failure Modes
│  ├─ allocation stalls
│  ├─ heap headroom too small
│  ├─ CPU throttling
│  ├─ native/container memory pressure
│  ├─ object churn still too high
│  ├─ live set too large
│  └─ wrong root-cause attribution
│
└─ 7. Production Workflow
   ├─ prove GC pause issue under G1
   ├─ baseline p99 / p999 / CPU / RSS / GC
   ├─ switch to ZGC in controlled env
   ├─ load test same workload
   ├─ compare latency, throughput, CPU, memory
   ├─ verify no allocation stalls
   └─ document rollback
```

---

## Core Mental Model

```text id="zgtu62"
G1 tries to meet pause goals by choosing collection work carefully.

ZGC tries to keep pauses extremely short by doing almost all expensive work concurrently.
```

Konsekuensinya:

```text id="42bsy0"
ZGC can reduce GC pause impact,
but it does not eliminate memory pressure,
does not fix object leaks,
does not reduce bad allocation patterns by itself,
and may consume more CPU/heap headroom.
```

---

## Status Modern ZGC: Java 21 vs Java 24+

Penting untuk tidak salah pakai flag.

| JDK Version | ZGC Mode | Cara enable | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| JDK 17 | ZGC tersedia dan supported, non-generational | `-XX:+UseZGC` | Banyak enterprise masih di Java 17; evaluasi carefully. |
| JDK 21 | Generational ZGC tersedia di samping non-generational | `-XX:+UseZGC -XX:+ZGenerational` | JEP 439 memperkenalkan Generational ZGC; `-XX:+UseZGC` saja memilih non-generational di JDK 21. ([openjdk.org](https://openjdk.org/jeps/439)) |
| JDK 24+ | ZGC generational by default; non-generational mode dihapus | `-XX:+UseZGC` | Oracle JDK 25 docs menyatakan sejak JDK 24, ZGC adalah generational GC dan option `ZGenerational` sudah dihapus. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/gctuning/z-garbage-collector.html)) |
| JDK 25+ | Generational ZGC only | `-XX:+UseZGC` | JEP 490 delivered di JDK 24: non-generational ZGC removed, generational mode retained as default. ([openjdk.org](https://openjdk.org/jeps/490)) |

Untuk project Anda yang menyebut **Java 17+**, rule praktisnya:

```text id="jqrza7"
Java 17: use -XX:+UseZGC
Java 21: use -XX:+UseZGC -XX:+ZGenerational if you want generational ZGC
Java 24+: use -XX:+UseZGC only
```

Pastikan selalu validasi dengan:

```bash id="vtxazz"
jcmd <pid> VM.flags
java -Xlog:gc -version
```

---

## Tabular Decomposition Map

| # | Category / Skill | Penjelasan praktis | Flag / Evidence | Kapan dipakai | Failure mode |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **ZGC positioning** | ZGC adalah low-latency collector, bukan default universal untuk semua service. | `-XX:+UseZGC` | Strict p99/p999 latency. | Memakai ZGC tanpa GC pause evidence. |
| 2 | **Low-latency objective** | Tujuan utama ZGC adalah pause sangat kecil, bukan throughput maksimum. Oracle menyebut ZGC memberi max pause time di bawah 1 ms dengan cost throughput. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/gctuning/available-collectors.html)) | GC log, JFR, p99/p999 latency | API latency-sensitive. | Throughput turun karena concurrent/barrier overhead. |
| 3 | **Pause independent from heap size** | Pause ZGC tidak meningkat secara langsung mengikuti ukuran heap. | ZGC docs, GC log | Large heap service. | Mengira heap besar tidak butuh headroom. |
| 4 | **Concurrent GC work** | Expensive GC work berjalan bersamaan dengan app thread. | CPU metrics, GC log | Menghindari STW pause panjang. | CPU contention dengan application threads. |
| 5 | **Heap headroom** | Karena GC concurrent, heap harus cukup untuk live set dan allocation selama GC berjalan. Oracle menyebut tuning terpenting ZGC adalah maximum heap size agar live set dan allocation headroom cukup. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/gctuning/z-garbage-collector.html)) | `-Xmx`, `MaxRAMPercentage`, allocation rate | Semua ZGC deployment. | Allocation stall karena heap terlalu kecil. |
| 6 | **Generational ZGC** | Heap dibagi young dan old generation; young untuk recent allocations, old untuk long-lived objects. JEP 439 mendeskripsikan young/old generation dan independent collection. ([openjdk.org](https://openjdk.org/jeps/439)) | JDK 21+: `-XX:+ZGenerational`; JDK 24+: default | Workload dengan banyak short-lived objects. | Salah flag antar versi JDK. |
| 7 | **Colored pointers** | ZGC memakai metadata pada pointer untuk melacak state object. | Internal concept | Memahami barrier behavior. | Tidak perlu dituning langsung. |
| 8 | **Load barriers** | Code barrier saat aplikasi membaca reference agar view object graph tetap konsisten. JEP 439 menjelaskan load barrier sebagai mekanisme yang disisipkan saat field reference dibaca. ([openjdk.org](https://openjdk.org/jeps/439)) | JFR/CPU indirectly | Semua ZGC. | Barrier overhead tampak sebagai CPU cost. |
| 9 | **Store barriers** | Generational ZGC memakai store barriers untuk tracking reference antar generasi. | Generational ZGC | Young/old remembered-set tracking. | Workload write-heavy bisa punya overhead. |
| 10 | **Remembered set** | Menyimpan pointer old-to-young agar young collection aman. JEP 439 menjelaskan remembered set pada Generational ZGC. ([openjdk.org](https://openjdk.org/jeps/439)) | GC internals/log | Banyak old-to-young refs. | Overhead jika object graph sangat mutable. |
| 11 | **Young collection** | Generational ZGC dapat fokus pada object muda yang profitable untuk dikumpulkan. | GC log, JFR | REST workload object churn. | Allocation rate tetap terlalu tinggi. |
| 12 | **Old collection** | Mengumpulkan long-lived object secara concurrent. | GC log | Cache/state/workflow object. | Live set besar membuat heap pressure tetap tinggi. |
| 13 | **Large object handling** | Generational ZGC menangani large objects lebih baik daripada beberapa collector karena design virtual/physical memory dan aging. JEP 439 menyebut ZGC sudah menangani large objects dengan baik dan generational mode memungkinkan large objects berada di young generation. ([openjdk.org](https://openjdk.org/jeps/439)) | JFR allocation, GC log | Large JSON/file/document. | Tetap buruk jika aplikasi materialize payload sangat besar. |
| 14 | **Allocation stall** | Aplikasi tertahan karena allocation tidak bisa dilayani sebelum GC reclaim cukup memory. | ZGC log, JFR | Heap terlalu kecil / allocation spike. | ZGC pause kecil tapi latency tetap spike karena stall. |
| 15 | **Throughput cost** | ZGC bisa mengurangi throughput karena concurrent work dan barriers. Oracle available collectors menyebut ZGC pause rendah dengan cost some throughput. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/gctuning/available-collectors.html)) | req/s, CPU, GC CPU | Latency-first service. | Latency membaik tapi capacity turun. |
| 16 | **CPU headroom** | ZGC butuh CPU untuk concurrent marking/relocation. | CPU usage/throttling | Kubernetes CPU-limited pod. | CPU throttling membuat GC tidak catch up. |
| 17 | **Container memory budget** | ZGC tetap butuh native/heap headroom. | RSS, pod memory | Docker/Kubernetes. | Pod OOMKilled walau pause kecil. |
| 18 | **SoftMaxHeapSize** | Soft limit untuk heap growth; ZGC berusaha tetap di bawahnya tetapi boleh melebihi sampai `Xmx` agar aplikasi tidak stall. OpenJDK ZGC wiki menjelaskan behavior ini. ([wiki.openjdk.org](https://wiki.openjdk.org/spaces/zgc/pages/34668579/Main)) | `-XX:SoftMaxHeapSize=...` | Footprint-sensitive env. | Salah paham sebagai hard limit. |
| 19 | **ZUncommit** | ZGC dapat mengembalikan unused memory ke OS; bisa membantu footprint tapi bisa berdampak latency. OpenJDK ZGC wiki mencatat unused memory uncommit aktif default dan bisa dinonaktifkan dengan `-XX:-ZUncommit`. ([wiki.openjdk.org](https://wiki.openjdk.org/spaces/zgc/pages/34668579/Main)) | `-XX:-ZUncommit`, `-Xms` | Memory footprint vs latency. | Latency spike saat memory recommit. |
| 20 | **ConcGCThreads** | Mengatur jumlah concurrent GC threads, tapi Generational ZGC makin adaptif sehingga jarang perlu dituning manual. | `-XX:ConcGCThreads` | CPU/GC catch-up issue. | Terlalu tinggi mencuri CPU app; terlalu rendah GC tertinggal. |
| 21 | **ParallelGCThreads** | STW phase kecil tetap bisa memakai worker threads. | `-XX:ParallelGCThreads` | CPU-limited container. | Oversubscription di CPU quota kecil. |
| 22 | **GC logging** | Evidence utama untuk melihat ZGC behavior. | `-Xlog:gc*` | Semua eksperimen ZGC. | Switch collector tanpa observability. |
| 23 | **JFR profiling** | Penting untuk allocation, CPU, latency, socket/file, lock. | `jcmd JFR.start` | Bandingkan G1 vs ZGC. | Mengira semua p99 dari GC padahal DB/network. |
| 24 | **G1 vs ZGC decision** | ZGC dipilih jika G1 pause terbukti root cause dan trade-off diterima. | before/after benchmark | Production migration. | Pindah collector sebelum fix code/pool/query. |
| 25 | **Rollback strategy** | Collector switch harus reversible. | Helm flag, deployment strategy | Production rollout. | Tidak bisa rollback saat throughput turun. |

---

## Kapan ZGC Cocok?

ZGC layak dipertimbangkan jika beberapa kondisi ini benar:

| Kondisi | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| p99/p999 latency sangat penting | ZGC dirancang untuk pause sangat kecil. |
| GC pause terbukti berkontribusi ke tail latency | Harus ada correlation antara GC log/JFR dan APM latency. |
| Heap relatif besar atau live set besar | ZGC pause tidak bergantung langsung pada heap size. |
| Service punya CPU headroom | ZGC melakukan kerja concurrent. |
| Throughput sedikit turun masih diterima | Ada trade-off throughput. |
| Anda bisa melakukan load test representatif | Collector migration harus dibuktikan. |
| Observability siap | GC log, JFR, CPU, RSS, allocation rate, p99/p999. |

Contoh service yang mungkin cocok:

```text id="sg7lvo"
pricing / quote API dengan strict p99
real-time decisioning service
latency-sensitive orchestration API
high-volume order validation API
large-heap in-memory rule/evaluation service
workflow service dengan tail-latency sensitif
```

---

## Kapan Tetap Pakai G1?

G1 lebih masuk akal jika:

| Kondisi | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Latency requirement tidak ekstrem | G1 sudah default kuat untuk banyak server workload. |
| Throughput lebih penting daripada sub-ms pause | ZGC bisa punya throughput cost. |
| Heap kecil dan GC pause G1 sudah aman | Switching tidak memberi value. |
| Bottleneck utama DB/network/lock | ZGC tidak menyelesaikan bottleneck eksternal. |
| CPU limit ketat | ZGC concurrent work bisa berebut CPU. |
| Service banyak batch throughput | Parallel/G1 bisa lebih cocok. |
| Team belum punya GC/JFR observability | Migration tanpa data berisiko. |

Oracle available collectors guide menyebut G1 dipilih default di banyak hardware/OS dan dapat memenuhi pause-time goal dengan high probability sambil tetap high throughput; ZGC ditujukan untuk low-latency requirement yang lebih ketat. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/gctuning/available-collectors.html))

---

## G1 vs ZGC Practical Comparison

| Dimensi | G1 | ZGC |
|---|---|---|
| Primary goal | Balanced throughput + pause goal | Very low pause |
| Default status | Default pada banyak server config | Explicit via `-XX:+UseZGC` |
| Pause target | Soft goal, misalnya `MaxGCPauseMillis` | Pause sangat kecil by design |
| Heap model | Region-based, young/old/mixed GC | Generational concurrent collector di JDK 24+ |
| Large heap pause | Bisa meningkat tergantung workload | Pause independent dari heap size |
| Throughput | Biasanya kuat | Bisa lebih rendah |
| CPU overhead | Moderate | Bisa lebih tinggi karena concurrent work/barriers |
| Tuning | Heap + pause target + G1-specific knobs | Mostly heap/headroom; minimal manual tuning |
| Best for | General server microservice | Strict low-latency workloads |
| Failure risk | Full GC, humongous allocation, old pressure | Allocation stall, CPU contention, insufficient headroom |
| First choice? | Ya untuk banyak service | Setelah evidence menunjukkan kebutuhan low pause |

---

## ZGC Enablement Templates

### Java 17

```bash id="p0jz7c"
java \
  -XX:+UseZGC \
  -Xmx2g \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

### Java 21 - Generational ZGC

```bash id="knaif1"
java \
  -XX:+UseZGC \
  -XX:+ZGenerational \
  -Xmx2g \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

JEP 439 menyatakan pada JDK 21, `-XX:+UseZGC` memilih non-generational ZGC dan `-XX:+ZGenerational` diperlukan untuk memilih Generational ZGC. ([openjdk.org](https://openjdk.org/jeps/439))

### Java 24+ / Java 25+

```bash id="hqcgtw"
java \
  -XX:+UseZGC \
  -Xmx2g \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

Sejak JDK 24, non-generational mode dihapus dan `-XX:+UseZGC` memakai Generational ZGC. ([openjdk.org](https://openjdk.org/jeps/490))

---

## Production Baseline Template for Kubernetes

### Conservative ZGC template

```yaml id="ykwt26"
env:
  - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
    value: >-
      -XX:+UseZGC
      -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
      -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
      -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
      -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
      -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
      -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
      -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
      -XX:ErrorFile=/dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log
```

Kenapa `MaxRAMPercentage=60` sebagai awal? Karena ZGC tetap butuh headroom untuk concurrent operation, native memory, thread stack, metaspace, code cache, direct buffers, dan agents. Oracle menekankan bahwa ZGC membutuhkan maximum heap yang cukup untuk live set dan allocation selama GC berjalan. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/gctuning/z-garbage-collector.html))

---

### Java 21 Generational ZGC template

```yaml id="wxhism"
env:
  - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
    value: >-
      -XX:+UseZGC
      -XX:+ZGenerational
      -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
      -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
      -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
      -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
      -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
      -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
      -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
```

---

### Investigation template

```yaml id="8s8f4i"
env:
  - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
    value: >-
      -XX:+UseZGC
      -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
      -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
      -XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary
      -Xlog:gc*,safepoint,os+container=info:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
      -XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=/recordings/zgc-startup.jfr,duration=120s,settings=profile
      -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
      -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
```

---

## ZGC Tuning: Urutan yang Benar

### 1. Jangan mulai dari banyak flag

OpenJDK ZGC wiki menyatakan ZGC didesain adaptif dan membutuhkan minimal manual configuration; tuning knob utama adalah maximum heap size. ([wiki.openjdk.org](https://wiki.openjdk.org/spaces/zgc/pages/34668579/Main))

Jadi urutannya:

```text id="1p1r2f"
1. Set collector correctly.
2. Set heap maximum safely.
3. Ensure CPU headroom.
4. Enable GC logs.
5. Run representative load.
6. Inspect allocation stalls, GC cycles, CPU, RSS, p99/p999.
7. Only then consider advanced flags.
```

---

### 2. Heap maximum adalah tuning utama

```bash id="3mu7eq"
-Xmx4g
```

atau container-aware:

```bash id="zll1eu"
-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
```

ZGC perlu heap cukup untuk:

```text id="xx0asf"
live set
allocation while GC is running
short-lived object bursts
relocation/concurrent progress
traffic spike
```

Jika heap terlalu kecil:

```text id="kn3v1n"
pause tetap kecil,
tetapi aplikasi bisa mengalami allocation stall,
CPU GC naik,
latency tetap buruk.
```

---

### 3. SoftMaxHeapSize untuk footprint-sensitive service

```bash id="746zn7"
-Xmx5g
-XX:SoftMaxHeapSize=4g
```

Makna:

```text id="dnusqc"
ZGC akan berusaha memakai 4g sebagai soft target,
tetapi boleh tumbuh sampai 5g jika perlu untuk mencegah stall.
```

OpenJDK ZGC wiki menjelaskan `SoftMaxHeapSize` sebagai soft limit yang ZGC usahakan tidak dilewati, tetapi ZGC tetap boleh melewati sampai `Xmx` jika diperlukan agar aplikasi tidak menunggu GC reclaim memory. ([wiki.openjdk.org](https://wiki.openjdk.org/spaces/zgc/pages/34668579/Main))

Cocok untuk:

```text id="nb4jea"
service dengan normal footprint rendah,
tetapi butuh burst headroom,
dan ingin menghindari allocation stall saat spike.
```

---

### 4. ZUncommit untuk footprint vs latency

Default ZGC dapat mengembalikan unused memory ke OS. Ini bagus untuk footprint, tetapi recommit memory dapat berdampak pada latency. OpenJDK ZGC wiki menyebut uncommit aktif default dan bisa dimatikan dengan `-XX:-ZUncommit`. ([wiki.openjdk.org](https://wiki.openjdk.org/spaces/zgc/pages/34668579/Main))

Disable uncommit:

```bash id="5l7lxw"
-XX:-ZUncommit
```

Cocok jika:

```text id="9xm329"
latency sangat sensitif
memory sudah dedicated
RSS stabil lebih penting daripada footprint rendah
```

Tidak cocok jika:

```text id="nbicw5"
cluster memory ketat
banyak pod sharing node
footprint idle penting
```

---

### 5. Concurrent GC threads: jangan tuning dulu

Possible flag:

```bash id="0omqh4"
-XX:ConcGCThreads=<n>
```

Tetapi untuk Generational ZGC, ZGC makin adaptif. OpenJDK ZGC wiki bahkan mencatat bagian `ConcGCThreads` lebih relevan untuk non-generational ZGC dan Generational ZGC lebih adaptif sehingga lebih jarang perlu tuning thread GC. ([wiki.openjdk.org](https://wiki.openjdk.org/spaces/zgc/pages/34668579/Main))

Gunakan hanya jika evidence menunjukkan:

```text id="t1j56k"
GC tidak catch up
allocation stalls
CPU headroom masih ada
concurrent phase terlalu lambat
```

---

## ZGC Failure Modes

### 1. Allocation Stall

Gejala:

```text id="22x9c1"
GC pause kecil
tetapi p99 tetap spike
ZGC log menunjukkan allocation stall
heap mendekati limit
CPU GC tinggi
```

Kemungkinan:

```text id="7tosn8"
Xmx terlalu kecil
allocation rate terlalu tinggi
live set terlalu besar
CPU tidak cukup untuk concurrent GC
```

Action:

```text id="6djr9r"
increase heap if RSS allows
reduce allocation rate
increase CPU request/limit
check object retention
check payload/query size
```

---

### 2. CPU Contention

Gejala:

```text id="mxtz5n"
ZGC menurunkan pause
tetapi throughput turun
CPU naik
application threads bersaing dengan GC threads
Kubernetes CPU throttling naik
```

Action:

```text id="fy3icx"
increase CPU limit/request
relax workload expectations
compare with G1
avoid over-tight CPU quota
profile with JFR
```

---

### 3. Heap Headroom Too Small

Gejala:

```text id="6cp13q"
heap usage sering dekat Xmx
GC cycle sering
allocation stalls
p99 tetap buruk
```

Action:

```text id="7ibdgu"
increase Xmx / MaxRAMPercentage if container allows
increase pod memory limit
reduce live set
reduce allocation burst
```

---

### 4. Wrong Root Cause

Gejala:

```text id="ruevkg"
Switch ke ZGC
GC pause turun
p99 tidak berubah
```

Kemungkinan root cause sebenarnya:

```text id="6u61w8"
DB query latency
connection pool wait
HTTP downstream latency
lock contention
CPU throttling
thread starvation
large payload processing
Camunda job backlog
```

Action:

```text id="s9qmsq"
JFR end-to-end
APM trace correlation
thread dump
DB pool metrics
downstream latency metrics
```

---

## ZGC Observability Checklist

Minimal yang harus dilihat saat evaluasi ZGC:

| Metric / Evidence | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| GC pause p99/p999 | Membuktikan pause memang turun |
| Allocation stalls | ZGC-specific risk jika heap/CPU tidak cukup |
| Application p99/p999 | Outcome utama |
| Throughput req/s atau jobs/s | Trade-off capacity |
| CPU usage | Concurrent GC cost |
| CPU throttling | K8s risk |
| Heap used / committed / max | Headroom |
| RSS / container memory | Pod OOM risk |
| Allocation rate | Pressure source |
| Live set estimate | Minimal heap requirement |
| Thread count | Native memory / scheduling |
| DB pool waiting | Membuktikan bukan DB bottleneck |
| JFR allocation hot methods | Code-level fix target |
| GC log before/after | Evidence migration |

---

## G1 → ZGC Migration Workflow

```text id="f4oh8w"
1. Baseline current G1 service
   ├─ GC log
   ├─ JFR
   ├─ p95/p99/p999
   ├─ CPU
   ├─ RSS
   ├─ heap after-GC
   └─ throughput

2. Prove GC pause matters
   ├─ correlate GC pause timestamp with latency spike
   └─ rule out DB/network/lock/pool bottleneck

3. Switch to ZGC in non-prod
   ├─ same heap or slightly more headroom
   ├─ same traffic profile
   └─ same request mix

4. Compare
   ├─ p99/p999 improved?
   ├─ throughput changed?
   ├─ CPU changed?
   ├─ RSS changed?
   ├─ allocation stalls?
   └─ error rate?

5. Tune minimally
   ├─ Xmx / MaxRAMPercentage
   ├─ SoftMaxHeapSize if needed
   ├─ ZUncommit only if latency/footprint trade-off known
   └─ avoid advanced flags unless evidence-driven

6. Rollout safely
   ├─ canary
   ├─ rollback flag to G1
   ├─ dashboard
   └─ post-deploy comparison
```

---

## Before / After Comparison Table

| Measurement | G1 Baseline | ZGC Candidate | Decision |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Throughput req/s |  |  |  |
| p95 latency |  |  |  |
| p99 latency |  |  |  |
| p999 latency |  |  |  |
| Max GC pause |  |  |  |
| GC pause p99 |  |  |  |
| CPU avg |  |  |  |
| CPU p95 |  |  |  |
| CPU throttling |  |  |  |
| Container RSS |  |  |  |
| Heap max |  |  |  |
| Heap used steady-state |  |  |  |
| Allocation rate |  |  |  |
| Allocation stalls |  |  |  |
| Full GC / severe events |  |  |  |
| Error rate |  |  |  |

Decision rule praktis:

```text id="26qrnc"
Use ZGC if p99/p999 improves meaningfully
AND throughput/cost impact is acceptable
AND no allocation stalls appear
AND memory/CPU budget remains safe.
```

---

## Practical Decision Matrix

| Scenario | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| G1 p99 pause 20-50 ms dan SLO longgar | Tetap G1 |
| G1 p99 pause 150-500 ms dan user-facing SLO ketat | Evaluasi ZGC |
| G1 Full GC muncul karena leak | Fix leak dulu |
| G1 pause spike karena large payload | Limit/stream payload dulu |
| Heap small, CPU tight, throughput more important | Tetap G1 / tune app |
| Large heap, strict latency, CPU headroom good | ZGC kandidat kuat |
| Batch job throughput-first | G1/Parallel biasanya lebih cocok |
| File/media service native memory tinggi | Evaluasi hati-hati; ZGC tidak menyelesaikan native memory |
| Camunda worker p99 job delay karena DB lock | ZGC bukan solusi utama |
| API p99 spike correlated dengan GC pause | ZGC bisa layak |

---

## Application-Level Fixes Tetap Penting

ZGC tidak menggantikan engineering discipline.

| Problem aplikasi | Kenapa ZGC tidak cukup | Fix utama |
|---|---|---|
| MyBatis query tanpa limit | Object tetap harus dialokasikan | Pagination/cursor/projection |
| Large JSON materialized | Allocation burst tetap ada | Streaming/request limit |
| Unbounded cache | Live set tetap naik | Bounded cache/TTL |
| Camunda variable besar | Heap/DB pressure tetap ada | Store reference, not payload |
| Excessive exception | CPU/allocation tetap tinggi | Avoid exception for normal flow |
| Too many threads | Native memory/CPU contention tetap | Bound executor |
| DB pool wait | Bukan GC issue | Tune query/pool/DB |
| Downstream slow | ZGC tidak mempercepat network | Timeout/retry/circuit breaker |
| CPU throttling | ZGC bisa makin terasa | Fix K8s CPU budget |

---

## Recommended ZGC Runbook

### Step 1 - Confirm JDK and mode

```bash id="f5tv1c"
java --version
jcmd <pid> VM.flags
```

Cari:

```text id="vgxg0b"
UseZGC
ZGenerational, if Java 21
MaxHeapSize
SoftMaxHeapSize, if used
```

---

### Step 2 - Enable GC log

```bash id="k0osyy"
-Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
```

Atau file:

```bash id="t4du1d"
-Xlog:gc*:file=/var/log/app/zgc.log:time,uptime,level,tags:filecount=5,filesize=50M
```

OpenJDK ZGC wiki quick start juga menyarankan `-XX:+UseZGC -Xmx<size> -Xlog:gc`, dan detailed logging dengan `-Xlog:gc*`. ([wiki.openjdk.org](https://wiki.openjdk.org/spaces/zgc/pages/34668579/Main))

---

### Step 3 - Capture JFR

```bash id="zpcxzr"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=zgc-profile settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/zgc-profile.jfr
```

Lihat:

```text id="u6tdl2"
allocation hotspots
GC events
CPU hotspots
thread states
lock contention
socket/file I/O
exceptions
```

---

### Step 4 - Check container reality

```bash id="uxmb12"
kubectl top pod <pod>
kubectl describe pod <pod>
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- cat /proc/1/status | egrep 'VmRSS|VmSize|Threads'
```

---

### Step 5 - Compare application metrics

```text id="lbmqzw"
p95
p99
p999
throughput
error rate
CPU
RSS
DB pool wait
downstream latency
```

---

## ZGC Production Templates

### Java 21 latency-sensitive API

```bash id="kpzq23"
java \
  -XX:+UseZGC \
  -XX:+ZGenerational \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0 \
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta \
  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8 \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

### Java 25 latency-sensitive API

```bash id="63g0i2"
java \
  -XX:+UseZGC \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0 \
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta \
  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8 \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

### ZGC with soft heap target

```bash id="muzvif"
java \
  -XX:+UseZGC \
  -Xmx5g \
  -XX:SoftMaxHeapSize=4g \
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

### ZGC with uncommit disabled

```bash id="t2otn8"
java \
  -XX:+UseZGC \
  -Xms4g \
  -Xmx4g \
  -XX:-ZUncommit \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

Gunakan pattern ini hanya jika memory dedicated dan latency lebih penting daripada mengembalikan unused memory ke OS.

---

## Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa salah | Yang benar |
|---|---|---|
| Switch ke ZGC karena “lebih modern” | Bisa menurunkan throughput tanpa benefit | Prove GC pause root cause |
| Pakai Java 21 `-XX:+UseZGC` lalu mengira generational | Di JDK 21 perlu `-XX:+ZGenerational` | Validasi mode |
| Pakai `-XX:+ZGenerational` di JDK 25 | Option sudah dihapus/obsolete sejak JDK 24+ | Pakai `-XX:+UseZGC` saja |
| Heap terlalu kecil | ZGC butuh headroom concurrent | Set `Xmx`/percentage aman |
| CPU limit terlalu ketat | Concurrent GC butuh CPU | Beri CPU headroom |
| Tidak cek throughput | ZGC bisa punya throughput cost | Compare req/s/jobs/s |
| Tidak cek allocation stalls | Pause kecil belum tentu latency aman | Baca GC log/JFR |
| Mengabaikan RSS | Heap bukan total memory | Monitor container memory |
| Mengira ZGC fix leak | Reachable objects tetap hidup | Heap dump dan fix retention |
| Mengganti collector dan code sekaligus | Tidak tahu penyebab improvement/regression | One change at a time |

---

| Step | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | ZGC positioning | Bisa menjelaskan kapan ZGC layak dipakai |

| 3 | Generational ZGC model | Paham young/old, barrier, remembered set secara praktis |
| 4 | Heap headroom | Bisa sizing ZGC agar tidak allocation stall |
| 5 | CPU trade-off | Bisa menilai throughput/CPU impact |
| 6 | GC log + JFR | Bisa membandingkan G1 vs ZGC dengan evidence |
| 7 | Kubernetes deployment | Bisa membuat template ZGC aman |

# Seri 9/12 - JVM Observability: GC Log, `-Xlog`, JFR, JMX, Micrometer, Prometheus, Dashboard, Alerting, Evidence Package

## Introduction

Seri ini fokus pada **cara membuat JVM observable di production**.

Untuk senior software engineer, observability JVM bukan hanya “punya dashboard heap”. Yang dibutuhkan adalah kemampuan menjawab cepat saat incident:

```text id="kooyfz"
Apakah latency naik karena GC?
Apakah heap leak atau allocation pressure?
Apakah RSS naik karena native/direct memory?
Apakah request stuck karena lock/thread pool/DB pool?
Apakah CPU tinggi karena JIT, GC, busy loop, JSON mapping, atau agent?
Apakah pod OOMKilled atau JVM yang melempar OutOfMemoryError?
Apakah config JVM yang aktif sama dengan yang kita kira?
```

Oracle mendokumentasikan JFR sebagai tool terintegrasi di JVM untuk mengumpulkan diagnostic/profiling data dengan overhead kecil sehingga bisa dipakai di production; datanya mencakup thread samples, lock profiles, dan garbage collection details. Oracle juga mendokumentasikan `jcmd` sebagai utility untuk mengirim diagnostic command ke JVM, termasuk mengelola JFR dan troubleshooting JVM. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/troubleshoot/diagnostic-tools.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

```text id="7jac41"
JVM Observability Map
│
├─ 1. Runtime Identity
```

## Core Mental Model

```text id="ikdqwf"
JVM observability has 4 layers:

1. Metrics     → continuous signals
2. Logs        → event timeline
3. Dumps       → point-in-time forensic snapshots
4. Profiles    → causal runtime evidence
```

Dashboard metrics memberi tahu **ada masalah**.  
GC log memberi tahu **kapan dan bagaimana GC terjadi**.  
Thread dump memberi tahu **thread sedang stuck di mana**.  
Heap dump memberi tahu **object apa yang menahan memory**.  
JFR memberi tahu **timeline gabungan CPU, allocation, GC, lock, thread, socket/file I/O**.

---

## Tabular Decomposition Map

| # | Category / Skill | Penjelasan praktis | Tool / Artefact | Kapan dipakai | Failure mode yang dikenali |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Runtime identity** | Mengetahui JVM yang benar-benar berjalan: versi Java, vendor, flags, command line, system properties. | `java --version`, `jcmd VM.flags`, `jcmd VM.command_line`, `jcmd VM.system_properties` | Baseline dan incident. | Wiki/Helm bilang satu hal, runtime aktual berbeda. |
| 2 | **JVM unified logging** | `-Xlog` adalah logging framework JVM modern untuk GC, safepoint, class loading, container detection, dan tag JVM lain. | `-Xlog:gc*`, `-Xlog:safepoint`, `-Xlog:os+container` | Semua production JVM. | Tidak ada event timeline saat incident. |
| 3 | **GC logging** | Mencatat GC event, pause, heap before/after, cause, collector behavior. | `-Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags` | Latency/memory/OOM. | GC disalahkan tanpa bukti atau tidak terlihat sama sekali. |
| 4 | **GC log rotation** | GC log ke file harus dibatasi ukuran dan jumlah file. | `filecount`, `filesize` | VM non-container atau log file sidecar. | Disk penuh karena GC log. |
| 5 | **Safepoint logging** | Melihat stop-the-world event yang bukan hanya GC. | `-Xlog:safepoint` | Latency spike tanpa GC besar. | Mengira semua pause berasal dari GC. |
| 6 | **Container detection logging** | Melihat apakah JVM membaca cgroup/container resource dengan benar. | `-Xlog:os+container=info/trace` | Docker/Kubernetes sizing. | JVM sizing berdasarkan asumsi resource yang salah. |
| 7 | **Class loading logging** | Melihat class loading/unloading. | `-Xlog:class+load`, `-Xlog:class+unload` | Startup lambat, classpath, metaspace leak. | Startup scanning besar, classloader leak. |
| 8 | **JFR startup recording** | Merekam startup/warmup tanpa perlu attach manual. | `-XX:StartFlightRecording=...` | Cold start, first request, autoscaling. | Startup lambat tidak punya evidence. |
| 9 | **JFR on-demand recording** | Mengambil profile saat incident tanpa restart. | `jcmd <pid> JFR.start`, `JFR.dump`, `JFR.stop` | CPU tinggi, p99 naik, GC pressure, lock contention. | Incident lewat tanpa profiling data. |
| 10 | **JFR analysis** | Membaca CPU hot methods, allocation hot classes, GC, thread states, locks, socket/file I/O. | JDK Mission Control, `jfr` CLI | Root cause analysis. | Fokus hanya pada heap padahal bottleneck lock/DB. |
| 11 | **JMX** | Standard API untuk monitoring/managing JVM dan aplikasi. Oracle mendokumentasikan JMX sebagai standard API untuk resources seperti aplikasi, service, dan JVM. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/troubleshoot/diagnostic-tools.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | JConsole, JMX exporter, MBeans | Metrics JVM dan custom app. | JMX remote exposure tidak aman. |
| 12 | **Micrometer JVM metrics** | Library metrics yang menyediakan JVM binders untuk classloader, memory, GC, processor, thread, dan deadlock. ([docs.micrometer.io](https://docs.micrometer.io/micrometer/reference/reference/jvm.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | `JvmMemoryMetrics`, `JvmGcMetrics`, `JvmThreadMetrics` | Aplikasi Java yang expose Prometheus/OTLP. | Dashboard heap ada, tetapi thread/GC/deadlock tidak ada. |
| 13 | **Prometheus scraping** | Pull-based metrics collection dari endpoint. | `/metrics`, JMX exporter, Micrometer Prometheus | Kubernetes monitoring. | High-cardinality labels membuat metrics backend berat. |
| 14 | **OpenTelemetry Java** | OpenTelemetry Java menyediakan API/SDK untuk telemetry seperti metrics, logs, dan traces; Java instrumentation ecosystem juga mendukung auto/manual instrumentation. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/languages/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | OTel Java agent, SDK, OTLP exporter | Distributed tracing dan telemetry standard. | Agent overhead, duplicate instrumentation, tag cardinality tinggi. |
| 15 | **JMX Exporter** | Mengubah JMX MBeans menjadi Prometheus metrics. | Java agent / standalone exporter | Legacy/non-Micrometer app. | Export terlalu banyak MBean → cardinality/overhead. |
| 16 | **Thread metrics** | Jumlah thread, daemon, blocked, waiting, deadlock. | JMX, Micrometer, `jcmd Thread.print` | Thread leak, stuck request, pool exhaustion. | Hanya monitor CPU/heap, thread explosion tidak terlihat. |
| 17 | **Thread dump** | Snapshot stack semua thread. | `jcmd Thread.print`, `jstack` | Request stuck, deadlock, CPU high, pool wait. | Satu dump tidak cukup; perlu series. |
| 18 | **Heap metrics** | Heap used/committed/max, young/old usage jika tersedia. | JMX/Micrometer/Prometheus | Memory trend. | Heap metrics normal tetapi RSS naik karena native memory. |
| 19 | **Non-heap metrics** | Metaspace, code cache, compressed class space. | JMX/Micrometer | Classloader/metaspace/code cache issue. | Hanya heap dipantau. |
| 20 | **Buffer pool metrics** | Direct dan mapped buffer memory/count. | JMX BufferPool MBeans | Off-heap/NIO/file processing. | RSS naik, heap normal. |
| 21 | **Native Memory Tracking** | HotSpot diagnostic untuk kategori native memory internal; harus diaktifkan saat startup. | `-XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary`, `jcmd VM.native_memory` | RSS tinggi, heap normal. | NMT belum aktif saat dibutuhkan. |
| 22 | **Class histogram** | Ringkasan jumlah dan shallow size object per class. | `jcmd GC.class_histogram` | Quick heap triage. | Salah tafsir karena bukan retained size. |
| 23 | **Heap dump** | Snapshot object graph untuk retained-size/leak analysis. | `.hprof`, `jcmd GC.heap_dump` | Leak/retention/OOM. | Heap dump besar, sensitif, dan bisa mengganggu service. |
| 24 | **OOM forensic config** | Otomatis menulis heap dump/error file saat Java OOM/fatal error. | `HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError`, `HeapDumpPath`, `ErrorFile` | Production readiness. | Dump gagal karena path tidak writable. |
| 25 | **Container metrics** | RSS/working set, CPU usage, CPU throttling, restart count, OOMKilled. | Kubernetes metrics, cAdvisor, kube-state-metrics | Pod-level truth. | JVM sehat tapi container mati. |
| 26 | **Latency correlation** | Menyelaraskan GC/JFR/thread events dengan p95/p99 request latency. | APM, Prometheus, logs timestamp | Membuktikan root cause. | GC disalahkan padahal DB/downstream. |
| 27 | **DB pool observability** | Active/idle/waiting connections, acquisition time, timeout count. | Hikari/MyBatis/datasource metrics | Jersey/MyBatis apps. | Thread stuck karena DB pool, dikira GC. |
| 28 | **Camunda observability** | Job backlog, failed jobs, incidents, acquisition latency, history growth. | Camunda metrics/tables/custom metrics | Workflow services. | Latency job dikira JVM, padahal job executor/backlog. |
| 29 | **Application structured logs** | Log event dengan request id, trace id, tenant, operation, status, duration. | JSON logs / structured logs | Debug distributed flow. | Log tidak bisa dikorelasikan dengan trace/metrics. |
| 30 | **Trace correlation** | Menghubungkan request path antar service. | OpenTelemetry trace/span id | Microservice distributed issue. | JVM lokal terlihat sehat, downstream lambat. |
| 31 | **Alerting** | Alert berbasis symptom dan saturation, bukan noise. | Prometheus alert rules | Production ops. | Alert heap used tinggi tanpa impact atau terlalu lambat saat OOM. |
| 32 | **Evidence package** | Paket data standar saat incident. | GC log, JFR, dumps, metrics snapshot, pod events | RCA dan postmortem. | Incident selesai tapi tidak ada bukti. |
| 33 | **Redaction/security** | Dumps/logs/JFR bisa mengandung PII/secret. | Access control, retention, redaction | Compliance/security. | Heap dump berisi token/password/customer data. |
| 34 | **Observability cost control** | Metrics/log/profile punya overhead dan cardinality cost. | sampling, retention, label policy | Production scale. | Monitoring backend overload. |

---

## Observability Layer 1 - JVM Logs

### Baseline GC log untuk container

```bash id="bojpyc"
-Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
```

Kenapa ke `stdout`? Di Kubernetes, log collection biasanya mengambil stdout/stderr container.

### GC log ke file dengan rotasi

```bash id="gz8erd"
-Xlog:gc*:file=/var/log/app/gc.log:time,uptime,level,tags:filecount=5,filesize=20M
```

### Investigation mode

```bash id="7qwcuw"
-Xlog:gc*,safepoint,os+container=info:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
```

### Class loading investigation

```bash id="snrh5e"
-Xlog:class+load=info,class+unload=info:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
```

### G1 humongous object investigation

```bash id="d96srh"
-Xlog:gc*,gc+heap=debug,gc+humongous=debug:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
```

**Rule praktis:** aktifkan `-Xlog:gc*` sebagai baseline. Aktifkan `class+load`, `safepoint`, atau `gc+humongous` hanya saat dibutuhkan karena log bisa menjadi noisy.

---

## Observability Layer 2 - JFR

JFR adalah tool paling berguna untuk **runtime timeline**. Oracle menyebut JFR terintegrasi dengan JVM, overhead kecil, dan dapat dipakai di production untuk data seperti thread samples, lock profiles, dan garbage collection details. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/troubleshoot/diagnostic-tools.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Startup JFR

```bash id="6n3tj1"
-XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=/recordings/startup.jfr,duration=120s,settings=profile
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="7kw33c"
startup lambat
first request lambat
class loading besar
JIT warmup
dependency/framework bootstrap
```

### On-demand JFR

```bash id="xus88m"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=incident settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/incident.jfr
```

Atau start lalu dump manual:

```bash id="adg822"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=incident settings=profile
jcmd <pid> JFR.dump name=incident filename=/tmp/incident.jfr
jcmd <pid> JFR.stop name=incident
```

Oracle mendokumentasikan `jcmd` sebagai utility untuk diagnostic command ke JVM dan useful untuk mengelola Flight Recorder recordings. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/troubleshoot/diagnostic-tools.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Apa yang dicari di JFR?

| JFR view / event | Dipakai untuk |
|---|---|
| CPU hot methods | CPU tinggi, expensive method |
| Allocation by class/method | Object churn, GC pressure |
| Garbage Collection | Pause, cycle, heap behavior |
| Thread states | Blocked/waiting/runnable pattern |
| Lock instances | Lock contention |
| Socket I/O | Downstream latency |
| File I/O | File/PDF/Excel/temp bottleneck |
| Exceptions | Exception storm |
| Class loading | Startup/metaspace issue |
| Environment/JVM flags | Runtime identity |

---

## Observability Layer 3 - JMX, Micrometer, Prometheus

### JMX

JMX adalah standard API untuk management dan monitoring resource seperti aplikasi, service, dan JVM. Oracle diagnostic docs menyebut JMX digunakan oleh JMC plugins untuk connect ke JVM. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/troubleshoot/diagnostic-tools.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Common MBeans:

```text id="s6gr01"
java.lang:type=Memory
java.lang:type=GarbageCollector,name=*
java.lang:type=Threading
java.lang:type=ClassLoading
java.lang:type=Compilation
java.nio:type=BufferPool,name=direct
java.nio:type=BufferPool,name=mapped
```

### Micrometer

Micrometer menyediakan JVM binders seperti `JvmMemoryMetrics`, `JvmGcMetrics`, `JvmThreadMetrics`, `JvmThreadDeadlockMetrics`, `ClassLoaderMetrics`, dan `ProcessorMetrics`. ([docs.micrometer.io](https://docs.micrometer.io/micrometer/reference/reference/jvm.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh dependency umum:

```xml id="y434ph"
<dependency>
  <groupId>io.micrometer</groupId>
  <artifactId>micrometer-core</artifactId>
  <version>${micrometer.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

Prometheus registry:

```xml id="2l8jk9"
<dependency>
  <groupId>io.micrometer</groupId>
  <artifactId>micrometer-registry-prometheus</artifactId>
  <version>${micrometer.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

Contoh bind manual untuk non-Spring Jersey app:

```java id="deekk9"
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.MeterRegistry;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.binder.jvm.ClassLoaderMetrics;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.binder.jvm.JvmGcMetrics;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.binder.jvm.JvmMemoryMetrics;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.binder.jvm.JvmThreadDeadlockMetrics;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.binder.jvm.JvmThreadMetrics;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.binder.system.ProcessorMetrics;

public final class MetricsBootstrap {
    public static void bindJvmMetrics(MeterRegistry registry) {
        new ClassLoaderMetrics().bindTo(registry);
        new JvmMemoryMetrics().bindTo(registry);
        new JvmGcMetrics().bindTo(registry);
        new JvmThreadMetrics().bindTo(registry);
        new JvmThreadDeadlockMetrics().bindTo(registry);
        new ProcessorMetrics().bindTo(registry);
    }
}
```

---

## Observability Layer 4 - OpenTelemetry

OpenTelemetry Java documentation describes Java APIs/SDKs for telemetry data such as metrics, logs, and traces, and its Java instrumentation ecosystem supports both manual and auto instrumentation approaches. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/languages/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### OpenTelemetry Java agent pattern

```bash id="62hd0m"
java \
  -javaagent:/otel/opentelemetry-javaagent.jar \
  -Dotel.service.name=order-service \
  -Dotel.exporter.otlp.endpoint=http://otel-collector:4317 \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

Atau via env:

```bash id="qzbyye"
OTEL_SERVICE_NAME=order-service
OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT=http://otel-collector:4317
OTEL_TRACES_EXPORTER=otlp
OTEL_METRICS_EXPORTER=otlp
OTEL_LOGS_EXPORTER=otlp
```

### Yang perlu diperhatikan

| Area | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Auto instrumentation | Bisa menambah overhead dan mengubah timing |
| Duplicate instrumentation | Metrics/traces dobel |
| High-cardinality attributes | Backend metrics/traces mahal |
| Sensitive attributes | PII/secret bocor |
| Sampling | Terlalu rendah kehilangan evidence, terlalu tinggi mahal |
| Agent version drift | Behavior berubah antar deployment |
| Startup overhead | Cold start bisa naik |

---

## JVM Dashboard Minimum

### Panel wajib JVM

| Panel | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| JVM heap used / max | Heap pressure |
| Heap after-GC trend | Live set/leak signal |
| Non-heap / metaspace | Class metadata/classloader issue |
| Code cache | JIT/code cache saturation |
| Direct buffer memory | Off-heap/NIO pressure |
| Mapped buffer memory | mmap/file processing |
| GC pause p50/p95/p99 | Latency impact |
| GC count by type | Young/mixed/full frequency |
| GC time ratio | Throughput loss |
| Allocation rate | Object churn |
| Thread count | Thread leak/executor issue |
| Blocked/waiting thread count | Lock/pool contention |
| Class loaded/unloaded | Classloader/metaspace trend |
| Process CPU | JVM/app CPU |
| Container RSS/working set | Total memory truth |
| CPU throttling | Kubernetes CPU limit problem |
| Pod restart count | Stability |
| OOMKilled events | Container memory failure |

### Panel wajib aplikasi

| Panel | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| HTTP request rate | Traffic context |
| HTTP latency p50/p95/p99/p999 | User impact |
| HTTP error rate by status | Failure visibility |
| DB pool active/idle/waiting | DB bottleneck vs JVM bottleneck |
| DB query latency | MyBatis/PostgreSQL root cause |
| Outbound HTTP latency/error | Downstream dependency |
| Camunda job backlog | Workflow pressure |
| Camunda failed jobs/incidents | Workflow failure |
| Queue depth / consumer lag | Async pressure |
| Business operation duration | Domain-level impact |

---

## Alerting Rules: Practical Signals

| Alert | Trigger idea | Why |
|---|---|---|
| JVM heap after-GC high | after-GC heap > 80-85% max for sustained window | Leak/live set pressure |
| GC pause p99 high | p99 GC pause > service threshold | Latency risk |
| Full GC detected | any Full GC in low-latency service | Severe signal |
| GC time ratio high | GC time > 10-20% sustained | Throughput loss |
| Allocation rate spike | large deviation from baseline | Object churn/regression |
| Metaspace growth | continuous increase without plateau | Classloader/generated class issue |
| Direct buffer growth | direct memory increasing with heap stable | Off-heap risk |
| Thread count growth | monotonic growth or over threshold | Thread leak |
| Deadlock detected | deadlock count > 0 | Immediate incident |
| Container RSS high | RSS > 85-90% limit | OOMKilled risk |
| CPU throttling high | throttling sustained | Latency/GC risk |
| Pod OOMKilled | restart reason OOMKilled | Memory incident |
| DB pool wait high | waiting threads or acquisition timeout | App latency not JVM GC |
| Camunda failed jobs spike | failed jobs/incidents increasing | Workflow reliability issue |

**Rule penting:** alert jangan hanya “heap used tinggi”. Lebih kuat jika dikombinasikan dengan **after-GC heap**, **GC time ratio**, **RSS**, **error rate**, dan **latency impact**.

---

## Evidence Package Saat Incident

Saat incident JVM, kumpulkan data dalam window yang sama, misalnya 5-15 menit sekitar kejadian.

```text id="6uzrpu"
incident-evidence/
├─ metadata.txt
├─ jvm-command-line.txt
├─ jvm-flags.txt
├─ jvm-system-properties-redacted.txt
├─ gc.log
├─ jfr/
│  └─ incident-120s.jfr
├─ thread-dumps/
│  ├─ thread-001.txt
│  ├─ thread-002.txt
│  └─ thread-003.txt
├─ heap/
│  ├─ class-histogram.txt
│  └─ heap.hprof        optional, sensitive
├─ native/
│  └─ nmt-summary.txt   if enabled
├─ k8s/
│  ├─ describe-pod.txt
│  ├─ pod-metrics.txt
│  └─ events.txt
└─ dashboard/
   └─ snapshot-links.txt
```

### Commands

Runtime identity:

```bash id="hojolz"
jcmd <pid> VM.command_line > jvm-command-line.txt
jcmd <pid> VM.flags > jvm-flags.txt
jcmd <pid> VM.system_properties > jvm-system-properties.txt
```

Thread dump series:

```bash id="cywljv"
for i in 1 2 3; do
  jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread-$i.txt
  sleep 10
done
```

Class histogram:

```bash id="6v3mhn"
jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram > class-histogram.txt
```

JFR:

```bash id="l5b19y"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=incident settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/incident.jfr
```

NMT, jika aktif:

```bash id="k1848o"
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory summary scale=MB > nmt-summary.txt
```

Kubernetes evidence:

```bash id="amrw6o"
kubectl describe pod <pod> > describe-pod.txt
kubectl top pod <pod> > pod-metrics.txt
kubectl get events --sort-by=.lastTimestamp > events.txt
```

---

## Incident Diagnosis Matrix

| Symptom | First evidence | Likely direction |
|---|---|---|
| p99 spike with GC pause | GC log + APM timestamp | GC pause root cause likely |
| p99 spike without GC pause | JFR + trace + DB metrics | DB/network/lock/thread issue |
| CPU high | JFR CPU + thread dump | Hot method, busy loop, GC, agent |
| Heap rising | GC log after-GC + heap histogram | Leak/retention |
| Heap stable, RSS rising | NMT + BufferPool + thread count | Native/direct/metaspace/thread |
| Pod restart 137 | `kubectl describe pod` | Container OOMKilled |
| Java OOM | app log + heap dump | Heap/metaspace/direct/native thread OOM |
| Request stuck | thread dump series | Lock, DB pool, downstream wait |
| Startup slow | startup JFR + class loading logs | classpath scanning, framework init, JIT |
| Camunda jobs delayed | Camunda metrics + DB + thread dump | job executor/backlog/DB lock |
| DB pool exhausted | pool metrics + thread dump | query latency/leak/pool sizing |

---

## Production JVM Observability Baseline Template

```bash id="v7oucv"
java \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0 \
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta \
  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8 \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps \
  -XX:ErrorFile=/dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

Investigation variant:

```bash id="20i3ks"
java \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=20.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0 \
  -XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary \
  -Xlog:gc*,safepoint,os+container=info:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=/recordings/startup.jfr,duration=120s,settings=profile \
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps \
  -XX:ErrorFile=/dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log \
  -jar /app/app.jar
```

---

## Security & Compliance Considerations

| Artefact | Risiko | Perlakuan |
|---|---|---|
| Heap dump | Bisa berisi PII, token, password, customer data | Encrypt, restrict access, expire |
| JFR | Bisa berisi path, args, method names, SQL-ish strings, environment info | Treat as sensitive |
| Thread dump | Bisa berisi stack dengan business context | Restrict access |
| System properties | Bisa mengandung secret jika salah desain | Redact before sharing |
| Logs | Bisa mengandung request/customer data | Structured logging + redaction |
| Metrics labels | High-cardinality PII risk | Label allowlist |
| Trace attributes | PII propagation risk | Attribute policy |
| GC logs | Biasanya lebih aman, tapi tetap metadata runtime | Retention policy |

---

## Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa salah | Yang benar |
|---|---|---|
| Dashboard hanya heap | Native/RSS/thread/GC tidak terlihat | JVM + container + app metrics |
| Tidak aktifkan GC log | Hilang timeline GC | `-Xlog:gc*` baseline |
| JFR tidak pernah dicoba sampai incident | Saat incident bingung | Latihan JFR di non-prod |
| Thread dump cuma sekali | Sulit bedakan stuck vs transient | Ambil 3-5 dump berjarak 5-10 detik |
| Alert heap used tanpa after-GC | Banyak false positive | Alert after-GC/live set trend |
| Trace tanpa metrics | Tidak tahu saturation | Gabungkan metrics + traces |
| Metrics high cardinality | Backend mahal/lambat | Batasi label |
| Secret di system properties | Bocor di diagnostics | Secret via file/secret manager/env dengan redaction |
| Heap dump ke disk kecil | Disk penuh / dump gagal | Dedicated volume + quota |
| Observability agent tanpa budget | CPU/RSS naik | Ukur overhead agent |

---

## Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Step | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | JVM identity commands | Bisa membuktikan runtime flags/config aktual |
| 2 | GC logging | Bisa mengaktifkan dan membaca GC timeline |
| 3 | JFR | Bisa merekam dan membaca CPU/allocation/GC/thread evidence |
| 4 | JMX/Micrometer metrics | Bisa expose JVM metrics ke Prometheus |
| 5 | Container correlation | Bisa membandingkan heap vs RSS vs OOMKilled |
| 6 | Thread/heap/native diagnostics | Bisa mengambil dump/histogram/NMT |
| 7 | Dashboard & alerting | Bisa desain panel dan alert yang actionable |
| 8 | Incident evidence package | Bisa mengumpulkan data RCA yang repeatable |

---

## Mini Practice

Untuk satu service Java/Jersey, pastikan Anda bisa menjalankan:

```bash id="na88k9"
jcmd 1 VM.command_line
jcmd 1 VM.flags
jcmd 1 GC.heap_info
jcmd 1 Thread.print
jcmd 1 GC.class_histogram
jcmd 1 JFR.start name=test settings=profile duration=60s filename=/tmp/test.jfr
```

Lalu cek dashboard punya minimal:

| Check | Ada? |
|---|---|
| JVM heap used/max |  |
| Heap after-GC trend |  |
| GC pause p95/p99 |  |
| GC count by type |  |
| Thread count |  |
| Metaspace |  |
| Direct buffer |  |
| Container RSS |  |
| CPU throttling |  |
| HTTP p95/p99 |  |
| Error rate |  |
| DB pool waiting |  |
| Pod restarts/OOMKilled |  |

# Seri 10/12 - JVM Diagnostic Tools: `jcmd`, `jstack`, `jmap`, `jstat`, `jinfo`, Heap Dump, Thread Dump, NMT, Incident Playbook

## Introduction

Seri ini fokus pada **alat diagnosis JVM yang dipakai saat aplikasi Java/Jersey microservice bermasalah di runtime**.

Tujuannya bukan hafal semua command, tetapi tahu **tool mana dipakai untuk gejala apa**, kapan aman dipakai di production, dan evidence apa yang harus dikumpulkan sebelum menyimpulkan root cause.

Oracle JDK 25 mendefinisikan module `jdk.jcmd` sebagai module yang berisi tools diagnostic/troubleshooting seperti `jcmd`, `jinfo`, `jmap`, `jps`, `jstack`, dan `jstat`. Untuk diagnosis modern, Oracle juga menyarankan menggunakan `jcmd` dibanding utility lama seperti `jmap` untuk enhanced diagnostics dan lower performance overhead. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/api/jdk.jcmd/module-summary.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Diagram

```text id="t6o2n8"
JVM Diagnostic Tools Map
│
├─ 1. Process Discovery
│  ├─ jps
│  ├─ ps
│  ├─ pgrep
│  ├─ kubectl exec ps

│
├─ 2. Runtime Identity
│  ├─ jcmd VM.version

│  ├─ jcmd VM.system_properties

│  └─ java --version
│
```
