---

## Core Mental Model

```text id="go3p8s"
Metrics tell you something is wrong.
Logs tell you when it happened.
Dumps tell you what the JVM looked like at one point in time.
JFR tells you the timeline and causal shape.
```

Untuk incident JVM, jangan langsung “tuning”. Ambil evidence dulu:

```text id="e71vdg"
1. What JVM is actually running?
2. What flags are actually active?
3. What are threads doing?
4. What is heap doing?
5. What is native/RSS doing?
6. Is GC involved?
7. Is CPU hot path known?
8. Is the bottleneck inside JVM, DB, network, lock, pool, or container limit?
```

---

## Important Operational Constraint

`jcmd` harus dijalankan di host yang sama dengan JVM target dan dengan effective user/group yang sama dengan process JVM target. Dokumentasi Oracle `jcmd` juga menyatakan setiap diagnostic command punya argumen sendiri, dan syntax command dapat dilihat via `jcmd <pid> help <command>`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/24/docs/specs/man/jcmd.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Di Kubernetes, implikasinya:

```bash id="gcqbl8"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.flags
```

Tetapi ini hanya berhasil jika:

```text id="mhip9y"
1. Image punya tool jcmd.
2. User di container punya permission attach ke process.
3. JVM tidak dibatasi security setting tertentu.
4. PID target benar, sering kali PID 1 di container.
```

---

## Tabular Decomposition Map

| # | Tool / Domain | Fungsi praktis | Command utama | Kapan dipakai | Risiko / batasan |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **`jcmd`** | Swiss-army knife diagnostic modern untuk JVM: flags, command line, heap info, thread dump, JFR, native memory, class histogram. | `jcmd <pid> help` | First-line diagnostic. | Harus same machine/user; tidak selalu ada di minimal runtime image. |
| 2 | **`jps`** | Menemukan Java process dan main class/jar. | `jps -lv` | Cari PID lokal/non-container. | Dalam container kadang hanya ada satu PID; `ps` bisa lebih jelas. |
| 3 | **`jinfo`** | Melihat atau mengubah sebagian VM flags/system properties. | `jinfo <pid>` | Runtime identity/debug legacy. | Banyak kebutuhan modern lebih nyaman via `jcmd`. |
| 4 | **`jstack`** | Mengambil thread dump. | `jstack <pid>` | Deadlock, stuck request, high CPU. | Prefer `jcmd Thread.print` jika tersedia. |
| 5 | **`jmap`** | Memory-related statistics dan heap dump legacy. Oracle menyebut `jmap` bisa print memory-related statistics, tetapi menyarankan `jcmd` untuk enhanced diagnostics dan lower overhead. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/troubleshoot/diagnostic-tools.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | `jmap -histo`, `jmap -dump` | Heap investigation, legacy workflow. | Heap dump bisa stop-the-world/berat. |
| 6 | **`jstat`** | Statistik lightweight GC/class/compiler dari JVM. | `jstat -gcutil <pid> 1s` | Quick GC trend tanpa full GC log. | Output sampling; bukan pengganti GC log/JFR. |
| 7 | **JFR** | Timeline profiling: CPU, allocation, GC, lock, thread, I/O. Oracle menyebut JFR terintegrasi ke JVM dan berguna untuk production diagnostics dengan low overhead. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/troubleshoot/diagnostic-tools.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | `jcmd <pid> JFR.start ...` | CPU tinggi, p99 latency, allocation, lock. | File sensitif; setting detail bisa menambah overhead. |
| 8 | **Heap dump** | Snapshot object graph untuk leak/retention. | `jcmd <pid> GC.heap_dump file.hprof` | Heap OOM/leak. | Bisa besar, STW, mengandung PII/secret. |
| 9 | **Class histogram** | Ringkasan jumlah dan shallow size object per class. | `jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram` | Quick memory triage. | Bukan retained size; jangan jadikan final conclusion. |
| 10 | **Thread dump** | Stack semua thread. | `jcmd <pid> Thread.print` | Stuck, deadlock, pool exhausted. | Satu dump sering tidak cukup; ambil series. |
| 11 | **NMT** | Native Memory Tracking untuk kategori internal native memory HotSpot. Oracle menyebut NMT melacak internal memory HotSpot dan diakses via `jcmd`, tetapi tidak melacak third-party native code/JDK class library allocations. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/vm/native-memory-tracking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | `jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory summary` | RSS tinggi, heap normal. | Harus diaktifkan saat startup; ada overhead. |
| 12 | **`/proc` tools** | OS-level process memory/thread/file descriptor. | `/proc/<pid>/status`, `/proc/<pid>/smaps` | Container RSS/native issue. | Perlu Linux knowledge; data low-level. |
| 13 | **`kubectl describe pod`** | Pod lifecycle, restart reason, OOMKilled, events. | `kubectl describe pod <pod>` | Pod restart/OOMKilled. | Bukan JVM detail; harus dikorelasikan. |
| 14 | **`kubectl top`** | Current CPU/memory pod/node. | `kubectl top pod <pod>` | Resource saturation. | Snapshot; butuh metrics history. |
| 15 | **GC log** | Event timeline GC. | `-Xlog:gc*` | Memory/latency issue. | Harus aktif sejak startup. |
| 16 | **JMC / MAT** | GUI analysis untuk JFR/heap dump. | JDK Mission Control, Eclipse MAT | Offline analysis. | Butuh akses file dan skill interpretasi. |

---

## Diagnostic Tool Priority

Untuk Java 17+ production, urutan praktis:

```text id="84sw04"
1. jcmd
2. JFR
3. GC logs
4. thread dump series
5. class histogram
6. heap dump
7. NMT
8. jstat/jmap/jstack/jinfo legacy/supporting tools
9. OS/Kubernetes evidence
```

**Kenapa `jcmd` dulu?** Karena satu tool bisa melakukan banyak hal: melihat command line, flags, heap info, thread dump, class histogram, JFR, dan native memory. Module `jdk.jcmd` memang mendefinisikan tools diagnostic/troubleshooting JVM termasuk `jcmd`, `jinfo`, `jmap`, `jps`, `jstack`, dan `jstat`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/api/jdk.jcmd/module-summary.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Command Cookbook by Use Case

## 1. Process Discovery

### Local Linux

```bash id="x8fkrk"
jps -lv
ps -ef | grep java
pgrep -af java
```

### Kubernetes

```bash id="527j95"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- ps -ef
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jps -lv
```

Kalau Java process adalah PID 1:

```bash id="ekhf53"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.version
```

---

## 2. Runtime Identity

Gunakan ini sebelum diagnosis apa pun:

```bash id="he19qg"
jcmd <pid> VM.version
jcmd <pid> VM.command_line
jcmd <pid> VM.flags
jcmd <pid> VM.system_properties
```

Untuk Kubernetes:

```bash id="fpgmp4"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.version
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.command_line
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.flags
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.system_properties
```

Yang dicari:

| Evidence | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| `VM.version` | JDK/JVM vendor dan version aktual apa? |
| `VM.command_line` | Argumen JVM benar-benar masuk atau tidak? |
| `VM.flags` | `Xmx`, GC, NMT, JFR, container support aktif? |
| `VM.system_properties` | `user.timezone`, `file.encoding`, config `-D` benar? |

---

## 3. Thread Dump

### Single thread dump

```bash id="ieupz2"
jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread-dump.txt
```

Alternative:

```bash id="ffllr4"
jstack <pid> > thread-dump.txt
```

### Thread dump series

Untuk stuck request/high CPU/deadlock, ambil beberapa dump:

```bash id="6a9av4"
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread-dump-$i.txt
  sleep 10
done
```

Kenapa series? Karena satu dump hanya snapshot. Series menunjukkan apakah thread benar-benar stuck di stack yang sama atau hanya transient.

### Yang dicari di thread dump

| Pattern | Interpretasi |
|---|---|
| Banyak thread `BLOCKED` pada lock sama | Lock contention |
| Banyak thread `WAITING` di pool | Worker idle atau menunggu queue |
| Banyak request thread menunggu DB pool | DB pool exhausted |
| Banyak thread socket read | Downstream lambat |
| Thread runnable di method sama | CPU hotspot / busy loop candidate |
| Deadlock section muncul | Deadlock nyata |
| Thread count sangat tinggi | Thread leak / pool oversizing |

---

## 4. Heap Info & Class Histogram

### Heap summary

```bash id="1dvh5p"
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_info
```

### Class histogram

```bash id="65krsk"
jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram > class-histogram.txt
```

Alternative legacy:

```bash id="rxo8mp"
jmap -histo <pid> > class-histogram.txt
```

Interpretasi class histogram:

| Output besar | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| Banyak `byte[]` | file/JSON/buffer/blob |
| Banyak `char[]` / `String` | text/JSON/logging/cache |
| Banyak DTO/domain object | query besar/cache/retention |
| Banyak `HashMap$Node` | map/cache besar |
| Banyak exception/stack trace | exception storm |
| Banyak proxy/class metadata indirectly | framework/proxy/classloader issue |

Catatan: class histogram menunjukkan **shallow size**, bukan retained size. Untuk leak serius, tetap perlu heap dump + dominator analysis.

---

## 5. Heap Dump

### Manual heap dump

```bash id="b4t044"
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_dump /dumps/heap-$(date +%s).hprof
```

Alternative legacy:

```bash id="3qlk9m"
jmap -dump:format=b,file=/dumps/heap.hprof <pid>
```

### Automatic heap dump saat OOM

```bash id="j4o9pq"
-XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
-XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
```

### Heap dump rules

| Rule | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Jangan ambil heap dump sembarangan di peak traffic | Bisa berat dan memicu pause/disk pressure |
| Pastikan `/dumps` writable dan cukup space | Dump bisa sebesar heap atau lebih |
| Treat heap dump as sensitive | Bisa berisi PII, token, payload, password |
| Ambil histogram dulu jika ragu | Lebih ringan daripada full heap dump |
| Untuk leak, idealnya ambil dump lebih dari satu waktu | Bisa bandingkan growth/retention |

---

## 6. Native Memory Tracking / NMT

NMT dipakai saat:

```text id="g1c97c"
heap normal,
tetapi RSS/container memory naik.
```

Aktifkan saat startup:

```bash id="cecbkr"
-XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary
```

Atau lebih detail:

```bash id="g95a46"
-XX:NativeMemoryTracking=detail
```

Lihat summary:

```bash id="q0lam3"
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory summary scale=MB
```

Baseline dan diff:

```bash id="bsxgbu"
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory baseline
sleep 300
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory summary.diff scale=MB
```

NMT adalah fitur HotSpot untuk tracking internal native memory dan diakses via `jcmd`; batas pentingnya, NMT tidak melacak semua third-party native code allocations dan sebagian JDK class library allocations. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/vm/native-memory-tracking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### NMT categories yang umum

| Category | Arti praktis |
|---|---|
| Java Heap | Heap reserved/committed |
| Class | Metaspace/class metadata |
| Thread | Thread stacks/native thread memory |
| Code | Code cache/JIT compiled code |
| GC | Struktur internal collector |
| Compiler | JIT compiler memory |
| Internal | JVM internal allocations |
| Symbol | Symbol/class metadata internals |
| Native Memory Tracking | Overhead NMT |
| Arena Chunk | Arena allocator memory |

---

## 7. JFR Commands

JFR penting karena menggabungkan banyak evidence dalam satu timeline. JDK 25 menyediakan module `jdk.jfr` untuk API Flight Recorder, dan dokumentasi Java/Oracle menggambarkan JFR sebagai observability/monitoring framework built into HotSpot JVM. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/api/jdk.jfr/module-summary.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Start recording durasi 2 menit

```bash id="aip4tn"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=incident settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/incident.jfr
```

### Start, dump, stop manual

```bash id="724ge2"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=incident settings=profile
sleep 120
jcmd <pid> JFR.dump name=incident filename=/tmp/incident.jfr
jcmd <pid> JFR.stop name=incident
```

### Cek recording aktif

```bash id="rpgaf1"
jcmd <pid> JFR.check
```

### Startup JFR

```bash id="r0900s"
-XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=/recordings/startup.jfr,duration=120s,settings=profile
```

### Yang dicari di JFR

| Problem | JFR view |
|---|---|
| CPU tinggi | Method profiling / hot methods |
| GC pressure | GC events + allocation |
| Object churn | Allocation by class/method |
| Request stuck | Thread states |
| Lock contention | Java monitor blocked / lock events |
| Downstream lambat | Socket read/write |
| File bottleneck | File I/O |
| Exception storm | Exception events |
| Startup lambat | Class loading + execution sample |
| JIT/warmup | Compilation events |

---

## 8. `jstat` Quick GC Sampling

`jstat` berguna untuk quick sampling saat GC log tidak tersedia, meski bukan pengganti GC log/JFR.

### GC utilization setiap 1 detik

```bash id="y11kc5"
jstat -gcutil <pid> 1000
```

### GC capacity

```bash id="89cfsr"
jstat -gccapacity <pid> 1000
```

### GC cause

```bash id="bl1eae"
jstat -gccause <pid> 1000
```

### Class loading

```bash id="l4kd4z"
jstat -class <pid> 1000
```

### Compiler

```bash id="6dpl69"
jstat -compiler <pid> 1000
```

Praktisnya:

| `jstat` output | Gunakan untuk |
|---|---|
| Young GC count naik cepat | Allocation pressure |
| Full GC count naik | Severe memory pressure |
| Old usage naik | Retention/live set |
| Metaspace/class trend | Classloading/metaspace |
| Compiler activity tinggi | Warmup/JIT behavior |

---

## 9. Compiler / Code Cache Diagnostics

### Code cache

```bash id="6s6kx4"
jcmd <pid> Compiler.codecache
```

### Compiler queue

```bash id="tmuy5x"
jcmd <pid> Compiler.queue
```

### Compiled methods list

```bash id="yvkcbr"
jcmd <pid> Compiler.codelist
```

Kapan dipakai:

```text id="78hd9f"
startup/warmup lambat
high CPU saat deploy
code cache full warning
performance berubah setelah running lama
```

---

## 10. Class Loading Diagnostics

### Classloader info

```bash id="cym2xw"
jcmd <pid> VM.classloaders
```

### Classloader stats

```bash id="hlayfp"
jcmd <pid> VM.classloader_stats
```

### Class loading log saat startup

```bash id="hw33sk"
-Xlog:class+load=info,class+unload=info
```

Kapan dipakai:

| Symptom | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| Startup lambat | Classpath scanning besar |
| Metaspace naik terus | Classloader leak |
| `ClassNotFoundException` | Packaging/classpath issue |
| `NoSuchMethodError` | Dependency mismatch |
| Banyak generated class | Proxy/bytecode generation/framework |

---

## Incident Playbook by Symptom

## A. High CPU

```text id="bzg474"
Goal: bedakan CPU karena app hot method, GC, busy loop, lock spin, JIT, atau agent.
```

Commands:

```bash id="mnp12r"
jcmd <pid> VM.command_line
jcmd <pid> VM.flags
jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread-1.txt
sleep 10
jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread-2.txt
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=cpu settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/cpu.jfr
```

OS/container:

```bash id="6qd5sn"
top -H -p <pid>
ps -L -p <pid> -o pid,tid,pcpu,comm
kubectl top pod <pod>
```

Diagnosis:

| Evidence | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| JFR CPU hot method jelas | Code hotspot |
| Banyak GC events + CPU | GC pressure |
| Same thread runnable di same stack | Busy loop |
| CPU throttling tinggi | K8s CPU limit |
| Agent method dominan | APM/security agent overhead |

---

## B. High Memory / RSS Naik

Commands:

```bash id="015x1i"
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_info
jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram > class-histogram.txt
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory summary scale=MB
cat /proc/<pid>/status | egrep 'VmRSS|VmSize|Threads'
```

Kubernetes:

```bash id="guocj7"
kubectl top pod <pod>
kubectl describe pod <pod>
```

Diagnosis:

| Evidence | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| Heap after-GC naik | Leak/retention |
| Heap normal, RSS naik | Native/direct/thread/metaspace |
| Thread count naik | Thread leak |
| NMT Thread besar | Stack/thread overhead |
| NMT Class besar | Metaspace/classloading |
| BufferPool direct besar | Direct buffer issue |
| Pod OOMKilled | Container memory exceeded |

---

## C. Java Heap OOM

Commands / config:

```bash id="p5bsap"
-XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
-XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
-Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
```

If still running:

```bash id="o69bh0"
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_info
jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_dump /dumps/heap-before-crash.hprof
```

Diagnosis flow:

```text id="9wzbmp"
1. Cek GC log before OOM.
2. Lihat heap after-GC trend.
3. Ambil class histogram.
4. Ambil heap dump jika aman.
5. Analisis dominator tree.
6. Cari retained object terbesar.
7. Bedakan leak vs large request/query/batch.
```

---

## D. Pod `OOMKilled` tanpa Java OOM

Commands:

```bash id="qtp8cn"
kubectl describe pod <pod>
kubectl top pod <pod>
kubectl get events --sort-by=.lastTimestamp
```

Inside pod if still alive:

```bash id="nqe4rd"
jcmd 1 VM.flags
jcmd 1 GC.heap_info
cat /proc/1/status | egrep 'VmRSS|VmSize|Threads'
```

Diagnosis:

| Evidence | Interpretasi |
|---|---|
| `Reason: OOMKilled`, exit code 137 | Kernel/cgroup killed process |
| Tidak ada Java `OutOfMemoryError` | Bukan Java-level OOM |
| Heap jauh di bawah max | Native/RSS issue |
| `Xmx` dekat pod limit | Native headroom kurang |
| Thread count tinggi | Thread stack/native issue |
| Direct buffer besar | Off-heap issue |

Action:

```text id="rw2bzt"
Turunkan heap percentage,
naikkan pod memory,
kurangi thread/direct buffer/native usage,
aktifkan NMT di next rollout.
```

---

## E. Request Stuck / Latency Spike

Commands:

```bash id="n044rl"
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread-$i.txt
  sleep 10
done

jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=latency settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/latency.jfr
```

Correlate with:

```text id="n45auc"
HTTP p99
DB pool waiting
DB query latency
downstream latency
GC pause
CPU throttling
Camunda job backlog
```

Diagnosis:

| Thread dump pattern | Root cause candidate |
|---|---|
| Waiting for JDBC connection | DB pool exhausted |
| Socket read | Downstream slow |
| Blocked on monitor | Lock contention |
| Runnable hot stack | CPU-bound code |
| Waiting queue | Worker starvation |
| GC pause timestamp matches spike | GC impact |

---

## F. Deadlock

Command:

```bash id="ehmqxd"
jcmd <pid> Thread.print > deadlock.txt
```

Thread dump biasanya menampilkan deadlock section jika JVM mendeteksi monitor deadlock.

Action:

```text id="xfb1c3"
1. Simpan thread dump lengkap.
2. Identifikasi lock owner dan waiting thread.
3. Map ke code path.
4. Cari lock ordering inversion.
5. Tambahkan timeout atau ubah locking strategy.
6. Buat regression test/concurrency test jika memungkinkan.
```

---

## G. Startup Lambat

Commands / startup args:

```bash id="5q42na"
-XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=/recordings/startup.jfr,duration=180s,settings=profile
-Xlog:class+load=info
-Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
```

Yang dicari:

| Evidence | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| Class loading sangat banyak | Scanning/autodiscovery |
| CPU hot di reflection | Framework bootstrap |
| JIT/compiler activity | Warmup |
| File I/O | Config/resource scanning |
| Socket I/O | Startup dependency call |
| GC banyak saat startup | Allocation burst |

---

## H. Classpath / Dependency Issue

Commands:

```bash id="6q9p4q"
jcmd <pid> VM.command_line
jcmd <pid> VM.system_properties
jar tf app.jar | grep SomeClass
mvn dependency:tree
jdeps --multi-release 17 app.jar
```

Runtime logs:

```bash id="i3lh82"
-Xlog:class+load=info
```

Symptom mapping:

| Error | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| `ClassNotFoundException` | Jar/class tidak ada di runtime |
| `NoClassDefFoundError` | Class ada saat compile, gagal saat runtime/init |
| `NoSuchMethodError` | Versi dependency runtime beda dari compile |
| `VerifyError` | Bytecode/instrumentation/shading issue |
| `IllegalAccessError` | Module/access boundary |
| `ClassCastException` class name sama | Classloader berbeda |

---

## Production Safety Matrix

| Action | Impact | Aman di production? | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| `jcmd VM.flags` | Low | Ya | Read-only |
| `jcmd VM.command_line` | Low | Ya | Hati-hati secret di args |
| `jcmd VM.system_properties` | Low | Ya dengan redaction | Bisa mengandung secret |
| `jcmd Thread.print` | Low-Medium | Ya | Bisa ada sedikit pause |
| Thread dump series | Low-Medium | Ya | Jangan terlalu sering tanpa alasan |
| `jcmd GC.class_histogram` | Medium | Ya, hati-hati | Bisa memicu safepoint |
| `jcmd GC.heap_info` | Low | Ya | Read-only |
| `jcmd GC.heap_dump` | High | Hati-hati | Bisa berat, file besar, sensitif |
| JFR profile 60-120s | Low-Medium | Biasanya ya | Gunakan setting wajar |
| NMT summary | Low-Medium | Ya jika aktif | NMT harus aktif startup |
| NMT detail | Medium | Hati-hati | Lebih overhead |
| `jstat` | Low | Ya | Sampling |
| `jmap` heap dump | High | Hati-hati | Prefer `jcmd` modern |
| `System.gc()` | Medium-High | Hindari | Bisa memicu pause |

---

## Kubernetes Debug Pattern

### Problem: production image tidak punya `jcmd`

Banyak image runtime minimal hanya berisi JRE/custom runtime. Jika `jcmd` tidak ada, opsi:

```text id="yvjm8t"
1. Sediakan debug image variant berbasis JDK.
2. Gunakan ephemeral container dengan tooling jika cluster policy mengizinkan.
3. Build runtime image dengan module/tool diagnostic minimal untuk non-prod.
4. Pastikan baseline logs/JFR/metrics cukup tanpa attach.
```

Cek:

```bash id="hde89l"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- which jcmd
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- ls $JAVA_HOME/bin
```

### PID check

```bash id="5ynzn1"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- ps -ef
```

Jika Java PID adalah 1:

```bash id="ve257z"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.flags
```

---

## Standard Incident Evidence Script

Contoh script Linux sederhana:

```bash id="dy2hlq"
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

PID="${1:-1}"
OUT_DIR="${2:-jvm-evidence-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)}"

mkdir -p "$OUT_DIR/thread" "$OUT_DIR/memory" "$OUT_DIR/jfr"

jcmd "$PID" VM.version > "$OUT_DIR/vm-version.txt" || true
jcmd "$PID" VM.command_line > "$OUT_DIR/vm-command-line.txt" || true
jcmd "$PID" VM.flags > "$OUT_DIR/vm-flags.txt" || true
jcmd "$PID" VM.system_properties > "$OUT_DIR/vm-system-properties.txt" || true
jcmd "$PID" GC.heap_info > "$OUT_DIR/memory/heap-info.txt" || true
jcmd "$PID" GC.class_histogram > "$OUT_DIR/memory/class-histogram.txt" || true

for i in 1 2 3; do
  jcmd "$PID" Thread.print > "$OUT_DIR/thread/thread-$i.txt" || true
  sleep 10
done

jcmd "$PID" VM.native_memory summary scale=MB > "$OUT_DIR/memory/nmt-summary.txt" || true

jcmd "$PID" JFR.start name=incident settings=profile duration=120s filename="$OUT_DIR/jfr/incident.jfr" || true

cat "/proc/$PID/status" > "$OUT_DIR/proc-status.txt" || true

echo "Evidence written to: $OUT_DIR"
```

Catatan: redaksi `vm-system-properties.txt` sebelum dibagikan karena bisa mengandung secret.

---

## Diagnostic Decision Matrix

| Symptom | First commands | Next if confirmed |
|---|---|---|
| High CPU | `Thread.print`, JFR | CPU flame/hot methods |
| High heap | `GC.heap_info`, `GC.class_histogram` | heap dump |
| RSS high, heap normal | NMT, `/proc/status`, BufferPool metrics | native investigation |
| Java OOM | GC log, heap dump, class histogram | MAT/JMC dominator |
| Pod OOMKilled | `kubectl describe`, RSS, flags | adjust heap/native budget |
| Latency spike | GC log + JFR + thread series | correlate DB/downstream/lock |
| Deadlock | `Thread.print` | lock-order fix |
| Startup slow | startup JFR, class loading log | reduce scanning/lazy/eager balance |
| Metaspace OOM | `VM.metaspace`, classloader stats | classloader leak analysis |
| Direct memory OOM | BufferPool/NMT | close buffers/resources, limit direct usage |
| Code cache full | `Compiler.codecache` | review JIT/code cache setting |
| Config drift | `VM.command_line`, `VM.flags`, system props | fix Helm/Docker/systemd |

---

## Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Kenapa berbahaya | Correct approach |
|---|---|---|
| Ambil heap dump dulu untuk semua memory issue | Berat dan belum tentu root cause | Cek heap info, histogram, RSS, NMT dulu |
| Satu thread dump untuk stuck issue | Bisa transient | Ambil 3-5 dump |
| Tidak cek `VM.flags` | Bisa mendiagnosis runtime yang salah | Selalu capture flags |
| Tidak cek pod events | Java log bisa kosong saat OOMKilled | `kubectl describe pod` |
| Share heap dump sembarangan | Bisa ada PII/secret | Treat as sensitive |
| Mengabaikan native memory | Heap normal bukan berarti memory sehat | RSS + NMT + BufferPool |
| JFR terlalu lama/detail di prod | Overhead dan file besar | 60-120 detik profile cukup |
| Tools tidak ada di image | Incident tidak bisa di-debug | Sediakan debug path |
| Fokus JVM saat DB pool exhausted | Root cause salah | Correlate thread dump + DB pool |
| Tuning sebelum evidence | Bisa memperburuk | Capture → hypothesize → change → verify |

---

## Practical Checklist untuk Onboarding Service Baru

| Checklist | Target |
|---|---|
| Apakah image punya `jcmd`? | Bisa attach saat incident |
| Apakah GC log aktif? | Ada timeline GC |
| Apakah heap dump path writable? | OOM forensic siap |
| Apakah JFR bisa dijalankan? | Profiling on-demand siap |
| Apakah NMT bisa diaktifkan di non-prod? | Native/RSS debug siap |
| Apakah dashboard punya heap + RSS? | Bedakan JVM vs container memory |
| Apakah thread count dimonitor? | Detect thread leak/pool issue |
| Apakah DB pool metrics ada? | Hindari salah diagnosis JVM |
| Apakah Camunda metrics/job backlog ada? | Workflow observability |
| Apakah runbook command tersedia? | Incident response cepat |
| Apakah dump/log policy aman? | Security/compliance |

---

## Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Step | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `jcmd` basics | Bisa ambil flags, command line, heap info, thread dump |
| 2 | Thread dump analysis | Bisa baca BLOCKED/WAITING/RUNNABLE/deadlock |
| 3 | Heap histogram & dump | Bisa triage memory dan tahu kapan ambil heap dump |
| 4 | NMT | Bisa debug RSS tinggi dengan heap normal |
| 5 | JFR | Bisa ambil profile 120 detik dan baca CPU/allocation/GC/lock |
| 6 | `jstat`/legacy tools | Bisa pakai sampling cepat |
| 7 | Kubernetes evidence | Bisa bedakan Java OOM vs OOMKilled |
| 8 | Incident package | Bisa mengumpulkan evidence RCA standar |

---

## Mini Practice

Di satu service Java non-prod, jalankan:

```bash id="qyv5lg"
jcmd 1 VM.version
jcmd 1 VM.command_line
jcmd 1 VM.flags
jcmd 1 GC.heap_info
jcmd 1 GC.class_histogram
jcmd 1 Thread.print
jcmd 1 JFR.start name=test settings=profile duration=60s filename=/tmp/test.jfr
```

Jika NMT aktif:

```bash id="yjgqvo"
jcmd 1 VM.native_memory summary scale=MB
```

Lalu jawab:

| Pertanyaan | Jawaban |
|---|---|
| JVM version dan vendor? |  |
| GC aktif apa? |  |
| Max heap berapa? |  |
| GC log aktif? |  |
| Thread count berapa? |  |
| Object class terbesar apa? |  |
| JFR berhasil dibuat? |  |

| Heap dump path writable? |  |
| Tool apa yang tidak tersedia di image? |  |

## Response:
7/14/2026, 12:42:35 AM

# Seri 11/12 - JVM Performance Tuning: JIT, Warmup, Thread Pools, CPU, Allocation, Lock, I/O, DB Pool

## Introduction

Seri ini fokus pada **performance tuning end-to-end** untuk Java/Jersey/JAX-RS microservice. Bedanya dengan seri GC: di sini GC hanya salah satu bagian. Performance issue sering berasal dari kombinasi:

```text id="fgwqxr"
JIT warmup
CPU throttling
thread pool starvation
DB pool exhaustion
blocking I/O
lock contention
high allocation rate
serialization/deserialization overhead
large payload / large query result
JVM flags/config mismatch
APM/security agent overhead
Kubernetes resource limit
```

Oracle mendokumentasikan JFR sebagai tool JVM yang terintegrasi untuk mengumpulkan data diagnostic/profiling seperti thread samples, lock profiles, dan garbage collection details dengan overhead kecil sehingga cocok untuk production diagnostics. Ini penting karena tuning JVM modern harus berbasis evidence, bukan intuisi. ([docs.oracle.com](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/troubleshoot/diagnostic-tools.html))

---

## Diagram

```text id="om51rf"
JVM Performance Tuning Map
│
├─ 1. Runtime Baseline
│  ├─ Java version / vendor
│  ├─ JVM flags
│  ├─ GC collector
│  ├─ heap/native memory
│  ├─ CPU request/limit
│  └─ container resource view
│
├─ 2. Execution Engine
│  ├─ interpreter
│  ├─ tiered compilation
│  ├─ C1 / C2 JIT
│  ├─ profiling counters
│  ├─ optimized native code
│  ├─ deoptimization
│  └─ code cache
│
├─ 3. Warmup & Startup
│  ├─ class loading
│  ├─ framework bootstrap
│  ├─ Jersey resource/provider registration
│  ├─ MyBatis mapper loading
│  ├─ Camunda engine/job executor startup
│  ├─ JIT warmup
│  └─ first-request lazy initialization
│
├─ 4. CPU Performance
│  ├─ hot methods
│  ├─ busy loops
│  ├─ CPU throttling
│  ├─ GC CPU
│  ├─ serialization CPU
│  ├─ crypto/compression CPU
│  └─ agent overhead
│
├─ 5. Concurrency & Threading
│  ├─ request thread pool
│  ├─ DB connection pool
│  ├─ HTTP client pool
│  ├─ Camunda job executor
│  ├─ async executor
│  ├─ scheduler
│  ├─ lock contention
│  └─ deadlock/starvation
│
├─ 6. Allocation & Memory Pressure
│  ├─ object churn
│  ├─ DTO mapping
│  ├─ JSON/XML parsing
│  ├─ exception allocation
│  ├─ large byte[] / String
│  ├─ cache retention
│  └─ GC pressure
│
├─ 7. I/O & External Dependencies
│  ├─ PostgreSQL query latency
│  ├─ DB pool wait
│  ├─ HTTP downstream latency
│  ├─ file I/O
│  ├─ network timeout
│  ├─ retry storm
│  └─ backpressure
│
├─ 8. Measurement
│  ├─ JFR
│  ├─ GC logs
│  ├─ thread dumps
│  ├─ heap histogram/dump
│  ├─ Prometheus/Micrometer
│  ├─ APM traces
│  ├─ load tests
│  └─ benchmark discipline
│
└─ 9. Tuning Loop
   ├─ define SLO
   ├─ baseline
   ├─ identify bottleneck
   ├─ form hypothesis
   ├─ change one variable
   ├─ run same workload
   ├─ compare
   └─ document / rollback
```

---

## Core Mental Model

```text id="vbz2gd"
Performance tuning is not JVM flag tuning.

Performance tuning =
  workload understanding
+ bottleneck identification
+ runtime evidence
+ one-change experiment
+ before/after comparison
+ rollback discipline
```

Jangan mulai dari:

```text id="uzdik5"
-XX random flags
collector switch
heap increase
thread pool increase
DB pool increase
```

Mulai dari:

```text id="ine5ri"
Where is time actually spent?
Where is CPU actually spent?
Where are threads waiting?
Where are objects allocated?
Where is memory retained?
Where is queueing happening?
```

---

## Tabular Decomposition Map

| # | Category / Skill | Penjelasan praktis | Tool / Evidence | Kapan dipakai | Failure mode |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Performance goal definition** | Tentukan target: throughput, p95/p99 latency, startup time, memory footprint, job completion time. | SLO/SLA, dashboard | Sebelum tuning. | Optimasi metrik yang salah. |
| 2 | **Baseline measurement** | Ambil kondisi awal sebelum perubahan. | APM, Prometheus, GC log, JFR | Semua tuning. | Tidak tahu perubahan membaik atau memburuk. |
| 3 | **JFR profiling** | Timeline CPU, allocation, GC, lock, thread, file/socket I/O. | `jcmd JFR.start` | Root-cause runtime. | Menebak bottleneck dari log aplikasi saja. |
| 4 | **JIT warmup** | JVM mengoptimasi hot code berdasarkan runtime profiling. | JFR compiler events, latency timeline | Startup/first request/autoscaling. | Load test tanpa warmup menghasilkan angka misleading. |
| 5 | **Tiered compilation** | JVM dapat menjalankan interpreter dan beberapa level JIT untuk balance startup/peak performance. | `TieredCompilation`, JFR | Startup vs steady-state. | Mengukur performance sebelum steady-state. |
| 6 | **Code cache** | JIT compiled code disimpan di code cache. Oracle `java` command docs mencatat default max code cache 240 MB, dan 48 MB jika tiered compilation dimatikan. ([docs.oracle.com](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/specs/man/java.html)) | `jcmd Compiler.codecache` | Warning code cache full, performance turun. | JIT tidak bisa compile hot methods. |
| 7 | **CPU hotspot identification** | Cari method yang menghabiskan CPU. | JFR method profiling, async profiler jika tersedia | CPU tinggi. | Optimasi bagian kode yang bukan bottleneck. |
| 8 | **CPU throttling awareness** | Di Kubernetes, CPU limit bisa membuat app/GC/JIT lambat. | container CPU throttling metrics | Latency naik padahal CPU app “normal”. | JVM tuning tidak membantu karena CPU dibatasi. |
| 9 | **GC CPU cost** | GC memakai CPU; allocation tinggi bisa membuat CPU habis di GC. | GC logs, JFR GC, CPU metrics | High CPU + memory pressure. | Mengira CPU dipakai business logic. |
| 10 | **Allocation profiling** | Cari class/method yang membuat object paling banyak. | JFR allocation events | GC pressure, p99 noise. | Naikkan heap padahal object churn bisa dikurangi. |
| 11 | **DTO/mapping cost** | Mapping layer bisa menciptakan banyak object. | JFR allocation, CPU hot methods | Jersey/MyBatis/MapStruct/manual mapper. | Terlalu banyak copy antar layer. |
| 12 | **Serialization cost** | JSON/XML processing bisa CPU-heavy dan allocation-heavy. | JFR CPU/allocation, payload metrics | REST API high traffic. | Large payload membuat GC/CPU naik. |
| 13 | **Exception cost** | Exception stack trace mahal jika dipakai untuk normal flow. | JFR exception events | High CPU/allocation, noisy logs. | Exception storm. |
| 14 | **Thread pool sizing** | Thread pool harus sesuai CPU, blocking behavior, queueing, dan downstream capacity. | thread dump, pool metrics | Request stuck/latency. | Terlalu kecil → starvation; terlalu besar → context switching/memory. |
| 15 | **DB pool sizing** | DB pool bukan semakin besar semakin baik. Harus sesuai DB capacity dan request concurrency. | active/idle/waiting/acquisition time | MyBatis/PostgreSQL bottleneck. | DB pool exhausted atau DB overload. |
| 16 | **HTTP client pool tuning** | Outbound connection pool dan timeout harus eksplisit. | client metrics, socket events, traces | Downstream dependency. | Thread stuck di socket read. |
| 17 | **Lock contention** | Thread saling menunggu lock. | JFR lock events, thread dump | High latency with blocked threads. | Banyak request blocked pada monitor sama. |
| 18 | **Deadlock detection** | Dua atau lebih thread saling menunggu lock. | `jcmd Thread.print` | Request stuck total/partial. | Service tidak progress. |
| 19 | **Queueing & backpressure** | Latency bisa berasal dari queue sebelum work dieksekusi. | pool queue depth, executor metrics | Burst traffic. | Menambah thread tanpa kontrol memperburuk overload. |
| 20 | **Blocking I/O** | Request thread menunggu DB/network/file. | thread dump, JFR socket/file I/O, traces | Latency issue. | Salah dikira CPU/JVM issue. |
| 21 | **MyBatis query performance** | SQL, result size, mapper, fetch size, transaction boundary. | DB query metrics, slow query log, JFR allocation | PostgreSQL/MyBatis. | Query besar memori dan latency tinggi. |
| 22 | **Camunda worker performance** | Job executor, retries, acquisition, DB lock, external task worker. | job backlog, failed jobs, thread dump | Workflow service. | JVM dituning padahal job backlog/DB lock root cause. |
| 23 | **Cache performance** | Cache bisa menurunkan latency tetapi menaikkan memory/live set. | hit rate, size, eviction, heap dump | Read-heavy API. | Unbounded cache → old gen pressure. |
| 24 | **Startup performance** | Class loading, framework scanning, dependency init, JIT. | startup JFR, class loading log | Autoscaling/cold start. | First request lambat karena lazy init. |
| 25 | **Benchmark discipline** | JMH untuk microbenchmark, load test untuk service behavior. OpenJDK JMH project mendeskripsikan JMH sebagai harness untuk benchmark nano/micro/milli/macro JVM. ([openjdk.org](https://openjdk.org/projects/code-tools/jmh/)) | JMH, load test, statistical comparison | Code-level optimization. | Microbenchmark tidak representatif. |
| 26 | **Microbenchmark caveat** | Microbenchmark bisa misleading jika profil JIT/konteks tidak seperti aplikasi nyata. Paper 2026 tentang misleading JVM microbenchmarks menekankan konteks eksekusi dan profil JIT dapat membuat hasil isolasi tidak representatif. ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23570)) | Compare with app-level load test | Library/algorithm choice. | Optimasi microbenchmark memperburuk production. |
| 27 | **APM/agent overhead** | Agent bisa menambah CPU, allocation, class loading, code cache, startup time. | before/after JFR, CPU/RSS | Setelah agent ditambahkan. | Agent dianggap netral padahal overhead nyata. |
| 28 | **One-variable experiment** | Ubah satu hal per eksperimen. | change log, before/after table | Semua tuning. | Tidak tahu perubahan mana yang berdampak. |
| 29 | **Regression guardrail** | Tuning satu metrik bisa merusak metrik lain. | p99, throughput, CPU, RSS, error rate | CI/performance testing. | p99 membaik tapi throughput drop 30%. |
| 30 | **Rollback readiness** | Semua perubahan tuning harus bisa dikembalikan. | Helm values, feature flag, config version | Production rollout. | Tidak bisa rollback saat regresi. |

---

## Performance Taxonomy: Symptom → Possible Root Cause

| Symptom | Kemungkinan root cause | Evidence pertama |
|---|---|---|
| p99 latency naik | GC pause, DB pool wait, downstream slow, lock contention, CPU throttling | APM + GC log + thread dump |
| CPU tinggi | hot method, GC, serialization, crypto, compression, busy loop, agent overhead | JFR CPU |
| Memory naik | heap leak, allocation pressure, native/direct, thread explosion, metaspace | heap metrics + RSS + NMT |
| Throughput turun | CPU saturated, DB bottleneck, GC time, lock contention, pool starvation | dashboard + JFR |
| First request lambat | lazy init, class loading, JIT warmup, DB connection init | startup JFR |
| Startup lambat | classpath scanning, dependency injection, mapper loading, Camunda bootstrap | startup JFR + class load log |
| Request stuck | blocked thread, DB pool exhausted, socket read, deadlock | thread dump series |
| GC sering | allocation rate tinggi, heap kecil, payload/query besar | GC log + JFR allocation |
| Full GC | live set besar, leak, humongous object, heap headroom kurang | GC log + heap dump |
| Pod OOMKilled | RSS > cgroup limit | `kubectl describe pod` |
| DB query lambat | missing index, large result, lock, bad plan | DB slow query + trace |
| Camunda job delay | job executor config, DB lock, retries, backlog | Camunda metrics + DB metrics |

---

## JIT, Warmup, dan Code Cache

### Mental model

```text id="xvq2g8"
Java method starts interpreted
        ↓
JVM profiles execution
        ↓
Hot methods are compiled by JIT
        ↓
Optimized native code is used
        ↓
If assumptions break, code can deoptimize
```

Implikasi untuk microservice:

```text id="9c9b03"
1. Latency saat startup berbeda dari steady-state.
2. First request bisa lambat karena class loading + JIT + lazy init.
3. Load test harus punya warmup phase.
4. Autoscaling bisa terlihat buruk jika pod menerima traffic sebelum warm.
5. Code path yang jarang dipakai mungkin tetap dingin.
```

### Commands

```bash id="2m1teo"
jcmd <pid> Compiler.codecache
jcmd <pid> Compiler.queue
```

Optional diagnostic, jangan aktifkan sembarangan di prod karena noisy:

```bash id="yqzjwg"
-XX:+PrintCompilation
```

`java` command docs juga mencantumkan opsi compile command dan compiler-related options, tetapi biasanya JIT tuning low-level bukan first-line tuning untuk microservice biasa. ([docs.oracle.com](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/specs/man/java.html))

### Warmup checklist

| Check | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Warmup endpoint tersedia? | Trigger Jersey resources/providers |
| DB pool pre-initialized? | Hindari first DB request lambat |
| MyBatis mapper loaded? | Hindari lazy mapper penalty |
| JSON serializer initialized? | Hindari first serialization spike |
| Camunda engine ready? | Hindari first job penalty |
| Health/readiness gate benar? | Pod tidak menerima traffic terlalu cepat |
| Load test punya warmup phase? | Pisahkan cold vs steady-state |

---

## CPU Tuning

### Jangan langsung tambah CPU

Pertama jawab:

```text id="b7gq0y"
CPU dipakai untuk apa?
```

Evidence:

```bash id="gj2yuw"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=cpu settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/cpu.jfr
jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread.txt
kubectl top pod <pod>
```

Jika di Linux:

```bash id="8sp1j5"
top -H -p <pid>
ps -L -p <pid> -o pid,tid,pcpu,comm
```

### CPU root cause map

| Evidence | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| JFR hot method di JSON serializer | Serialization CPU |
| JFR hot method di mapper/copy | DTO mapping cost |
| JFR hot method di regex | Regex expensive |
| CPU tinggi + GC frequent | Allocation/GC pressure |
| Thread runnable same loop | Busy loop |
| CPU throttling tinggi | K8s CPU limit |
| Agent methods dominan | APM/security agent overhead |
| Crypto/compression hot | TLS/compression/file workload |
| DB wait tinggi tapi CPU rendah | I/O bottleneck, bukan CPU |

### K8s CPU caution

CPU limit yang terlalu ketat bisa membuat:

```text id="p2c860"
GC concurrent work lambat
JIT compilation lambat
request processing lambat
p99 naik walau heap aman
```

Jangan hanya lihat average CPU. Lihat throttling.

---

## Thread Pool & Concurrency Tuning

### Thread pool bukan angka ajaib

Rule salah:

```text id="2rffti"
more threads = more throughput
```

Rule yang benar:

```text id="j0xsi5"
Throughput improves only if bottleneck has capacity
and extra concurrency does not cause queueing, lock contention, DB overload, memory pressure, or CPU context switching.
```

### Thread pool categories

| Pool | Contoh | Tuning risk |
|---|---|---|
| HTTP request pool | Jersey/container server | Terlalu besar → memory/context switching |
| DB connection pool | PostgreSQL/MyBatis | Terlalu besar → DB overload |
| HTTP client pool | downstream REST | Terlalu kecil → wait; terlalu besar → downstream overload |
| Camunda job executor | BPMN async jobs | Over-acquisition, DB lock, retry storm |
| Scheduler pool | periodic jobs | Overlap job |
| Async executor | background processing | queue growth, lost backpressure |
| ForkJoin/common pool | parallel operations | starvation jika blocking |

### Thread dump patterns

| Pattern | Diagnosis |
|---|---|
| Banyak `WAITING` di executor queue | Worker idle atau queue kosong |
| Banyak request thread menunggu DB connection | DB pool bottleneck |
| Banyak thread `BLOCKED` same monitor | Lock contention |
| Banyak socket read | Downstream slow |
| Banyak runnable CPU-heavy | CPU-bound |
| Thread count naik terus | Thread leak |

### Practical pool sizing rule

| Workload | Direction |
|---|---|
| CPU-bound | Thread count sekitar CPU cores / quota |
| Blocking I/O | Lebih banyak thread bisa berguna, tetapi harus bounded |
| DB-bound | Dibatasi DB pool dan DB capacity |
| Downstream-bound | Dibatasi outbound pool, timeout, circuit breaker |
| Workflow worker | Dibatasi job cost, DB lock, retry, SLA |
| Mixed workload | Pisahkan pool per workload class |

---

## DB Pool & MyBatis Performance

Untuk Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL service, bottleneck sering bukan JVM, melainkan DB query atau DB pool.

### Signals

| Signal | Meaning |
|---|---|
| DB pool active=max | Pool penuh |
| DB pool waiting > 0 | Request menunggu connection |
| Acquisition timeout | Pool starvation |
| Query latency p95 naik | DB/query/index issue |
| Thread dump waiting for connection | App blocked before query |
| Large result allocation | Query result terlalu besar |
| GC pressure after query | Result materialization cost |

### Common anti-patterns

```text id="8tkh25"
SELECT tanpa LIMIT
fetch semua row lalu filter di Java
N+1 query
mapper result object terlalu besar
transaction terlalu lama
DB connection ditahan saat remote call
pool size lebih besar dari DB capacity
timeout terlalu panjang
```

### Tuning direction

| Problem | Fix direction |
|---|---|
| Query lambat | index, query rewrite, explain plan |
| Result terlalu besar | pagination, cursor, projection |
| Pool exhausted | fix slow query/leak; baru adjust pool |
| Connection leak | leak detection, try-with-resources/transaction boundary |
| Transaction lama | shorten transaction scope |
| DB overloaded | reduce concurrency/backpressure |

---

## Allocation & Object Churn

### High allocation sources di Java/Jersey

| Source | Contoh |
|---|---|
| JSON parsing | request body → DTO |
| JSON serialization | DTO → response |
| XML processing | JAXB/FasterXML XML |
| MyBatis mapping | ResultSet → object graph |
| MapStruct/manual mapping | domain ↔ DTO ↔ entity |
| Validation | violation objects |
| Exceptions | stack traces |
| Logging | string building / MDC / structured fields |
| Large file | byte[], String, buffer |
| Camunda variable serialization | process variable object |

### Evidence

```bash id="ri6uk3"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=alloc settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/alloc.jfr
```

Lihat di JFR:

```text id="t3n3x0"
Allocation by class
Allocation by method
TLAB allocations
Outside TLAB allocations
Object allocation in new TLAB
Object allocation outside TLAB
```

### Fix direction

| Pattern | Fix |
|---|---|
| Large `byte[]` | streaming/chunking/request limit |
| Banyak DTO copy | reduce mapping layers on hot path |
| Banyak `String` | avoid repeated formatting/parsing |
| Banyak exception | avoid exception for normal flow |
| Large `List` | pagination/cursor |
| Cache object besar | max weight/TTL/eviction |
| Repeated parsing | cache parsed immutable config |

---

## Lock Contention

### Symptoms

```text id="my0wdc"
CPU tidak terlalu tinggi
latency tinggi
thread banyak BLOCKED
throughput turun
```

### Evidence

```bash id="88byuh"
jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread.txt
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=locks settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/locks.jfr
```

### Common sources

| Source | Example |
|---|---|
| synchronized cache | shared mutable map |
| singleton lazy init | synchronized getter |
| global registry | provider/mapper registry |
| logging appender | blocking sync appender |
| connection/resource pool | lock around checkout |
| rate limiter | shared counter |
| Camunda/job coordination | DB-level or JVM-level contention |

### Fix direction

```text id="gyuffw"
reduce lock scope
use concurrent data structures carefully
avoid global locks on request path
split lock per key/tenant
move blocking I/O outside lock
use immutable snapshot
measure after change
```

---

## I/O Bottleneck

Thread waiting is not always bad. For I/O services, threads often wait. The question is whether waiting is bounded and expected.

### Thread dump clues

| Stack | Meaning |
|---|---|
| JDBC driver read | Waiting DB |
| HTTP client socket read | Waiting downstream |
| FileInputStream/FileChannel | File I/O |
| SSL/TLS stack | TLS/network/crypto |
| pool acquire | Waiting for pool resource |

### Required metrics

| Metric | Why |
|---|---|
| DB query p95/p99 | DB bottleneck |
| DB pool wait time | Pool saturation |
| Outbound HTTP p95/p99 | Downstream bottleneck |
| Timeout count | Reliability issue |
| Retry count | Retry storm |
| Queue depth | Backpressure |
| File I/O duration | File bottleneck |

### Tuning direction

```text id="bfu32k"
set explicit timeouts
bound retries
add circuit breaker/backpressure
right-size pools
avoid holding DB connection during remote call
stream large files
separate slow workload pools
```

---

## JMH vs Load Test

### JMH use case

JMH cocok untuk microbenchmark:

```text id="ysxnah"
algorithm comparison
serialization library micro-test
mapper implementation comparison
collection/data structure comparison
regex/parser comparison
small hot method optimization
```

OpenJDK JMH project mendeskripsikan JMH sebagai harness untuk membangun, menjalankan, dan menganalisis benchmark nano/micro/milli/macro untuk JVM. ([openjdk.org](https://openjdk.org/projects/code-tools/jmh/))

### Load test use case

Load test wajib untuk:

```text id="trr1sn"
HTTP endpoint latency
DB pool behavior
GC pressure under traffic
thread pool saturation
downstream timeout/retry
Camunda job throughput
container CPU/memory behavior
```

### Caveat penting

Microbenchmark bisa misleading karena JVM mengoptimasi berdasarkan profil runtime. Paper 2026 tentang misleading microbenchmarks pada JVM menekankan bahwa meskipun JMH mengatasi banyak pitfall, hasil isolated benchmark tetap bisa tidak representatif karena konteks eksekusi dan profil JIT berbeda dari aplikasi nyata. ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23570))

Praktisnya:

```text id="2tmelv"
Use JMH to compare local code choices.
Use load tests to decide production performance.
```

---

## Performance Tuning Workflow

```text id="l4wg6v"
1. Define target
   ├─ p95 / p99 latency
   ├─ throughput
   ├─ CPU budget
   ├─ memory budget
   ├─ error rate
   └─ job completion time

2. Capture baseline
   ├─ traffic profile
   ├─ JVM flags
   ├─ GC log
   ├─ JFR
   ├─ thread dump
   ├─ heap/RSS
   ├─ DB pool metrics
   └─ app traces

3. Identify bottleneck
   ├─ CPU?
   ├─ GC?
   ├─ lock?
   ├─ DB?
   ├─ network?
   ├─ queue?
   ├─ memory?
   └─ startup?

4. Form hypothesis
   └─ Example: p99 caused by DB pool wait, not GC.

5. Change one variable
   ├─ code
   ├─ pool size
   ├─ timeout
   ├─ query
   ├─ heap
   ├─ GC flag
   └─ CPU/memory request

6. Run same workload
   └─ same data, traffic, duration, warmup, environment

7. Compare
   ├─ target metric improved?
   ├─ guardrails stable?
   ├─ no new bottleneck?
   └─ cost acceptable?

8. Document
   ├─ before/after
   ├─ reasoning
   ├─ rollback
   └─ owner
```

---

## Production Tuning Playbooks

## A. p99 Latency High

Evidence:

```bash id="kfndw1"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=p99 settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/p99.jfr
jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread-1.txt
sleep 10
jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread-2.txt
```

Check:

```text id="eexs9m"
GC pause correlate?
DB pool wait?
Downstream socket read?
Blocked locks?
CPU throttling?
Request queueing?
Large allocation?
```

Action examples:

| Root cause | Action |
|---|---|
| GC pause | reduce allocation, tune heap/GC |
| DB pool wait | fix query/leak/pool, reduce transaction scope |
| Downstream slow | timeout/circuit breaker/retry budget |
| Lock contention | reduce lock scope |
| CPU throttling | adjust CPU request/limit |
| Queueing | backpressure/pool split |

---

## B. Throughput Low

Check:

```text id="g7q5sw"
CPU saturated?
DB saturated?
GC time high?
Thread pool exhausted?
Lock contention?
Downstream bottleneck?
```

Evidence:

```bash id="4jo20i"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=throughput settings=profile duration=180s filename=/tmp/throughput.jfr
jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread.txt
```

Action examples:

| Root cause | Action |
|---|---|
| CPU-bound hot method | optimize code/algorithm/serialization |
| DB-bound | query/index/pagination/pool |
| GC-bound | reduce allocation/increase heap |
| lock-bound | reduce contention |
| network-bound | pool/timeout/backpressure |
| thread starvation | pool sizing/separation |

---

## C. High Allocation / GC Pressure

Evidence:

```bash id="rvrmb7"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=alloc settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/alloc.jfr
```

Check:

```text id="6tyqae"
top allocating class
top allocating method
large byte[] / String
DTO mapping chain
JSON/XML parser
exceptions
large query result
```

Action:

```text id="7dcpz4"
limit payload/result
stream/chunk
reduce mapping copies
avoid exception normal flow
cache immutable computed values
adjust heap only after reducing waste
```

---

## D. Startup / Cold Start Slow

Evidence:

```bash id="kb9b4n"
-XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=/recordings/startup.jfr,duration=180s,settings=profile
-Xlog:class+load=info
```

Check:

```text id="64kh95"
class loading volume
Jersey package scanning
MyBatis mapper init
Camunda engine bootstrap
DB connection init
JIT compilation
first request lazy path
```

Action:

```text id="vfke8a"
reduce classpath scanning
explicit provider/resource registration
warmup critical endpoint
pre-initialize DB pool carefully
use readiness gate
consider CDS/AppCDS later
```

---

## E. DB Pool Exhaustion

Evidence:

```text id="1mfqgj"
DB pool active=max
waiting threads > 0
acquisition timeout
thread dump waiting for connection
```

Thread dump may show request threads waiting around datasource/pool acquire.

Action:

```text id="i43l8w"
find slow queries
check connection leak
shorten transactions
avoid remote calls while holding DB connection
right-size pool with DB capacity
add backpressure
```

Do not blindly double DB pool. That can move the bottleneck from app to PostgreSQL.

---

## JVM Flags: Performance-Relevant but Use Carefully

| Flag | Use | Caution |
|---|---|---|
| `-XX:ActiveProcessorCount=N` | Override CPU count seen by JVM | Useful in container testing; wrong value hurts GC/thread ergonomics |
| `-XX:+UseG1GC` | Balanced collector | Default often, verify |
| `-XX:+UseZGC` | Low-latency collector | Needs CPU/heap headroom |
| `-XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=N` | G1 pause target | Soft goal; too low can increase overhead |
| `-Xms`, `-Xmx` | Heap sizing | Must respect container memory |
| `-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=N` | Container heap sizing | Reserve native headroom |
| `-Xss` | Thread stack size | Too low can StackOverflow |
| `-XX:ReservedCodeCacheSize=N` | Code cache size | Tune only if code cache pressure proven |
| `-XX:StartFlightRecording=...` | Startup profiling | Manage file/overhead |
| `-XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary` | Native memory debug | Overhead, needs startup flag |
| `-Xlog:gc*` | GC observability | Baseline recommended |

---

## Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa salah | Yang benar |
|---|---|---|
| Tuning JVM flag sebelum tahu bottleneck | Bisa memperburuk | JFR/metrics/thread dump dulu |
| Hanya lihat average latency | Tail latency yang rusak | p95/p99/p999 |
| Load test tanpa warmup | Mencampur startup dan steady-state | Pisahkan warmup dan measurement |
| Naikkan thread pool untuk semua latency issue | Bisa overload DB/downstream | Cari wait point |
| Naikkan DB pool tanpa DB analysis | Bisa overload PostgreSQL | Slow query, pool wait, capacity |
| Mengandalkan JMH untuk endpoint | Tidak mencakup DB/network/pool/GC | Load test service |
| Menyimpulkan dari satu run | Noise tinggi | Repeat/statistical comparison |
| Mengabaikan CPU throttling | JVM terlihat lambat karena quota | Monitor throttling |
| Mengabaikan agent overhead | APM/security bisa signifikan | Before/after with agent |
| Optimasi code tanpa guardrail | Bisa merusak readability/correctness | Measure and review |

---

## Senior-Level Review Checklist

| Question | Expected evidence |
|---|---|
| Apa SLO performance service ini? | p95/p99, throughput, job SLA |
| Bottleneck utama saat load test apa? | JFR/trace/metrics |
| Sudah ada warmup phase? | Load test config |
| GC pause correlate dengan latency? | GC log + APM |
| CPU throttling ada? | K8s metrics |
| Allocation hotspot apa? | JFR allocation |
| Thread pool mana yang saturate? | pool metrics/thread dump |
| DB pool waiting ada? | datasource metrics |
| Query terbesar/lambat apa? | DB slow query/trace |
| Ada lock contention? | JFR/thread dump |
| Heap/RSS aman? | JVM + container metrics |
| Throughput vs latency trade-off diterima? | before/after table |
| Rollback tersedia? | config/Helm version |

---

## Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Step | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Performance SLO | Bisa menentukan target p95/p99/throughput/memory |
| 2 | JFR profiling | Bisa menemukan CPU/allocation/lock/I/O bottleneck |
| 3 | Warmup & JIT | Bisa bedakan cold-start dan steady-state |
| 4 | Thread/DB pool analysis | Bisa mengenali starvation dan pool wait |
| 5 | Allocation tuning | Bisa mengurangi object churn |
| 6 | I/O and timeout tuning | Bisa mengontrol blocking latency |
| 7 | Load test discipline | Bisa menjalankan repeatable benchmark |
| 8 | Production experiment | Bisa membuat before/after + rollback |

---

## Mini Practice

Ambil satu endpoint kritikal, lalu buat eksperimen:

```text id="oiu51u"
1. 5 menit warmup
2. 15 menit steady-state load
3. Capture JFR 120 detik di tengah test
4. Capture GC log sepanjang test
5. Capture p95/p99, throughput, CPU, RSS, DB pool
6. Ubah satu hal
7. Ulangi test yang sama
8. Compare
```

Commands:

```bash id="gh9yfd"
jcmd <pid> JFR.start name=perf settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/perf.jfr
jcmd <pid> VM.flags
jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread.txt
jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram > histogram.txt
```

Isi:

| Pertanyaan | Jawaban |
|---|---|
| Endpoint / workload yang diuji |  |
| Target p99 |  |
| Throughput target |  |
| Bottleneck utama dari JFR |  |
| Allocation hotspot |  |
| CPU hotspot |  |
| Thread wait paling dominan |  |
| DB pool wait? |  |
| GC pause p99 |  |
| Candidate fix |  |
| Guardrail yang harus tidak memburuk |  |
