## Response:
7/14/2026, 12:46:33 AM

# Seri 12/12 - Production JVM Playbook: Docker/Kubernetes Templates, JVM Flags Baseline, GC/JFR/NMT Profiles, Incident Checklist, Rollout, Rollback

## Introduction

Seri terakhir ini merangkum semua seri sebelumnya menjadi **playbook operasional production** untuk Java/Jersey/JAX-RS microservice di Docker/Kubernetes/on-prem/cloud.

Tujuannya sederhana:

```text id="vdgkyu"
Setiap service Java harus punya:
1. JVM runtime contract yang eksplisit
2. memory/GC sizing yang aman
3. observability default
4. diagnostic path saat incident
5. rollout/canary/rollback yang jelas
6. dokumentasi keputusan tuning
```

Untuk Java 17+ production, baseline yang sehat biasanya bukan banyak flag aneh, tetapi kombinasi: heap sizing yang sesuai container limit, GC log aktif, OOM dump path aman, JFR on-demand siap, NMT tersedia untuk investigasi native memory, dan dashboard yang menggabungkan JVM + container + aplikasi. Oracle mendokumentasikan JFR sebagai diagnostic/profiling tool yang terintegrasi ke JVM dan cocok untuk production dengan overhead rendah; Oracle juga menyediakan `jcmd` untuk diagnostic command seperti JFR, VM flags, heap, thread, dan native memory. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/troubleshoot/diagnostic-tools.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Diagram

```text id="6i1e0p"
Production JVM Playbook
│
├─ 1. Runtime Contract
│  ├─ Java version / vendor
│  ├─ artifact type
│  ├─ java command
│  ├─ JVM args
│  ├─ app args
│  ├─ system properties
│  ├─ env vars
│  └─ config precedence
│
├─ 2. Memory Contract
│  ├─ pod memory request / limit
│  ├─ heap max
│  ├─ native headroom
│  ├─ metaspace/code cache
│  ├─ thread stack
│  ├─ direct buffer
│  ├─ dump volume
│  └─ OOM behavior
│
├─ 3. GC Contract
│  ├─ default collector: G1
│  ├─ optional low-latency: ZGC
│  ├─ pause target
│  ├─ GC log baseline
│  ├─ GC evidence retention
│  └─ collector rollback
│
├─ 4. Observability Contract
│  ├─ JVM metrics
│  ├─ container metrics
│  ├─ application metrics
│  ├─ GC logs
│  ├─ JFR on demand
│  ├─ thread dump
│  ├─ heap dump
│  └─ NMT investigation mode
│
├─ 5. Diagnostic Contract
│  ├─ jcmd available?
│  ├─ JFR writable path?
│  ├─ heap dump writable path?
│  ├─ thread dump command
│  ├─ class histogram command
│  ├─ native memory command
│  └─ redaction/security policy
│
├─ 6. Deployment Contract
│  ├─ Docker ENTRYPOINT
│  ├─ JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS / JDK_JAVA_OPTIONS
│  ├─ Kubernetes env
│  ├─ ConfigMap / Secret
│  ├─ resources request/limit
│  ├─ probes
│  ├─ volume mounts
│  └─ Helm values
│
├─ 7. Rollout Contract
│  ├─ baseline metrics
│  ├─ canary
│  ├─ compare p95/p99
│  ├─ compare CPU/RSS/GC
│  ├─ monitor OOM/restart
│  ├─ rollback switch
│  └─ decision log
│
└─ 8. Incident Contract
   ├─ classify symptom
   ├─ capture evidence
   ├─ protect sensitive artifacts
   ├─ form hypothesis
   ├─ mitigate
   ├─ verify
   └─ RCA / follow-up
```

---

## Core Principle

```text id="jxcb7j"
A JVM production setup is not complete until it is observable, diagnosable, and reversible.
```

Artinya:

```text id="1hppe0"
Flag bagus tetapi tidak bisa diverifikasi    → belum cukup.
Heap besar tetapi pod OOMKilled              → salah budget.
GC low-latency tetapi throughput jatuh       → trade-off gagal.
JFR bisa jalan tetapi file tidak bisa diambil → diagnostic path gagal.
Heap dump aktif tetapi path tidak writable   → forensic gagal.
Canary tanpa rollback                        → rollout risk.
```

---

## Tabular Decomposition Map

| # | Area | Production skill | Artefact / command | Risiko jika tidak ada |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Runtime identity** | Bisa membuktikan Java version, JVM vendor, flags, command line aktual. | `java --version`, `jcmd <pid> VM.version`, `VM.flags`, `VM.command_line` | Debug berdasarkan asumsi, bukan runtime aktual. |
| 2 | **JVM arg baseline** | Memiliki template JVM args standar per service type. | `JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS`, Helm values | Config drift antar environment. |
| 3 | **Heap sizing** | Menyelaraskan heap dengan container memory limit dan native headroom. | `-Xmx`, `-XX:MaxRAMPercentage` | Pod `OOMKilled` walau heap “aman”. |
| 4 | **GC collector policy** | Menentukan kapan pakai G1, kapan evaluasi ZGC. | `-XX:+UseG1GC`, `-XX:+UseZGC` | Collector diganti tanpa evidence. |
| 5 | **GC logging** | Selalu punya GC event timeline. | `-Xlog:gc*` | Tidak bisa membuktikan GC impact saat incident. |
| 6 | **JFR readiness** | Bisa mengambil runtime profile tanpa restart. | `jcmd JFR.start`, `-XX:StartFlightRecording` | CPU/allocation/lock/GC root cause tidak terlihat. |
| 7 | **NMT readiness** | Bisa investigasi RSS tinggi dengan heap normal. | `-XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary`, `jcmd VM.native_memory` | Native/direct/thread/metaspace issue sulit dibuktikan. |
| 8 | **OOM forensic** | Heap dump/error file otomatis dan path writable. | `HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError`, `HeapDumpPath`, `ErrorFile` | OOM terjadi tanpa artefak. |
| 9 | **Thread diagnostic** | Bisa ambil thread dump series. | `jcmd Thread.print` | Stuck/deadlock/pool exhaustion tidak jelas. |
| 10 | **Heap diagnostic** | Bisa ambil class histogram dan heap dump. | `jcmd GC.class_histogram`, `GC.heap_dump` | Leak/retention tidak bisa dianalisis. |
| 11 | **Container evidence** | Bisa membedakan Java OOM vs kernel OOMKill. | `kubectl describe pod`, `kubectl top pod` | Salah mitigasi: menaikkan heap saat RSS yang bermasalah. |
| 12 | **Dashboard** | JVM + container + aplikasi terlihat dalam satu view. | Prometheus/APM/Grafana | Root cause salah karena hanya lihat heap. |
| 13 | **Alerting** | Alert berbasis symptom dan saturation. | p99, GC pause, RSS, OOMKilled, DB pool wait | Alert noisy atau terlambat. |
| 14 | **Rollout/canary** | Perubahan JVM diuji bertahap. | canary deployment, Helm override | Regressi langsung kena semua traffic. |
| 15 | **Rollback** | Bisa kembali ke JVM config lama cepat. | previous Helm values / image / env | Tuning gagal sulit dipulihkan. |
| 16 | **Security policy** | Dump/JFR/log diperlakukan sebagai artefak sensitif. | redaction, access control, retention | PII/secret bocor. |

---

## Production Baseline Profiles

## Profile A - Default Java/Jersey Microservice, G1, Balanced

Cocok untuk:

```text id="eojcwh"
REST API umum
Jersey/JAX-RS
MyBatis
PostgreSQL
moderate traffic
tidak banyak file/native processing
latency penting tetapi bukan sub-ms GC requirement
```

```bash id="spnz6u"
JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS="
  -XX:+UseG1GC
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0
  -XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=200
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
  -XX:ErrorFile=/dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log
"
```

Kenapa ini baseline bagus:

| Flag | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| `UseG1GC` | Collector umum untuk server workload |
| `InitialRAMPercentage` | Heap awal tidak terlalu kecil |
| `MaxRAMPercentage` | Heap mengikuti container limit |
| `MaxGCPauseMillis=200` | Pause goal awal untuk G1 |
| `user.timezone` | Timestamp/scheduler/audit konsisten |
| `file.encoding` | Text/file/log encoding konsisten |
| `-Xlog:gc*` | GC evidence selalu ada |
| `HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError` | Forensic saat heap OOM |
| `ErrorFile` | Fatal JVM crash evidence |

Oracle `java` command mendokumentasikan opsi seperti `-Xlog`, `-XX:NativeMemoryTracking`, heap-related options, dan startup diagnostics sebagai bagian dari command-line JVM options; untuk flags advanced, tetap verifikasi di JDK target karena detail dapat berbeda antar versi. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/22/docs/specs/man/java.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Profile B - Conservative Memory / Banyak Thread / Banyak Worker

Cocok untuk:

```text id="fza7w6"
Camunda job executor aktif
scheduler banyak
request worker cukup banyak
APM/security agent aktif
pod memory terbatas
```

```bash id="kht6sg"
JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS="
  -XX:+UseG1GC
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=20.0
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=55.0
  -XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=200
  -Xss768k
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
  -XX:ErrorFile=/dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log
"
```

Catatan:

```text id="yfnkbj"
-Xss768k harus diuji.
Jangan turunkan stack size jika call stack dalam, recursion, parser, framework stack berat, atau ada StackOverflow risk.
```

---

## Profile C - File / Document / Media Processing

Cocok untuk:

```text id="o7h5ve"
CSV/Excel/PDF/Word generation
large upload/download
OCR/media/native library
large byte[]
direct buffer / mmap risk
```

```bash id="vah70f"
JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS="
  -XX:+UseG1GC
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=20.0
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=50.0
  -XX:NativeMemoryTracking=summary
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
  -Xlog:gc*,safepoint:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
  -XX:ErrorFile=/dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log
"
```

Kenapa heap ratio lebih rendah:

```text id="d06qo3"
Service file/media sering butuh native/direct/temp memory lebih besar.
Heap terlalu besar justru mengurangi headroom untuk RSS.
```

NMT berguna untuk melihat kategori native memory HotSpot, tetapi NMT tidak melacak semua alokasi native pihak ketiga, jadi tetap korelasikan dengan RSS/container metrics. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/22/docs/specs/man/java.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Profile D - Low-Latency Candidate, ZGC

Cocok jika:

```text id="zmmt63"
G1 pause terbukti memengaruhi p99/p999
CPU headroom cukup
throughput trade-off diterima
heap headroom cukup
workload strict latency
```

### Java 17

```bash id="m6rakc"
JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS="
  -XX:+UseZGC
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
  -XX:ErrorFile=/dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log
"
```

### Java 21 Generational ZGC

```bash id="vcjsdb"
JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS="
  -XX:+UseZGC
  -XX:+ZGenerational
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
"
```

### Java 24+

```bash id="4imjm2"
JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS="
  -XX:+UseZGC
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=60.0
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
"
```

JEP 490 menghapus non-generational ZGC mode; setelah perubahan itu, `-XX:+UseZGC` memakai Generational ZGC, sedangkan `ZGenerational` menjadi obsolete. Ini penting untuk template Java 21 vs Java 24+ agar flag tidak salah antar versi. ([openjdk.org](https://openjdk.org/jeps/490?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Docker Template

### Recommended Dockerfile pattern

```dockerfile id="lg3w70"
FROM eclipse-temurin:21-jdk-jammy

WORKDIR /app

COPY target/app.jar /app/app.jar

RUN mkdir -p /dumps /recordings \
    && chown -R 10001:10001 /app /dumps /recordings

USER 10001

ENV JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS="\
  -XX:+UseG1GC \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0 \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0 \
  -XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=200 \
  -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta \
  -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8 \
  -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError \
  -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps \
  -XX:ErrorFile=/dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log \
"

ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "/app/app.jar"]
```

### Kenapa pakai exec-form `ENTRYPOINT`

```text id="1jpw9e"
ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "/app/app.jar"]
```

lebih baik daripada:

```dockerfile id="it5qf7"
ENTRYPOINT java -jar /app/app.jar
```

Karena exec-form membuat Java process menerima signal container dengan lebih jelas. Jika memakai shell-form, signal handling bisa tertahan shell wrapper kecuali ditangani dengan `exec`.

---

## Kubernetes Deployment Template

```yaml id="j1amgc"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-service
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: order-service
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: order-service
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-service
          image: company/order-service:1.0.0
          imagePullPolicy: IfNotPresent

          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 8080

          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "1000m"
              memory: "1Gi"
            limits:
              cpu: "2000m"
              memory: "2Gi"

          env:
            - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
              value: >-
                -XX:+UseG1GC
                -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=25.0
                -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=65.0
                -XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=200
                -Duser.timezone=Asia/Jakarta
                -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
                -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags
                -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError
                -XX:HeapDumpPath=/dumps
                -XX:ErrorFile=/dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log

            - name: APP_ENV
              value: "prod"

          volumeMounts:
            - name: dumps
              mountPath: /dumps
            - name: recordings
              mountPath: /recordings

          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/ready
              port: http
            initialDelaySeconds: 20
            periodSeconds: 10
            timeoutSeconds: 3
            failureThreshold: 6

          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: http
            initialDelaySeconds: 60
            periodSeconds: 20
            timeoutSeconds: 3
            failureThreshold: 3

      volumes:
        - name: dumps
          emptyDir: {}
        - name: recordings
          emptyDir: {}
```

Kubernetes memory limit berlaku untuk total memory container, bukan hanya heap JVM. Jika process melebihi limit, container dapat dihentikan oleh kernel/cgroup dan terlihat sebagai `OOMKilled`/exit code 137 di pod status. ([Komodor](https://komodor.com/learn/how-to-fix-oomkilled-exit-code-137/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Helm Values Pattern

```yaml id="wtkc6m"
java:
  gc: g1
  initialRamPercentage: 25.0
  maxRamPercentage: 65.0
  maxGcPauseMillis: 200
  timezone: Asia/Jakarta
  fileEncoding: UTF-8
  gcLog: true
  heapDumpOnOom: true
  heapDumpPath: /dumps
  errorFilePath: /dumps/hs_err_pid%p.log
  nativeMemoryTracking: false
  startFlightRecording: false

resources:
  requests:
    cpu: "1000m"
    memory: "1Gi"
  limits:
    cpu: "2000m"
    memory: "2Gi"
```

Template render:

```yaml id="nfn2ka"
env:
  - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
    value: >-
      {{- if eq .Values.java.gc "g1" }} -XX:+UseG1GC {{- end }}
      {{- if eq .Values.java.gc "zgc" }} -XX:+UseZGC {{- end }}
      -XX:InitialRAMPercentage={{ .Values.java.initialRamPercentage }}
      -XX:MaxRAMPercentage={{ .Values.java.maxRamPercentage }}
      -Duser.timezone={{ .Values.java.timezone }}
      -Dfile.encoding={{ .Values.java.fileEncoding }}
      {{- if .Values.java.gcLog }} -Xlog:gc*:stdout:time,uptime,level,tags {{- end }}
      {{- if .Values.java.heapDumpOnOom }} -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError -XX:HeapDumpPath={{ .Values.java.heapDumpPath }} {{- end }}
      -XX:ErrorFile={{ .Values.java.errorFilePath }}
```

Rule:

```text id="zqqmfy"
Jangan biarkan setiap service membuat JVM flags sendiri dari nol.
Pakai template standar, lalu override dengan alasan yang terdokumentasi.
```

---

## Runtime Verification Checklist

Setelah deploy, verifikasi runtime aktual.

```bash id="q673bo"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.version
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.command_line
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.flags
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.system_properties
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 GC.heap_info
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- cat /proc/1/status | egrep 'VmRSS|VmSize|Threads'
```

Jika NMT aktif:

```bash id="wk7hgd"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.native_memory summary scale=MB
```

Checklist:

| Check | Expected |
|---|---|
| Java version | Sesuai target service |
| Collector | G1/ZGC sesuai template |
| Max heap | Sesuai memory budget |
| Timezone | `Asia/Jakarta` atau standar organisasi |
| Encoding | `UTF-8` |
| GC log | Muncul di stdout/log backend |
| Dump path | `/dumps` writable |
| RSS | Stabil dan punya headroom |
| Thread count | Sesuai baseline |
| Pod restart | Tidak ada unexpected restart |

---

## Incident Playbook

## 1. Classify symptom

| Symptom | Kemungkinan awal |
|---|---|
| p99 latency naik | GC, DB, downstream, lock, CPU throttling |
| CPU tinggi | hot method, GC, busy loop, serialization, agent |
| Memory naik | heap leak, native memory, direct buffer, thread |
| Pod restart | OOMKilled, crash, liveness failure |
| Java OOM | heap/metaspace/direct/native thread OOM |
| Request stuck | DB pool, lock, downstream socket, deadlock |
| Startup lambat | class loading, framework init, JIT, DB init |
| Camunda job delay | job executor, DB lock, retries, backlog |

---

## 2. Capture evidence package

```bash id="bp8m28"
mkdir -p evidence/thread evidence/memory evidence/jfr evidence/k8s

kubectl exec <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.version > evidence/vm-version.txt
kubectl exec <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.command_line > evidence/vm-command-line.txt
kubectl exec <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.flags > evidence/vm-flags.txt
kubectl exec <pod> -- jcmd 1 GC.heap_info > evidence/memory/heap-info.txt
kubectl exec <pod> -- jcmd 1 GC.class_histogram > evidence/memory/class-histogram.txt

for i in 1 2 3; do
  kubectl exec <pod> -- jcmd 1 Thread.print > evidence/thread/thread-$i.txt
  sleep 10
done

kubectl exec <pod> -- jcmd 1 JFR.start name=incident settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/incident.jfr

kubectl describe pod <pod> > evidence/k8s/describe-pod.txt
kubectl top pod <pod> > evidence/k8s/top-pod.txt
kubectl get events --sort-by=.lastTimestamp > evidence/k8s/events.txt
```

Jika NMT aktif:

```bash id="h38ttr"
kubectl exec <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.native_memory summary scale=MB > evidence/memory/nmt-summary.txt
```

JFR dan heap dump harus diperlakukan sebagai artefak sensitif karena dapat mengandung data runtime, payload, path, bahkan PII/secret. JFR sendiri adalah event/profiling data JVM, dan Oracle mendokumentasikannya sebagai API/module untuk membuat dan mengonsumsi Flight Recorder data. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/api/jdk.jfr/module-summary.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3. Decision matrix saat incident

| Evidence | Diagnosis lebih kuat | Mitigasi awal |
|---|---|---|
| `OOMKilled`, no Java OOM | RSS melewati container limit | Turunkan heap ratio / naikkan limit / kurangi native/thread |
| `Java heap space` + heap dump | Heap leak/retention/large object | Fix retention; temporary heap increase |
| Heap normal, RSS naik | Native/direct/thread/metaspace | NMT/BufferPool/thread analysis |
| GC pause correlate p99 | GC root cause mungkin | Reduce allocation / tune heap/GC |
| p99 naik tanpa GC pause | DB/downstream/lock/thread | Trace + thread dump + pool metrics |
| CPU high + JFR hot method | Code hotspot | Optimize algorithm/serialization |
| Thread dump DB pool wait | DB pool/query bottleneck | Fix query/leak/transaction/pool |
| Thread dump BLOCKED | Lock contention | Reduce lock scope |
| Camunda backlog naik | Worker/DB/job executor issue | Tune job executor/query/retry/backpressure |

---

## Rollout / Canary Playbook untuk JVM Change

JVM changes yang perlu canary:

```text id="2npp6t"
GC collector change
heap percentage change besar
Xss change
JFR always-on config change
NMT detail enablement
APM/security agent change
thread pool / DB pool change
CPU/memory request-limit change
```

### Canary steps

```text id="ks1gq1"
1. Baseline current production
2. Deploy 1 pod / small traffic slice
3. Compare against control group
4. Watch 30-60 minutes minimum under meaningful traffic
5. Check p95/p99, throughput, CPU, RSS, GC, restarts
6. Expand gradually
7. Keep rollback ready
```

### Compare table

| Metric | Control | Canary | Decision |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| HTTP p95 |  |  |  |
| HTTP p99 |  |  |  |
| Error rate |  |  |  |
| Throughput |  |  |  |
| CPU avg/p95 |  |  |  |
| CPU throttling |  |  |  |
| RSS |  |  |  |
| Heap after-GC |  |  |  |
| GC pause p99 |  |  |  |
| Full GC count |  |  |  |
| Thread count |  |  |  |
| DB pool wait |  |  |  |
| Pod restarts |  |  |  |
| OOMKilled |  |  |  |

### Rollback rule

```text id="jlbevi"
Rollback if:
1. p99 worsens materially
2. error rate rises
3. RSS approaches memory limit
4. CPU throttling increases significantly
5. Full GC / allocation stalls appear
6. pod restarts / OOMKilled appear
7. throughput drops beyond accepted threshold
```

---

## Production Runbook: Common Scenarios

## Scenario A - Pod OOMKilled

```text id="c6i9kh"
Symptoms:
- pod restart
- no Java OOM in logs
- exit code 137 / OOMKilled
```

Commands:

```bash id="z5cxy9"
kubectl describe pod <pod>
kubectl top pod <pod>
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 VM.flags
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- cat /proc/1/status | egrep 'VmRSS|VmSize|Threads'
```

Likely actions:

```text id="bhf7ga"
1. Check if MaxRAMPercentage too high.
2. Lower heap ratio.
3. Increase pod memory limit if justified.
4. Check thread count.
5. Check direct/native/metaspace.
6. Enable NMT in next rollout.
```

---

## Scenario B - Java Heap OOM

```text id="2cmdhi"
Symptoms:
- java.lang.OutOfMemoryError: Java heap space
- heap dump may exist
- GC log shows heap not reclaiming enough
```

Commands:

```bash id="hka0gz"
jcmd 1 GC.heap_info
jcmd 1 GC.class_histogram
jcmd 1 GC.heap_dump /dumps/heap-$(date +%s).hprof
```

Likely actions:

```text id="d1h5ad"
1. Analyze heap dump dominator.
2. Identify retained object root.
3. Check cache/queue/ThreadLocal/static map.
4. Check large query/result/payload.
5. Increase heap only as mitigation, not final fix.
```

---

## Scenario C - p99 Latency Spike

Commands:

```bash id="b9n0ps"
jcmd 1 JFR.start name=p99 settings=profile duration=120s filename=/tmp/p99.jfr

for i in 1 2 3; do
  jcmd 1 Thread.print > /tmp/thread-$i.txt
  sleep 10
done
```

Correlate:

```text id="9nrxqf"
GC pause
CPU throttling
DB pool waiting
DB query p99
downstream HTTP p99
lock contention
Camunda job backlog
```

Likely actions:

| Root cause | Action |
|---|---|
| GC pause | reduce allocation / tune heap / G1 pause / evaluate ZGC |
| DB pool wait | query/transaction/pool/backpressure |
| Downstream slow | timeout/retry/circuit breaker |
| Lock contention | reduce lock scope |
| CPU throttling | adjust CPU request/limit |
| Allocation spike | payload/query/mapping optimization |

---

## Scenario D - Heap Normal, RSS High

Commands:

```bash id="te76oc"
jcmd 1 GC.heap_info
jcmd 1 VM.native_memory summary scale=MB
jcmd 1 Thread.print
cat /proc/1/status | egrep 'VmRSS|VmSize|Threads'
```

Check:

```text id="wcpr1w"
Thread count
direct buffer
mapped buffer
metaspace
code cache
native library
APM/security agent
tmpfs / memory-backed volume
```

Likely actions:

```text id="kqzb5d"
1. Lower heap percentage to restore native headroom.
2. Reduce thread pools.
3. Investigate direct buffer/file/native library usage.
4. Compare with/without agent.
5. Avoid heap-only fix.
```

---

## Scenario E - Startup Slow / First Request Slow

Startup flags:

```bash id="v1v3z0"
-XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=/recordings/startup.jfr,duration=180s,settings=profile
-Xlog:class+load=info
```

Check:

```text id="weyrn6"
Jersey auto-discovery / package scanning
MyBatis mapper loading
Camunda bootstrap
DB pool initialization
JSON provider initialization
JIT warmup
lazy first-request path
```

Likely actions:

```text id="hlv154"
explicit Jersey registration
reduce classpath scanning
readiness gate after warmup
preload critical mapper/provider
warmup endpoint
consider CDS/AppCDS later
```

---

## JVM Config Decision Records

Setiap perubahan JVM production harus punya record singkat:

```markdown id="ue9j65"
# JVM Runtime Decision Record

Service: order-service
Date: 2026-07-14
Owner: platform / service team

## Change
Set MaxRAMPercentage from 65 to 55.

## Reason
Pod RSS reached 88% of 2Gi limit during peak.
Heap after-GC was stable around 900Mi.
NMT showed Thread + Direct + Class memory pressure.

## Evidence
- GC log window: 2026-07-14 10:00-10:30
- p99 latency unchanged
- RSS before: 1.8Gi
- RSS after: 1.55Gi
- GC pause p99 before: 120ms
- GC pause p99 after: 135ms

## Risk
Slightly more frequent young GC.

## Rollback
Set MaxRAMPercentage back to 65.

## Review Date
2026-07-28
```

---

## Final Operational Cheat Sheet

### Always-on baseline

```text id="ba7yrw"
- explicit Java version
- explicit GC policy
- container-aware heap sizing
- GC log enabled
- OOM dump path configured
- JVM error file configured
- timezone/encoding explicit
- dashboard JVM + container + app
```

### Investigation-on-demand

```text id="dtz1d5"
JFR 120s
thread dump series
class histogram
NMT summary
pod describe/events
dashboard snapshot
```

### Avoid by default

```text id="tt1cpp"
random -XX tuning
Xmx equal to pod limit
GC collector change without evidence
heap dump without security handling
DB pool increase without DB evidence
thread pool increase without queue/backpressure model
```

### Verify after every deploy

```bash id="w47l8m"
jcmd 1 VM.version
jcmd 1 VM.command_line
jcmd 1 VM.flags
jcmd 1 GC.heap_info
cat /proc/1/status | egrep 'VmRSS|Threads'
```

---

## Production Readiness Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Java runtime | Version/vendor documented |
| JVM args | Standard template used |
| Heap | Container-aware sizing |
| Native headroom | Budget documented |
| GC | Collector and pause goal known |
| GC log | Enabled |
| OOM dump | Enabled and writable |
| JFR | On-demand command tested |
| `jcmd` | Available or debug path exists |
| NMT | Available in investigation profile |
| Metrics | JVM + container + app |
| Alerts | p99, GC, RSS, OOMKilled, DB pool |
| Security | Dump/JFR/log access controlled |
| Canary | JVM changes rolled out gradually |
| Rollback | Previous config available |
| Docs | Runtime decision record exists |

---

## Anti-Pattern Summary

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa salah |
|---|---|
| `-Xmx` sama dengan memory limit | Native/non-heap tidak punya ruang |
| Mengandalkan default tanpa verifikasi | Runtime bisa berbeda dari asumsi |
| Tidak aktifkan GC log | Hilang timeline penting |
| Tidak punya JFR path | Root cause CPU/allocation/lock sulit |
| Tidak punya dump volume | OOM forensic gagal |
| Menambah heap saat pod OOMKilled | Bisa memperparah RSS |
| Ganti ke ZGC tanpa p99-GC evidence | Bisa menurunkan throughput tanpa benefit |
| Menaikkan DB/thread pool tanpa bottleneck analysis | Bisa memindahkan bottleneck |
| Banyak JVM changes sekaligus | Tidak bisa atribusi dampak |
| Dump/JFR dishare bebas | Risiko PII/secret/compliance |

---

## Roadmap Belajar Singkat Setelah Seri Ini

| Step | Fokus | Output |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Ambil satu service nyata | Buat runtime inventory JVM |
| 2 | Terapkan baseline GC log + dump path | Service punya forensic readiness |
| 3 | Buat dashboard JVM/container/app | Heap, RSS, GC, p99, DB pool terlihat |
| 4 | Jalankan load test | Baseline p95/p99/CPU/RSS/GC |
| 5 | Ambil JFR 120 detik | Temukan CPU/allocation/lock/I/O hotspot |
| 6 | Buat memory budget | Heap + native headroom jelas |
| 7 | Simulasikan incident | Thread dump, JFR, histogram, pod events |
| 8 | Dokumentasikan runbook | Tim bisa ulangi tanpa tribal knowledge |

---

## Seri 12/12 selesai

Seluruh rangkaian **JVM, JVM Arguments, JVM Configuration, JVM Tuning & Optimization, dan GC Tuning & Optimization** selesai.

Rekomendasi materi selanjutnya yang paling nyambung: **Java Production Observability & Performance Engineering untuk Jersey/MyBatis/Camunda Microservice** - fokus lebih dalam ke Micrometer/Prometheus, OpenTelemetry tracing, DB pool metrics, Camunda metrics, structured logging, dashboard design, SLO, alerting, dan RCA workflow end-to-end.
